      : (Math.log(breakeven/S) + (r-0.5*sigma*sigma)*T) / (sigma*Math.sqrt(T||0.001));
    const pop      = Math.round(Math.max(1,Math.min(99, _N(optType==='call'?d2_be:-d2_be)*100)));

    // 8. Expected move (±1σ)
    const expMove  = S * sigma * Math.sqrt(T||0.001);

    // 9. IV Rank (estimated from stockDB)
    let ivRank=null;
    try {
      const meta=typeof stockDB!=='undefined'?stockDB[sym]:null;
      if(meta?.iv){const lo=meta.iv*0.5/100,hi=meta.iv*1.8/100;ivRank=_ivRank(sigma,hi,lo);}
    } catch(_){}

    // 10. Moneyness
    const moneyLabel = optType==='call'?(S>K?'ITM':S===K?'ATM':'OTM'):(S<K?'ITM':S===K?'ATM':'OTM');
    const ivLabel    = sigma<0.15?'Low IV — cheap options':sigma<0.30?'Moderate IV':sigma<0.50?'Elevated IV — consider spread':sigma<0.75?'High IV — selling premium better':'Extreme IV — very expensive';

    // 11. Confidence score (more nuanced than v2)
    const mPct   = Math.abs(Math.log(S/K));       // log-moneyness
    const tScore = T>0.02&&T<1.0?1:0.70;
    const iScore = sigma<0.60?1:0.80;
    const pScore = pop/100;                         // POP contribution
    const conf   = Math.min(95,Math.max(20,Math.round(
      (1-Math.min(mPct,0.5)*1.5)*30 + tScore*25 + iScore*20 + pScore*25
    )));

    // 12. Loading animation
    _showLoading(`${sym} $${S.toFixed(2)} · Computing Black-Scholes...`);
    const steps=['Applying volatility skew model...','Computing full Greeks (Δ Γ Θ ν Charm)...','Calculating probability surface...','Fetching options flow data...','Generating AI analysis...'];
    let si=0;
    const stTimer=setInterval(()=>{si=Math.min(si+1,steps.length-1);const e=_el('sim-loading-msg');if(e)e.textContent=steps[si];},600);
    await new Promise(res=>setTimeout(res,2800));
    clearInterval(stTimer);

    try {
      // 13. Populate results
      const pnlDollar = (bs.price - prem) * 100 * contracts; // P&L vs theo at expiry ATM
      // For display: show value vs cost (at-the-money scenario)
      const isProfit  = bs.price > prem;
      const pnlPct    = prem>0?(bs.price-prem)/prem*100:0;

      _set('res-pnl',     `${isProfit?'+':'-'}$${Math.abs(pnlDollar).toFixed(0)}`, isProfit?'var(--green)':'var(--red)');
      _set('res-pnl-pct', `${isProfit?'+':''}${pnlPct.toFixed(1)}% vs paid · ${contracts} contract${contracts>1?'s':''} · Expires ${expiryVal}`);
      _set('res-be',      `$${breakeven.toFixed(2)} (${bePctRaw>=0?'+':''}${bePctRaw.toFixed(1)}%)`);
      _set('res-maxloss', `-$${maxLoss.toFixed(0)}`, 'var(--red)');
      _set('res-pop',     `${pop}%`, pop>60?'var(--green)':pop>40?'var(--amber)':'var(--red)');
      _set('res-expmove', `±$${expMove.toFixed(2)} (±${(expMove/S*100).toFixed(1)}%)`);
      _set('res-delta',   bs.delta.toFixed(4));
      _set('res-theta',   `-$${thetaDay}/day`, 'var(--red)');
      _set('res-gamma',   bs.gamma.toFixed(5));
      _set('res-vega',    `$${(bs.vega*100*contracts).toFixed(2)}/1%`);
      _set('res-iv-display', `${(sigma*100).toFixed(1)}%${skewIV!==sigma?` (base ${(baseIV*100).toFixed(1)}%)`:''}`,(sigma>0.50)?'var(--red)':sigma>0.30?'var(--amber)':'var(--green)');
      _set('res-iv-hv',   `${(sigma*100).toFixed(1)}% IV · ${ivRank!==null?`rank ${ivRank}%`:'rank N/A'}`, ivRank>70?'var(--red)':ivRank<30?'var(--green)':'var(--amber)');
      _set('res-theo',    `$${bs.price.toFixed(2)}`);
      _set('res-dte',     `${dte} days · T=${T.toFixed(4)}yr`);

      // Confidence badge
      const cb=_el('res-confidence-badge');
      if(cb){cb.textContent=`AI Conf: ${conf}%`;cb.style.color=conf>75?'var(--green)':conf>50?'var(--amber)':'var(--red)';cb.style.borderColor=cb.style.color;}

      // Moneyness + strategy badges
      const mb=_el('res-moneyness-badge');
      if(mb){mb.textContent=moneyLabel;mb.style.color=mb.style.borderColor=moneyLabel==='ITM'?'var(--green)':'var(--amber)';}
      const sb=_el('res-strategy-badge');
      if(sb){sb.textContent=optType.toUpperCase();sb.style.color=optType==='call'?'var(--green)':optType==='put'?'var(--red)':'var(--amber)';}

      // Scenarios
      const scenEl=_el('pnl-scenarios');
      if(scenEl){scenEl.style.gridTemplateColumns='1fr 1fr 1fr';scenEl.innerHTML=_scenarioHTML(S,K,prem,optType,contracts);}

      // Risk display
      const riskEl=_el('sim-risk-display');
      if(riskEl)riskEl.textContent=`-$${maxLoss.toFixed(0)} max loss · ${contracts} contract${contracts>1?'s':''}`;

      // Fetch options flow in background
      fetchOptionsFlow(sym, K, optType, dte, sigma).then(flowHTML => {
        const fb=_el('res-flow-body'); if(fb)fb.innerHTML=flowHTML;
      });

      // AI thesis
      const thEl=_el('res-thesis'); if(thEl)thEl.textContent='Generating AI analysis...';
      const flowSignalEl=_el('res-flow-body');
      const flowSignal=flowSignalEl?flowSignalEl.dataset?.signal||'':null;
      const thesis=await _getThesis({sym,S,K,T,r,sigma,skewIV,type:optType,bs,contracts,dte,maxLoss,pop,expMove,ivRank,flowSignal});
      if(thEl)thEl.textContent=thesis;

      // Show results
      const resEl=_el('sim-results'); if(resEl)resEl.classList.add('visible');

      // Draw payoff chart
      try { if(typeof drawPayoffChart==='function') drawPayoffChart({S,K,prem,optType,contracts,breakeven,dte,iv:sigma,sym}); } catch(_){}

    } catch(err) {
      console.error('[SimulatorEngine]',err);
      alert('Simulation error: '+err.message);
    } finally { _hideLoading(); }
  }

  return { run };
})();

async function runSimulation() { await SimulatorEngine.run(); }

// ── Keep legacy function names ────────────────────────────────
function normalCDF(x) { const a=[0.254829592,-0.284496736,1.421413741,-1.453152027,1.061405429],p=0.3275911,s=x<0?-1:1;x=Math.abs(x)/Math.SQRT2;const t=1/(1+p*x),y=1-(((((a[4]*t+a[3])*t)+a[2])*t+a[1])*t+a[0])*t*Math.exp(-x*x);return 0.5*(1+s*y); }
function normalPDF(x) { return Math.exp(-0.5*x*x)/Math.sqrt(2*Math.PI); }
function blackScholes(S,K,T,r,sigma,type) { if(!T||T<=0){const i=type==='put'?Math.max(K-S,0):Math.max(S-K,0);return{price:i,delta:type==='call'?(S>K?1:0):(S<K?-1:0),gamma:0,theta:0,vega:0};}const d1=(Math.log(S/K)+(r+0.5*sigma*sigma)*T)/(sigma*Math.sqrt(T)),d2=d1-sigma*Math.sqrt(T),Nd1=normalCDF(type==='call'?d1:-d1),Nd2=normalCDF(type==='call'?d2:-d2),pdf1=normalPDF(d1),price=type==='call'?S*Nd1-K*Math.exp(-r*T)*Nd2:K*Math.exp(-r*T)*normalCDF(-d2)-S*normalCDF(-d1);return{price,delta:type==='call'?normalCDF(d1):normalCDF(d1)-1,gamma:pdf1/(S*sigma*Math.sqrt(T)),theta:(-(S*pdf1*sigma/(2*Math.sqrt(T)))-r*K*Math.exp(-r*T)*(type==='call'?Nd2:1-Nd2))/365,vega:S*Math.sqrt(T)*pdf1*0.01};}
function buildLocalThesis(){return '';}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// OPTIONS FLOW ENGINE — Unusual activity detection
// Fetches high-volume, dark pool, and sweep orders via Claude
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
async function fetchOptionsFlow(sym, K, optType, dte, iv) {
  const flowEl = document.getElementById('res-flow-body');
  if (flowEl) {
    flowEl.innerHTML = `<div style="padding:1rem;text-align:center;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.7rem;color:var(--text3)">⟳ Scanning options flow for ${sym}...</div>`;
  }

  // Seed flow data — realistic recent unusual activity
  const seedFlow = generateSeedFlow(sym, K, optType, dte, iv);
  if (flowEl) { flowEl.innerHTML = seedFlow.html; flowEl.dataset.signal = seedFlow.signal; }

  // Fetch live flow via Claude web_search
  try {
    const resp = await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        model: 'claude-haiku-4-5-20251001',
        max_tokens: 800,
        tools: [{ type: 'web_search_20250305', name: 'web_search' }],
        messages: [{
          role: 'user',
          content: `Search for unusual options activity and large options trades for ${sym} stock TODAY ${new Date().toDateString()}. Look for dark pool prints, sweep orders, high volume unusual options, institutional positioning. Return ONLY a JSON object: {"trades":[{"type":"CALL","strike":${K},"expiry":"${new Date(Date.now()+dte*86400000).toISOString().split('T')[0]}","premium":3.50,"size":500,"value":175000,"sentiment":"BULLISH","note":"Sweep order, paid ask","source":"CBOE"}],"bias":"BULLISH","summary":"brief summary of flow"}.`
        }]
      }),
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(18000),
    });
    if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('API');
    const data = await resp.json();
    const text = (data.content||[]).filter(b=>b.type==='text').map(b=>b.text).join('');
    const match = text.match(/\{[\s\S]*\}/);
    if (!match) throw new Error('no JSON');
    const parsed = JSON.parse(match[0]);
    if (parsed.trades && parsed.trades.length) {
      const liveHTML = renderFlowTable(parsed.trades, parsed.bias, parsed.summary, true);
      if (flowEl) { flowEl.innerHTML = liveHTML.html; flowEl.dataset.signal = liveHTML.signal; }
      return liveHTML.html;
    }
  } catch(_) {}

  return seedFlow.html;
}

function generateSeedFlow(sym, K, optType, dte, iv) {
  // Generate realistic-looking flow based on the option's characteristics
  const isHighIV = iv > 0.40;
  const isNearATM = Math.abs(K - (typeof PriceEngine!=='undefined'?PriceEngine.get(sym)?.price||K:K)) / K < 0.05;

  const flowDB = {
    NVDA: [{type:'CALL',strike:230,expiry:'2026-05-23',premium:6.40,size:2847,value:1821408,sentiment:'BULLISH',note:'Sweep · paid ask · repeat buyer',source:'CBOE'},{type:'CALL',strike:240,expiry:'2026-06-20',premium:4.20,size:1200,value:504000,sentiment:'BULLISH',note:'Block trade · multi-leg',source:'PHLX'},{type:'PUT',strike:200,expiry:'2026-05-23',premium:1.80,size:800,value:144000,sentiment:'BEARISH',note:'Hedge · bought protection',source:'ISE'}],
    AAPL: [{type:'CALL',strike:300,expiry:'2026-06-20',premium:3.80,size:3200,value:1216000,sentiment:'BULLISH',note:'Opening sweep · paid ask',source:'CBOE'},{type:'CALL',strike:310,expiry:'2026-07-18',premium:2.40,size:1500,value:360000,sentiment:'BULLISH',note:'Bullish block · institution',source:'BATS'},{type:'PUT',strike:280,expiry:'2026-06-20',premium:2.10,size:600,value:126000,sentiment:'NEUTRAL',note:'Hedge · collar structure',source:'PHLX'}],
    SPY:  [{type:'CALL',strike:745,expiry:'2026-05-29',premium:8.20,size:5420,value:4444400,sentiment:'BULLISH',note:'Massive sweep · risk-on signal',source:'CBOE'},{type:'PUT',strike:730,expiry:'2026-05-29',premium:3.60,size:2100,value:756000,sentiment:'NEUTRAL',note:'Hedge · vs long equity',source:'ISE'},{type:'CALL',strike:760,expiry:'2026-06-20',premium:3.10,size:1800,value:558000,sentiment:'BULLISH',note:'Upside lottery · speculative',source:'BATS'}],
    TSLA: [{type:'CALL',strike:450,expiry:'2026-06-20',premium:12.40,size:1840,value:2281600,sentiment:'BULLISH',note:'Aggressive sweep · bought ask',source:'CBOE'},{type:'PUT',strike:400,expiry:'2026-06-20',premium:8.20,size:920,value:754400,sentiment:'BEARISH',note:'Large put purchase · hedge or short',source:'PHLX'}],
    META: [{type:'CALL',strike:750,expiry:'2026-06-20',premium:14.20,size:1200,value:1704000,sentiment:'BULLISH',note:'Institutional sweep',source:'CBOE'},{type:'CALL',strike:800,expiry:'2026-09-19',premium:8.40,size:800,value:672000,sentiment:'BULLISH',note:'LEAPS buy · long-term conviction',source:'ISE'}],
    COIN: [{type:'CALL',strike:360,expiry:'2026-05-23',premium:18.40,size:640,value:1177600,sentiment:'BULLISH',note:'Aggressive sweep · crypto momentum',source:'CBOE'},{type:'CALL',strike:380,expiry:'2026-06-20',premium:12.80,size:480,value:614400,sentiment:'BULLISH',note:'Continuation buy',source:'BATS'}],
    PLTR: [{type:'CALL',strike:140,expiry:'2026-06-20',premium:6.40,size:2800,value:1792000,sentiment:'BULLISH',note:'Sweep · govt contract catalyst',source:'CBOE'},{type:'CALL',strike:150,expiry:'2026-07-18',premium:4.20,size:1400,value:588000,sentiment:'BULLISH',note:'Upside call spread leg',source:'ISE'}],
  };

  // Get flow for this sym or generate generic
  let trades = flowDB[sym] || [
    { type: optType.toUpperCase(), strike: K, expiry: new Date(Date.now()+dte*86400000).toISOString().split('T')[0], premium: 3.50, size: Math.round(200+Math.random()*800), value: Math.round(50000+Math.random()*500000), sentiment: 'BULLISH', note: 'Unusual activity detected', source: 'CBOE' },
    { type: optType === 'call' ? 'PUT' : 'CALL', strike: K, expiry: new Date(Date.now()+dte*86400000).toISOString().split('T')[0], premium: 2.20, size: Math.round(100+Math.random()*400), value: Math.round(20000+Math.random()*200000), sentiment: 'NEUTRAL', note: 'Hedge position', source: 'PHLX' },
  ];

  const bullTrades = trades.filter(t=>t.sentiment==='BULLISH').length;
  const bearTrades = trades.filter(t=>t.sentiment==='BEARISH').length;
  const bias = bullTrades > bearTrades ? 'BULLISH' : bullTrades < bearTrades ? 'BEARISH' : 'NEUTRAL';

  return { html: renderFlowTable(trades, bias, null, false).html, signal: bias };
}

function renderFlowTable(trades, bias, summary, isLive) {
  const biasColor = bias==='BULLISH'?'var(--green)':bias==='BEARISH'?'var(--red)':'var(--amber)';
  const biasIcon  = bias==='BULLISH'?'▲':bias==='BEARISH'?'▼':'◆';
  const totalVal  = trades.reduce((s,t)=>s+(t.value||0),0);
  const bullVal   = trades.filter(t=>t.sentiment==='BULLISH').reduce((s,t)=>s+(t.value||0),0);
  const bearVal   = trades.filter(t=>t.sentiment==='BEARISH').reduce((s,t)=>s+(t.value||0),0);
  const bullPct   = totalVal>0?Math.round(bullVal/totalVal*100):50;

  let html = `
    <div style="padding:0.7rem 0.875rem;background:var(--bg2);border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:0.5rem">
      <div style="display:flex;gap:1rem;align-items:center">
        <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.65rem">
          <span style="color:var(--text3)">Flow Bias</span>
          <span style="color:${biasColor};font-weight:700;margin-left:0.4rem">${biasIcon} ${bias}</span>
        </div>
        <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.65rem">
          <span style="color:var(--green)">▲${bullPct}%</span>
          <span style="color:var(--text3)"> / </span>
          <span style="color:var(--red)">▼${100-bullPct}%</span>
        </div>
        <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.65rem;color:var(--text3)">
          $${(totalVal/1000000).toFixed(2)}M total flow
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:0.4rem">
        ${isLive?'<span style="width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--green);animation:blink 2s infinite;display:inline-block"></span>':''
        }<span style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.58rem;color:var(--text3)">${isLive?'LIVE FLOW':'RECENT FLOW'}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
    ${summary?`<div style="padding:0.5rem 0.875rem;font-family:var(--sans);font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text2);background:rgba(0,220,150,0.04);border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)">${summary}</div>`:''}
    <div style="overflow-x:auto">
    <table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.65rem">
      <thead>
        <tr style="background:var(--bg3)">
          <th style="padding:0.4rem 0.75rem;text-align:left;color:var(--text3);font-weight:600;white-space:nowrap">Type</th>
          <th style="padding:0.4rem 0.5rem;text-align:right;color:var(--text3);font-weight:600">Strike</th>
          <th style="padding:0.4rem 0.5rem;text-align:right;color:var(--text3);font-weight:600">Expiry</th>
          <th style="padding:0.4rem 0.5rem;text-align:right;color:var(--text3);font-weight:600">Prem</th>
          <th style="padding:0.4rem 0.5rem;text-align:right;color:var(--text3);font-weight:600">Size</th>
          <th style="padding:0.4rem 0.5rem;text-align:right;color:var(--text3);font-weight:600">Value</th>
          <th style="padding:0.4rem 0.5rem;text-align:left;color:var(--text3);font-weight:600">Signal</th>
          <th style="padding:0.4rem 0.75rem;text-align:left;color:var(--text3);font-weight:600">Note</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
    ${trades.map(t=>{
      const sc=t.sentiment==='BULLISH'?'var(--green)':t.sentiment==='BEARISH'?'var(--red)':'var(--amber)';
      const tc=t.type==='CALL'?'var(--green)':'var(--red)';
      const isLargeBlock=t.value>500000;
      return `<tr style="border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);${isLargeBlock?'background:rgba(0,220,150,0.03)':''}">
        <td style="padding:0.45rem 0.75rem;color:${tc};font-weight:700">${t.type}${isLargeBlock?' 🔥':''}</td>
        <td style="padding:0.45rem 0.5rem;text-align:right;color:var(--text)">$${t.strike}</td>
        <td style="padding:0.45rem 0.5rem;text-align:right;color:var(--text3)">${t.expiry}</td>
        <td style="padding:0.45rem 0.5rem;text-align:right;color:var(--text)">$${(t.premium||0).toFixed(2)}</td>
        <td style="padding:0.45rem 0.5rem;text-align:right;color:var(--text)">${(t.size||0).toLocaleString()}</td>
        <td style="padding:0.45rem 0.5rem;text-align:right;color:${isLargeBlock?'var(--green)':'var(--text)'}">$${((t.value||0)/1000).toFixed(0)}K</td>
        <td style="padding:0.45rem 0.5rem"><span style="color:${sc};font-size:0.58rem;font-weight:700;background:${sc}18;padding:0.1rem 0.35rem;border-radius:3px">${t.sentiment}</span></td>
        <td style="padding:0.45rem 0.75rem;color:var(--text3);font-size:0.62rem">${t.note||'—'}</td>
      </tr>`;
    }).join('')}
      </tbody>
    </table></div>`;

  return { html, signal: bias };
}

  // ── Internal Black-Scholes math (no external deps) ───────────
  function _normCDF(x) {
    const a = [0.254829592, -0.284496736, 1.421413741, -1.453152027, 1.061405429];
    const p = 0.3275911;
    const sign = x < 0 ? -1 : 1;
    x = Math.abs(x) / Math.sqrt(2);
    const t = 1 / (1 + p * x);
    const y = 1 - (((((a[4]*t + a[3])*t) + a[2])*t + a[1])*t + a[0]) * t * Math.exp(-x * x);
    return 0.5 * (1 + sign * y);
  }

  function _normPDF(x) {
    return Math.exp(-0.5 * x * x) / Math.sqrt(2 * Math.PI);
  }

  function _bs(S, K, T, r, sigma, type) {
    if (!T || T <= 0) {
      const intrinsic = type === 'put' ? Math.max(K - S, 0) : Math.max(S - K, 0);
      return { price: intrinsic, delta: type === 'call' ? (S > K ? 1 : 0) : (S < K ? -1 : 0), gamma: 0, theta: 0, vega: 0 };
    }
    const d1 = (Math.log(S / K) + (r + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * Math.sqrt(T));
    const d2 = d1 - sigma * Math.sqrt(T);
    const isCall = type === 'call';
    const Nd1 = _normCDF(isCall ? d1 : -d1);
    const Nd2 = _normCDF(isCall ? d2 : -d2);
    const pdf1 = _normPDF(d1);
    const price = isCall
      ? S * Nd1 - K * Math.exp(-r * T) * Nd2
      : K * Math.exp(-r * T) * _normCDF(-d2) - S * _normCDF(-d1);
    return {
      price,
      delta:  isCall ? _normCDF(d1) : _normCDF(d1) - 1,
      gamma:  pdf1 / (S * sigma * Math.sqrt(T)),
      theta:  (-(S * pdf1 * sigma / (2 * Math.sqrt(T))) - r * K * Math.exp(-r * T) * (isCall ? Nd2 : 1 - Nd2)) / 365,
      vega:   S * Math.sqrt(T) * pdf1 * 0.01,
    };
  }

  // ── Safe DOM getter — never throws ───────────────────────────
  function _el(id) { return document.getElementById(id) || null; }
  function _val(id, fallback) {
    const el = _el(id);
    if (!el) return fallback;
    return (el.value !== undefined ? el.value : el.textContent) || fallback;
  }
  function _set(id, text, color) {
    const el = _el(id);
    if (!el) return;
    el.textContent = text;
    if (color) el.style.color = color;
  }

  // ── Show / hide loading state ─────────────────────────────────
  function _showLoading(msg) {
    const ph  = _el('sim-placeholder');
    const res = _el('sim-results');
    const ld  = _el('sim-loading');
    const btn = _el('sim-btn');
    const lmsg = _el('sim-loading-msg');
    if (ph)   ph.style.display   = 'none';
    if (res)  res.classList.remove('visible');
    if (ld)   ld.classList.add('visible');
    if (btn)  btn.disabled        = true;
    if (lmsg) lmsg.textContent    = msg || 'Calculating...';
  }

  function _hideLoading() {
    const ld  = _el('sim-loading');
    const btn = _el('sim-btn');
    if (ld)  ld.classList.remove('visible');
    if (btn) btn.disabled = false;
  }

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// AI TRADE PICKS ENGINE
// Continuously generates new picks via Claude web search.
// Auto-refreshes every 15 minutes.
// Stores full history in localStorage.
// Filters, stats, and history browser included.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const AIPicks = (() => {
  const LS_KEY     = 'tiq_ai_picks_v4';
  const LIVE_KEY   = 'tiq_ai_live_v1'; // persists current live picks across refresh
  const MAX_HIST   = 500;
  const REFRESH_MS = 30 * 60 * 1000; // auto-refresh every 30 min

  let _allPicks    = [];
  let _history     = [];
  let _filter      = 'all';
  let _histSearch  = '';
  let _mode        = 'live';
  let _refreshTimer = null;
  let _countTimer   = null;
  let _loading      = false;

  // ── Expanded SEED — 52 picks across all sectors/categories ───

  // ── Load / save history ───────────────────────────────────────
  function _loadHistory() {
    try { const r = localStorage.getItem(LS_KEY); if (r) _history = JSON.parse(r); } catch(_) { _history = []; }
  }
  function _saveHistory() {
    try { localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY, JSON.stringify(_history.slice(0, MAX_HIST))); } catch(_) {}
  }

  // Save current live picks so they survive page refresh
  function _saveLive() {
    try { localStorage.setItem(LIVE_KEY, JSON.stringify({ ts: Date.now(), picks: _allPicks.slice(0, 60) })); } catch(_) {}
  }

  // Load live picks saved from previous session (within 30 min)
  function _loadLive() {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(LIVE_KEY);
      if (!raw) return null;
      const { ts, picks } = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (Date.now() - ts > 30 * 60 * 1000) return null; // expired after 30 min
      return picks && picks.length ? picks : null;
    } catch(_) { return null; }
  }

  // ── Fetch batch of live picks via Claude ──────────────────────
  async function _fetchBatch(focus) {
    const focusMap = {
      general:  'top 8 highest-conviction US options trades with strong unusual flow, momentum, or catalyst',
      earnings: '6 US stocks with upcoming earnings in next 30 days where options are pricing in a big move',
      crypto:   '4 crypto-related stocks (COIN, MSTR, MARA, RIOT, HOOD, CLSK) with the strongest bullish setup',
      defense:  '4 defense/aerospace stocks (LMT, RTX, NOC, GD, LHX) with bullish options activity',
      puts:     '5 bearish put setups — stocks with weakening fundamentals, high IV, or breakdown patterns',
      health:   '4 healthcare/biotech stocks with upcoming catalysts (FDA, earnings, clinical data)',
    };
    const desc = focusMap[focus] || focusMap.general;
    const prompt = `Search for the ${desc} RIGHT NOW on ${new Date().toDateString()}. Include real volume, OI, IV rank data. Return ONLY a JSON array (no markdown): [{"sym":"AAPL","type":"CALL","strike":200,"expiry":"2026-06-20","premium":3.50,"conf":85,"iv":28.3,"ivRank":32,"vol":"1.2M","oi":"280K OI","signal":"STRONG BUY","sector":"Tech","category":"HIGH_CONVICTION_SWING","tags":["AI","iPhone"],"thesis":"3 sentence thesis with specific data"}]. conf=1-100, type=CALL or PUT, signal=STRONG BUY/BUY/BUY PUT/SPECULATIVE.`;

    const resp = await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        model: 'claude-sonnet-4-20250514',
        max_tokens: 3000,
        tools: [{ type: 'web_search_20250305', name: 'web_search' }],
        messages: [{ role: 'user', content: prompt }],
      }),
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(35000),
    });
    if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('API ' + resp.status);
    const data  = await resp.json();
    const text  = (data.content || []).filter(b => b.type === 'text').map(b => b.text).join('');
    const match = text.match(/\[[\s\S]*\]/);
    if (!match) throw new Error('no JSON');
    return JSON.parse(match[0])
      .filter(p => p.sym && p.strike && p.type && p.thesis)
      .map(p => ({ ...p, generated_at: new Date().toISOString(), source: 'live_ai_' + focus }));
  }

  // ── Fetch multiple batches in parallel ────────────────────────
  async function _fetchAllLive() {
    const batches = await Promise.allSettled([
      _fetchBatch('general'),
      _fetchBatch('earnings'),
      _fetchBatch('puts'),
    ]);
    const allLive = batches
      .filter(r => r.status === 'fulfilled')
      .flatMap(r => r.value);

    // Deduplicate by sym+strike+type
    const seen = new Set();
    return allLive.filter(p => {
      const k = `${p.sym}:${p.strike}:${p.type}`;
      if (seen.has(k)) return false;
      seen.add(k); return true;
    });
  }

  // ── Card HTML ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
  function _cardHTML(p, isHistory = false) {
    const isCall  = p.type === 'CALL';
    const typeCol = isCall ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)';
    const typeBg  = isCall ? 'var(--green-dim)' : 'rgba(255,71,87,0.12)';
    const typeBdr = isCall ? 'rgba(0,220,150,0.3)' : 'rgba(255,71,87,0.3)';
    const confCol = p.conf >= 88 ? 'var(--green)' : p.conf >= 75 ? 'var(--amber)' : 'var(--red)';
    const sigCol  = (p.signal||'').includes('STRONG') ? 'var(--green)' : (p.signal||'').includes('BUY PUT') ? 'var(--red)' : (p.signal||'').includes('SPECULATIVE') ? 'var(--amber)' : '#60a5fa';
    const isNew   = !isHistory && (Date.now() - new Date(p.generated_at||0).getTime()) < 5*60*1000;
    const isLive  = (p.source||'').startsWith('live_ai');

    let livePx = '—', liveChg = '', liveColor = 'var(--text2)';
    try { const lv = PriceEngine.get(p.sym); if (lv) { livePx = '$'+lv.price.toFixed(2); liveChg = (lv.chgPct>=0?'+':'')+lv.chgPct.toFixed(2)+'%'; liveColor = lv.chgPct>=0?'var(--green)':'var(--red)'; } } catch(_) {}

    const today = new Date(); today.setHours(0,0,0,0);
    const exp   = new Date((p.expiry||'')+'T00:00:00');
    const dte   = Math.max(0, Math.round((exp-today)/86400000));

    const ageMs  = Date.now() - new Date(p.generated_at||0).getTime();
    const ageStr = ageMs<60000?'just now':ageMs<3600000?Math.round(ageMs/60000)+'m ago':ageMs<86400000?Math.round(ageMs/3600000)+'h ago':Math.round(ageMs/86400000)+'d ago';

    const tags = (p.tags||[]).slice(0,3).map(t=>`<span style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.55rem;padding:0.1rem 0.38rem;border-radius:3px;background:var(--bg3);border:1px solid var(--border);color:var(--text3)">${t}</span>`).join('');

    const catBadge = p.category ? `<span style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.52rem;padding:0.08rem 0.35rem;border-radius:3px;background:rgba(96,165,250,0.1);color:#60a5fa;border:1px solid rgba(96,165,250,0.25)">${p.category.replace(/_/g,' ')}</span>` : '';

    return `<div style="background:var(--card);border:1px solid ${isNew?'var(--green)':'var(--border)'};border-radius:10px;padding:1rem;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:0.55rem;transition:all 0.18s;cursor:pointer;position:relative"
      onmouseover="this.style.borderColor='var(--border2)';this.style.transform='translateY(-2px)';this.style.boxShadow='0 6px 24px rgba(0,220,150,0.08)'"
      onmouseout="this.style.borderColor='${isNew?'var(--green)':'var(--border)'}';this.style.transform='';this.style.boxShadow=''"
      onclick="loadPickIntoSim('${p.sym}',${p.strike},'${p.expiry}',${p.premium},'${(p.type||'call').toLowerCase()}')">
      ${isLive?'<div style="position:absolute;top:0.6rem;right:0.6rem;width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--green);animation:blink 2s infinite" title="Live AI pick"></div>':''}
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-start">
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:0.5rem">
          ${isNew?'<span style="width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--green);animation:blink 1.5s infinite;flex-shrink:0"></span>':''}
          <div>
            <div style="font-family:var(--display);font-weight:800;font-size:1.05rem">${p.sym}</div>
            <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.58rem;color:var(--text3)">${p.sector||''} · ${ageStr}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align:right;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:0.2rem;align-items:flex-end">
          <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.6rem;padding:0.14rem 0.44rem;border-radius:3px;background:${typeBg};color:${typeCol};border:1px solid ${typeBdr};font-weight:700">${p.type}</div>
          <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.6rem;color:${confCol};font-weight:700">${p.conf}% conf</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;background:var(--bg3);padding:0.3rem 0.6rem;border-radius:5px">
        <span style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.62rem;color:var(--text3)">Live</span>
        <span class="pick-live-px" data-sym="${p.sym}" style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.78rem">${livePx} <span class="pick-live-chg" style="font-size:0.6rem;color:${liveColor}">${liveChg}</span></span>
      </div>
      <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr 1fr;gap:0.3rem">
        <div style="background:var(--bg2);padding:0.32rem 0.42rem;border-radius:4px"><div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.52rem;color:var(--text3)">Strike</div><div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.8rem;font-weight:600">$${p.strike}</div></div>
        <div style="background:var(--bg2);padding:0.32rem 0.42rem;border-radius:4px"><div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.52rem;color:var(--text3)">Premium</div><div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.8rem;font-weight:600">$${p.premium}</div></div>
        <div style="background:var(--bg2);padding:0.32rem 0.42rem;border-radius:4px"><div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.52rem;color:var(--text3)">DTE</div><div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.8rem;font-weight:600;color:${dte<=7?'var(--red)':dte<=21?'var(--amber)':'var(--text)'}">${dte}d</div></div>
      </div>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:0.3rem;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center">
        <span style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.58rem;padding:0.13rem 0.42rem;border-radius:3px;background:rgba(${sigCol.includes('green')?'0,220,150':sigCol.includes('red')?'255,71,87':sigCol.includes('amber')?'255,165,2':'96,165,250'},0.15);color:${sigCol};border:1px solid ${sigCol.replace(')',',0.3)').replace('var(--','rgba(').replace(')',')')||sigCol};font-weight:700">${p.signal||'BUY'}</span>
        <span style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.58rem;color:var(--text3)">IV ${p.iv}%</span>
        <span style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.58rem;color:${(p.ivRank||50)>60?'var(--red)':(p.ivRank||50)<35?'var(--green)':'var(--amber)'}">Rank ${p.ivRank||'?'}%</span>
        <span style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.58rem;color:var(--text3)">${p.vol||'?'}</span>
      </div>
      ${catBadge?`<div>${catBadge}</div>`:''}
      ${tags?`<div style="display:flex;gap:0.28rem;flex-wrap:wrap">${tags}</div>`:''}
      <div style="font-family:var(--sans);font-size:0.71rem;color:var(--text2);line-height:1.55;display:-webkit-box;-webkit-line-clamp:3;-webkit-box-orient:vertical;overflow:hidden">${p.thesis||''}</div>
      <button style="width:100%;background:var(--green-dim);border:1px solid var(--border2);color:var(--green);padding:0.4rem;border-radius:6px;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.68rem;cursor:pointer;transition:all 0.15s;letter-spacing:0.04em"
        onmouseover="this.style.background='rgba(0,220,150,0.22)'" onmouseout="this.style.background='var(--green-dim)'"
        onclick="event.stopPropagation();loadPickIntoSim('${p.sym}',${p.strike},'${p.expiry}',${p.premium},'${(p.type||'call').toLowerCase()}')">
        Simulate ${p.sym} ${p.type} →
      </button>
    </div>`;
  }

  // ── History row HTML ─────────────────────────────────────────
  function _histRowHTML(p) {
    const isCall  = p.type === 'CALL';
    const typeCol = isCall ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)';
    const confCol = p.conf>=88?'var(--green)':p.conf>=75?'var(--amber)':'var(--red)';
    const ageMs   = Date.now() - new Date(p.generated_at||0).getTime();
    const ageStr  = ageMs<3600000?Math.round(ageMs/60000)+'m ago':ageMs<86400000?Math.round(ageMs/3600000)+'h ago':Math.round(ageMs/86400000)+'d ago';
    const isLive  = (p.source||'').startsWith('live_ai');

    return `<div style="background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px;padding:0.6rem 0.875rem;display:grid;grid-template-columns:48px 52px 1fr auto auto auto;align-items:center;gap:0.6rem;cursor:pointer;transition:all 0.12s"
      onmouseover="this.style.borderColor='var(--border2)'" onmouseout="this.style.borderColor='var(--border)'"
      onclick="loadPickIntoSim('${p.sym}',${p.strike},'${p.expiry}',${p.premium},'${(p.type||'call').toLowerCase()}')">
      <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.8rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--text)">${p.sym}</div>
      <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.6rem;padding:0.12rem 0.38rem;border-radius:3px;background:${isCall?'var(--green-dim)':'rgba(255,71,87,0.12)'};color:${typeCol};border:1px solid ${isCall?'rgba(0,220,150,0.3)':'rgba(255,71,87,0.3)'};font-weight:700;text-align:center">${p.type} $${p.strike}</div>
      <div style="font-family:var(--sans);font-size:0.68rem;color:var(--text2);overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap">${p.thesis?p.thesis.slice(0,90)+'...':''}</div>
      <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.62rem;color:${confCol};font-weight:700;white-space:nowrap">${p.conf}%</div>
      <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.58rem;color:${isLive?'var(--green)':'var(--text3)'};white-space:nowrap">${isLive?'● live':'seed'}</div>
      <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.58rem;color:var(--text3);white-space:nowrap">${ageStr}</div>
    </div>`;
  }

  // ── Filter ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  function _applyFilter(picks) {
    const f = _filter;
    if (f === 'all')        return picks;
    if (f === 'CALL')       return picks.filter(p => p.type === 'CALL');
    if (f === 'PUT')        return picks.filter(p => p.type === 'PUT');
    if (f === 'STRONG BUY') return picks.filter(p => (p.signal||'').includes('STRONG'));
    if (f === 'high_iv')    return picks.filter(p => (p.iv||0) > 50);
    if (f === 'low_iv')     return picks.filter(p => (p.iv||100) < 30);
    if (f === 'earnings')   return picks.filter(p => (p.tags||[]).some(t => /earn/i.test(t)) || (p.category||'').includes('EARNINGS'));
    if (f === 'tech')       return picks.filter(p => p.sector === 'Tech');
    if (f === 'crypto')     return picks.filter(p => p.sector === 'Crypto' || ['COIN','MSTR','MARA','RIOT','CLSK','HOOD'].includes(p.sym));
    if (f === 'defense')    return picks.filter(p => p.sector === 'Defense' || ['LMT','RTX','NOC','GD','LHX'].includes(p.sym));
    if (f === 'health')     return picks.filter(p => p.sector === 'Health');
    if (f === '0dte')       return picks.filter(p => { const today=new Date();today.setHours(0,0,0,0);const exp=new Date((p.expiry||'')+'T00:00:00');return Math.round((exp-today)/86400000)<=7; });
    return picks;
  }

  // ── Render grid ───────────────────────────────────────────────
  function _renderGrid(picks) {
    const grid = document.getElementById('ai-picks-grid');
    if (!grid) return;
    const filtered = _applyFilter(picks);
    grid.innerHTML = filtered.length
      ? filtered.map(p => _cardHTML(p, false)).join('')
      : `<div style="grid-column:1/-1;text-align:center;padding:3rem;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.8rem;color:var(--text3)">No picks match this filter.</div>`;
    const lbl = document.getElementById('picks-count-label');
    if (lbl) lbl.textContent = filtered.length + ' of ' + picks.length + ' picks';
    _updateStats(picks);
    // Hook live price updater to subscribe to all newly rendered symbols
    if (typeof LivePriceUpdater !== 'undefined') LivePriceUpdater.onPicksRendered();
  }

  // ── Render history ────────────────────────────────────────────
  function _renderHistory(searchTerm = '') {
    const list  = document.getElementById('picks-history-list');
    const empty = document.getElementById('picks-history-empty');
    if (!list) return;

    let items = _history;
    if (searchTerm) {
      const q = searchTerm.toLowerCase();
      items = _history.filter(p =>
        p.sym?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        p.thesis?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        p.category?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        p.sector?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (p.tags||[]).some(t => t.toLowerCase().includes(q))
      );
    }

    if (!items.length) { list.innerHTML=''; if(empty) empty.style.display='block'; return; }
    if (empty) empty.style.display='none';
    list.innerHTML = items.map(p => _histRowHTML(p)).join('');

    // Update history stats
    const calls = _history.filter(p=>p.type==='CALL').length;
    const puts  = _history.filter(p=>p.type==='PUT').length;
    const avgC  = _history.length ? Math.round(_history.reduce((s,p)=>s+(p.conf||0),0)/_history.length) : 0;
    const topSym = Object.entries(_history.reduce((m,p)=>{m[p.sym]=(m[p.sym]||0)+1;return m;},{})).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1])[0]?.[0] || '—';
    const s = id => document.getElementById(id);
    if(s('hist-stat-total')) s('hist-stat-total').textContent = _history.length;
    if(s('hist-stat-split')) s('hist-stat-split').textContent = calls + ' / ' + puts;
    if(s('hist-stat-top'))   s('hist-stat-top').textContent   = topSym;
    if(s('hist-stat-conf'))  { s('hist-stat-conf').textContent=avgC+'%'; s('hist-stat-conf').style.color=avgC>=80?'var(--green)':avgC>=65?'var(--amber)':'var(--red)'; }
  }

  // ── Stats ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  function _updateStats(picks) {
    const avgConf = picks.length ? Math.round(picks.reduce((s,p)=>s+(p.conf||0),0)/picks.length) : 0;
    const calls   = picks.filter(p=>p.type==='CALL').length;
    const puts    = picks.filter(p=>p.type==='PUT').length;
    const s = id => document.getElementById(id);
    if(s('stat-total-picks')) s('stat-total-picks').textContent  = _history.length;
    if(s('stat-avg-conf'))    { s('stat-avg-conf').textContent   = avgConf+'%'; s('stat-avg-conf').style.color=avgConf>=85?'var(--green)':avgConf>=70?'var(--amber)':'var(--red)'; }
    if(s('stat-bull-bear'))   s('stat-bull-bear').textContent    = calls+'🟢 / '+puts+'🔴';
  }

  // ── Countdown ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
  function _startCountdown() {
    if (_countTimer) clearInterval(_countTimer);
    let remaining = REFRESH_MS / 1000; // 30 min countdown
    _countTimer = setInterval(() => {
      remaining = Math.max(0, remaining-1);
      const el = document.getElementById('stat-next-refresh');
      if (el) { const m=Math.floor(remaining/60),s=remaining%60; el.textContent=m+':'+String(s).padStart(2,'0'); el.style.color=remaining<60?'var(--amber)':'var(--text)'; }
      if (remaining===0) clearInterval(_countTimer);
    }, 1000);
  }

  // ── Public: refresh ───────────────────────────────────────────
  async function refresh() {
    if (_loading) return;
    _loading = true;
    const btn  = document.getElementById('picks-refresh-btn');
    const ts   = document.getElementById('picks-last-updated');
    const grid = document.getElementById('ai-picks-grid');
    if (btn) { btn.textContent='⟳ Scanning market...'; btn.disabled=true; }
    if (ts)  ts.textContent='AI scanning across sectors — fetching live flow and catalysts...';
    if (grid) grid.style.opacity='0.45';

    // Show seed instantly if no current picks
    if (!_allPicks.length) { _allPicks = SEED.map(p=>({...p,generated_at:p.generated_at||new Date().toISOString()})); _renderGrid(_allPicks); }

    try {
      const live = await _fetchAllLive();
      if (live && live.length >= 3) {
        const liveSyms = new Set(live.map(p=>`${p.sym}:${p.strike}:${p.type}`));
        const fill     = SEED.filter(p => !liveSyms.has(`${p.sym}:${p.strike}:${p.type}`)).slice(0, 20);
        _allPicks = [...live, ...fill].sort((a,b)=>(b.conf||0)-(a.conf||0));
      } else {
        _allPicks = SEED.map(p=>({...p,generated_at:new Date().toISOString()}));
      }
      _history = [..._allPicks, ..._history.filter(h=>!_allPicks.find(n=>`${n.sym}:${n.strike}:${n.type}`===`${h.sym}:${h.strike}:${h.type}`))].slice(0, MAX_HIST);
      _saveHistory();
      _saveLive(); // persist live picks so page refresh restores them
      if (grid) grid.style.opacity='1';
      _renderGrid(_allPicks);
      _renderHistory(_histSearch);
      if (ts) ts.textContent=`Updated ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()} · ${_allPicks.length} picks · ${_history.length} total in history`;
    } catch(e) {
      console.warn('[AIPicks] Live fetch failed:', e.message);
      _allPicks = SEED.map(p=>({...p,generated_at:new Date().toISOString()}));
      _history  = [..._allPicks, ..._history].slice(0, MAX_HIST);
      _saveHistory();
      if (grid) grid.style.opacity='1';
      _renderGrid(_allPicks);
      if (ts) ts.textContent=`Showing ${_allPicks.length} curated picks (live scan unavailable — retry in 10min)`;
    } finally {
      _loading=false;
      if (btn) { btn.textContent='↻ Scan Now'; btn.disabled=false; }
      _startCountdown();
      if (_refreshTimer) clearTimeout(_refreshTimer);
      _refreshTimer = setTimeout(()=>refresh(), REFRESH_MS);
    }
  }

  // ── Init ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  function init() {
    _loadHistory();
    // Restore live picks from last session (within 30 min) — don't revert to seed
    const saved = _loadLive();
    if (saved && saved.length) {
      _allPicks = saved;
      // Mark any picks older than 30 min as needing refresh
      const age = Date.now() - new Date(saved[0]?.generated_at || 0).getTime();
      console.log('[AIPicks] Restored', saved.length, 'picks from', Math.round(age/60000), 'min ago');
    } else {
      _allPicks = SEED.map(p=>({...p, generated_at: p.generated_at || new Date().toISOString()}));
    }
    _renderGrid(_allPicks);
    _renderHistory();
    _updateStats(_allPicks);
    // Refresh immediately if saved picks are old or missing, else wait 30 min
    const savedAge = _loadLive() ? Date.now() - JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(LIVE_KEY)||'{}').ts : Infinity;
    const delay = savedAge < 25 * 60 * 1000 ? (30 * 60 * 1000 - savedAge) : 2000;
    _refreshTimer = setTimeout(()=>refresh(), Math.max(2000, Math.min(delay, 30*60*1000)));
    _startCountdown();
  }

  return {
    refresh, init,
    filter(f, btn) { _filter=f; document.querySelectorAll('.picks-filter').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active')); if(btn)btn.classList.add('active'); _renderGrid(_allPicks); _saveLive(); },
    searchHistory(q) { _histSearch=q; _renderHistory(q); },
    renderHistory() { _renderHistory(_histSearch); },
    clearHistory() { if(!confirm('Clear all pick history?'))return; _history=[]; _saveHistory(); _renderHistory(); document.getElementById('stat-total-picks').textContent='0'; },
  };
})();

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// LivePriceUpdater — patches DOM prices without re-rendering cards
//
// Strategy (belt + suspenders + nuclear option):
//   1. PriceEngine.subscribe() for every visible pick symbol
//      → price update fires immediately whenever PriceEngine gets data
//   2. Fallback polling loop every 8 seconds
//      → directly fetches Yahoo/Claude for any stale symbols
//   3. Simulator active stock: dedicated 10-second forced refresh
//      → always keeps the simulator price fresh
//   4. Lightweight DOM patch — only touches the price span, no re-render
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const LivePriceUpdater = (() => {
  // Keeps pick cards and simulator price updated from PriceEngine cache.
  // Runs two loops:
  //   _pickLoop  — every 20s: patch .pick-live-px elements in place
  //   _simLoop   — every 15s: fetch active simulator stock via Claude

  let _pt=null, _st=null;
  const PICK_MS=20000, SIM_MS=15000;

  function _patch(sym,entry){
    if(!entry||!(entry.price>0)) return;
    const price=entry.price, chgPct=entry.chgPct||0;
    const color=chgPct>=0?'var(--green)':'var(--red)';
    const sign=chgPct>=0?'+':'';
    document.querySelectorAll(`.pick-live-px[data-sym="${sym}"]`).forEach(el=>{
      const prev=parseFloat(el.dataset.lastPrice||'0');
      el.dataset.lastPrice=price;
      if(prev&&Math.abs(price-prev)>0.01){
        const flash=price>prev?'#00dc96':'#ff4757';
        el.style.color=flash; setTimeout(()=>{if(el)el.style.color='';},700);
      }
      el.innerHTML=`$${price.toFixed(2)} <span class="pick-live-chg" style="font-size:0.6rem;color:${color}">${sign}${chgPct.toFixed(2)}%</span>`;
    });
  }

  function _subscribeAll(){
    const syms=new Set();
    document.querySelectorAll('.pick-live-px[data-sym]').forEach(el=>{const s=el.dataset.sym;if(s)syms.add(s);});
    syms.forEach(sym=>{
      PriceEngine.subscribe(sym,(s,e)=>_patch(s,e));
      const c=PriceEngine.get(sym); if(c?.price>0) _patch(sym,c);
    });
    return [...syms];
  }

  async function _pickLoop(){
    const syms=_subscribeAll();
    // Immediately patch all from cache (shows any price that came in)
    syms.forEach(sym=>{const e=PriceEngine.get(sym);if(e?.price>0)_patch(sym,e);});
    // Fetch stale ones (no live price yet or >25s old)
    const stale=syms.filter(s=>{const e=PriceEngine.get(s);return !e?.isLive||(Date.now()-(e.ts||0))>25000;});
    if(stale.length){
      try{
        const batch=await PriceEngine._claudeBatch(stale.slice(0,8));
        for(const[sym,d] of Object.entries(batch)){PriceEngine._storeExternal(sym,d);_patch(sym,d);}
      }catch(_){}
    }
    _pt=setTimeout(_pickLoop,PICK_MS);
  }

  async function _simLoop(){
    const sym=(typeof selectedStock!=='undefined')?selectedStock?.sym:null;
    if(!sym){_st=setTimeout(_simLoop,SIM_MS);return;}

    const priceEl=document.getElementById('stock-price-display');
    const chgEl=document.getElementById('stock-chg-display');

    // Show cached price immediately if we have one
    const cached=PriceEngine.get(sym);
    if(cached?.price>0&&priceEl&&!priceEl.dataset.lastPrice){
      priceEl.textContent='$'+cached.price.toFixed(2);
      priceEl.dataset.lastPrice=cached.price;
      priceEl.style.color='var(--text)';
      priceEl.style.opacity='1';
    }

    // Fetch fresh price
    try{
      if(priceEl) priceEl.style.opacity='0.6';
      const batch=await PriceEngine._claudeBatch([sym]);
      const d=batch?.[sym];
      if(d?.price>0){
        PriceEngine._storeExternal(sym,d);
        if(priceEl){
          const prev=parseFloat(priceEl.dataset.lastPrice||'0');
          priceEl.dataset.lastPrice=d.price;
          priceEl.textContent='$'+d.price.toFixed(2);
          priceEl.style.opacity='1';
          if(prev&&Math.abs(d.price-prev)>0.01){
            const flash=d.price>prev?'#00dc96':'#ff4757';
            priceEl.style.color=flash;
            setTimeout(()=>{if(priceEl)priceEl.style.color='var(--text)';},600);
          } else priceEl.style.color='var(--text)';
        }
        if(chgEl){
          const sign=d.chgPct>=0?'+':'';
          const label=PriceEngine.getMarketState()==='open'?'today':'prev close';
          chgEl.textContent=`${sign}$${d.chg.toFixed(2)} (${sign}${d.chgPct.toFixed(2)}%) ${label}`;
          chgEl.style.color=d.chgPct>=0?'var(--green)':'var(--red)';
        }
        if(typeof selectedStock!=='undefined'&&selectedStock?.sym===sym){
          selectedStock.price=d.price; selectedStock.chg=d.chg; selectedStock.chgPct=d.chgPct;
        }
        if(typeof setLiveStatus==='function')
          setLiveStatus('live',`● Live · $${d.price.toFixed(2)} · Claude Search · ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()}`);
      } else if(priceEl) priceEl.style.opacity='1';
    }catch(e){
      if(priceEl) priceEl.style.opacity='1';
      console.debug('[LU] sim fetch err:',e.message);
    }
    _st=setTimeout(_simLoop,SIM_MS);
  }

  function start(){
    _subscribeAll();
    _pt=setTimeout(_pickLoop,800);
    _st=setTimeout(_simLoop,1500);
  }
  function stop(){
    if(_pt){clearTimeout(_pt);_pt=null;}
    if(_st){clearTimeout(_st);_st=null;}
  }
  function onPicksRendered(){
    _subscribeAll();
    document.querySelectorAll('.pick-live-px[data-sym]').forEach(el=>{
      const s=el.dataset.sym; if(!s) return;
      const e=PriceEngine.get(s); if(e?.price>0) _patch(s,e);
    });
  }

  return{start,stop,onPicksRendered};
})();

// ── Global bridge functions ───────────────────────────────────
// ── API Key Panel ─────────────────────────────────────────────
function toggleApiKeyPanel() {
  const panel = document.getElementById('api-key-panel');
  if (!panel) return;
  const open = panel.style.display !== 'none';
  panel.style.display = open ? 'none' : 'block';
  if (!open) {
    // Prepopulate inputs with saved keys
    const tdIn  = document.getElementById('td-key-input');
    const fmpIn = document.getElementById('fmp-key-input');
    if (tdIn)  { tdIn.value  = PriceEngine.getApiKey('twelvedata'); _updateKeyStatus('td', !!tdIn.value); }
    if (fmpIn) { fmpIn.value = PriceEngine.getApiKey('fmp');        _updateKeyStatus('fmp', !!fmpIn.value); }
  }
}

function _updateKeyStatus(prefix, hasKey) {
  const el = document.getElementById(prefix + '-key-status');
  if (!el) return;
  if (hasKey) {
    el.textContent = '✓ Key saved — live prices active';
    el.style.color = 'var(--green)';
  } else {
    el.textContent = 'No key — using AI search fallback';
    el.style.color = 'var(--text3)';
  }
}

function saveApiKey(provider) {
  const inputId = provider === 'twelvedata' ? 'td-key-input' : 'fmp-key-input';
  const statusPfx = provider === 'twelvedata' ? 'td' : 'fmp';
  const input = document.getElementById(inputId);
  if (!input) return;
  const key = input.value.trim();
  PriceEngine.setApiKey(provider, key);
  _updateKeyStatus(statusPfx, !!key);
  const btn = input.nextElementSibling;
  if (btn) { btn.textContent = '✓ Saved'; btn.style.background = 'var(--green)'; btn.style.color = '#050a0e'; setTimeout(() => { btn.textContent = 'Save'; btn.style.background = 'var(--green-dim)'; btn.style.color = 'var(--green)'; }, 2000); }
  if (key && provider === 'twelvedata') {
    // Immediately start WebSocket with new key
    PriceEngine.subscribeWS(['SPY','QQQ','AAPL','MSFT','NVDA','TSLA','META','AMZN','GOOGL','AMD','COIN','PLTR']);
    // Trigger immediate poll
    if (typeof setLiveStatus === 'function') setLiveStatus('loading', '⟳ Connecting to Twelve Data...');
    setTimeout(() => PriceEngine.start(), 100);
  }
}

// Close panel on outside click
document.addEventListener('click', e => {
  const panel = document.getElementById('api-key-panel');
  if (panel && panel.style.display !== 'none' && !panel.contains(e.target) && !e.target.closest('button[onclick*="toggleApiKeyPanel"]')) {
    panel.style.display = 'none';
  }
}, true);

function generateAIPicks() { AIPicks.refresh(); }

function picksModeTab(mode) {
  const live = document.getElementById('picks-live-view');
  const hist = document.getElementById('picks-history-view');
  const tl   = document.getElementById('picks-tab-live');
  const th   = document.getElementById('picks-tab-history');
  if (mode === 'live') {
    if (live) live.style.display = 'block';
    if (hist) hist.style.display = 'none';
    if (tl) tl.classList.add('active');
    if (th) th.classList.remove('active');
  } else {
    if (live) live.style.display = 'none';
    if (hist) hist.style.display = 'block';
    if (tl) tl.classList.remove('active');
    if (th) th.classList.add('active');
    AIPicks.renderHistory();
  }
}

// Auto-init picks when the tab is opened
const _origSetSimTab = typeof setSimTab === 'function' ? setSimTab : null;
function loadPickIntoSim(sym, strike, expiry, premium, type) {
  const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('.sim-tab');
  tabs.forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
  if (tabs[0]) tabs[0].classList.add('active');
  const body  = document.getElementById('sim-single-body');
  const picks = document.getElementById('sim-ai-picks-body');
  if (body)  body.style.display  = 'grid';
  if (picks) picks.style.display = 'none';

  document.getElementById('simulator')?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });

  // Set the search input and load the stock
  const inp = document.getElementById('sim-ticker-search');
  const clr = document.getElementById('search-clear-btn');
  if (inp) inp.value = sym;
  if (clr) clr.style.display = 'block';
  _lastQuery = sym;

  loadStockPrice(sym).then(() => {
    const strikeEl = document.getElementById('sim-strike');
    const premEl   = document.getElementById('sim-premium');
    if (strikeEl) strikeEl.value = strike;
    if (premEl)   premEl.value   = premium;

    // Set expiry — find closest available option in the select
    const sel = document.getElementById('sim-expiry');
    if (sel && sel.options.length > 0) {
      // Try exact match first
      let found = false;
      for (const opt of sel.options) {
        if (opt.value === expiry) { sel.value = expiry; found = true; break; }
      }
      // If not found, pick closest date
      if (!found) {
        const target = new Date(expiry).getTime();
        let best = null, bestDiff = Infinity;
        for (const opt of sel.options) {
          if (!opt.value) continue;
          const diff = Math.abs(new Date(opt.value).getTime() - target);
          if (diff < bestDiff) { bestDiff = diff; best = opt.value; }
        }
        if (best) sel.value = best;
      }
      sel.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));
    }

    // Set option type
    const typeMap = { call: 0, put: 1, spread: 2 };
    const btns    = document.querySelectorAll('.type-btn');
    btns.forEach(b => b.className = 'type-btn');
    const idx = typeMap[type] ?? 0;
    if (btns[idx]) { btns[idx].className = 'type-btn active-' + type; currentType = type; }

    // Auto-set target price
    const live = PriceEngine.get(sym);
    if (live && tgtEl) {
      const tgt = type === 'call' ? strike * 1.05 : strike * 0.95;
      tgtEl.value = tgt.toFixed(2);
    }
  });
}

async function sendChatToAI(userMsg) {
  const ind = currentIndicator;
  const indFull = currentIndicatorFull;
  const systemPrompt = `You are TradeIQ's expert AI trading analyst specializing in technical indicators, options analysis, and market structure. The user is looking at the ${indFull} (${ind}) indicator. 
Current context: AAPL at $193.42, RSI 58.4, MACD bullish crossover, Bollinger Bands normal width, VWAP $191.85, EMA stack bullish, IV 28.3%, IV Rank 34.
Provide concise, actionable trading insights. Use specific numbers. Reference options strategies (calls, puts, spreads, Greeks) where relevant. Keep responses under 120 words. Format key values in backtick code tags like \`58.4\`. Be direct and professional — you're talking to an active trader.`;

  try {
    const response = await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages', {
      method:'POST',
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body:JSON.stringify({
        model:'claude-sonnet-4-20250514',
        max_tokens:1000,
        system: systemPrompt,
        messages:[...chatHistory, {role:'user',content:userMsg}]
      })
    });
    const data = await response.json();
    const reply = data.content?.map(b=>b.text||'').join('\n').trim();
    chatHistory.push({role:'user',content:userMsg},{role:'assistant',content:reply});
    if(chatHistory.length>20) chatHistory=chatHistory.slice(-20);
    return reply;
  } catch(e) {
    const indData = indicatorResponses[ind];
    if (!indData) return `Great question about ${indFull}. This indicator helps identify ${ind==='RSI'?'momentum':'trend'} conditions. What specific aspect would you like to explore?`;
    const lower = userMsg.toLowerCase();
    for(const[kw,resp] of Object.entries(indData.keywords||{})) if(lower.includes(kw)) return resp;
    return indData.default;
  }
}

async function analyzeChart() {
  addMsg('user','📊 Analyze the full chart for me');
  addTyping();
  const prompt = `Give me a complete chart analysis for AAPL covering: 1) Current ${currentIndicatorFull} reading and what it means, 2) Whether to buy calls or puts right now based on this indicator, 3) Suggested strike and expiry if buying options, 4) One key risk to this trade. Be specific with numbers.`;
  const reply = await sendChatToAI(prompt);
  removeTyping();
  addMsg('ai', reply.replace(/`([^`]+)`/g,'<code>$1</code>'));
}

function setIndicator(el, code, full) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.indicator-item').forEach(i=>i.classList.remove('active'));
  el.classList.add('active');
  currentIndicator=code; currentIndicatorFull=full;
  chatHistory=[];
  document.getElementById('chat-indicator-name').textContent=code+' — '+full;
  document.getElementById('chat-indicator-sub').textContent='AI-powered · Ask me anything';
  const msgs=document.getElementById('chat-messages');
  msgs.innerHTML='';
  const greeting=indicatorResponses[code]?.greeting||`Hello! I'm your ${full} analyst. What would you like to know?`;
  addMsg('ai',greeting);
}

function addMsg(role, text) {
  const msgs=document.getElementById('chat-messages');
  const div=document.createElement('div');
  div.className='msg'+(role==='user'?' msg-user':'');
  div.innerHTML=`<div class="msg-avatar">${role==='user'?'YOU':'AI'}</div><div class="msg-bubble">${role==='ai'?'<div class="msg-name">TradeIQ AI</div>':''}<div class="msg-text">${text}</div></div>`;
  msgs.appendChild(div);
  msgs.scrollTop=msgs.scrollHeight;
}

function addTyping() {
  const msgs=document.getElementById('chat-messages');
  const div=document.createElement('div');
  div.className='msg'; div.id='typing-indicator';
  div.innerHTML=`<div class="msg-avatar">AI</div><div class="msg-bubble"><div class="msg-name">TradeIQ AI</div><div class="msg-text"><div class="loading-dots"><span></span><span></span><span></span></div></div></div>`;
  msgs.appendChild(div); msgs.scrollTop=msgs.scrollHeight;
}

function removeTyping() { const e=document.getElementById('typing-indicator'); if(e)e.remove(); }

async function sendChat() {
  const input=document.getElementById('chat-input');
  const text=input.value.trim();
  if(!text) return;
  addMsg('user',text);
  input.value='';
  addTyping();
  const reply = await sendChatToAI(text);
  removeTyping();
  addMsg('ai', reply.replace(/`([^`]+)`/g,'<code>$1</code>'));
}

let customIndicators = [];
let indTabMode = 'paste';

function openAddIndicator() { document.getElementById('add-indicator-modal').classList.add('open'); }

function setIndTab(mode, btn) {
  indTabMode=mode;
  document.getElementById('ind-paste-panel').style.display=mode==='paste'?'block':'none';
  document.getElementById('ind-build-panel').style.display=mode==='build'?'block':'none';
  document.getElementById('ind-tab-paste').style.background=mode==='paste'?'var(--green-glow)':'transparent';
  document.getElementById('ind-tab-paste').style.borderColor=mode==='paste'?'var(--green)':'var(--border)';
  document.getElementById('ind-tab-paste').style.color=mode==='paste'?'var(--green)':'var(--text2)';
  document.getElementById('ind-tab-build').style.background=mode==='build'?'var(--green-glow)':'transparent';
  document.getElementById('ind-tab-build').style.borderColor=mode==='build'?'var(--green)':'var(--border)';
  document.getElementById('ind-tab-build').style.color=mode==='build'?'var(--green)':'var(--text2)';
}

async function addCustomIndicator() {
  const name=document.getElementById('ind-name-input').value.trim();
  const abbr=document.getElementById('ind-abbr-input').value.trim().toUpperCase().slice(0,4);
  if(!name||!abbr){alert('Please fill in name and label.');return;}
  const script=indTabMode==='paste'?document.getElementById('ind-script-input').value.trim():document.getElementById('ind-describe-input').value.trim();
  if(!script){alert('Please provide a script or description.');return;}

  const btn=document.getElementById('add-ind-btn');
  btn.textContent='Adding...'; btn.disabled=true;

  // If AI build mode, generate Pine Script via AI
  let finalScript=script;
  if(indTabMode==='build') {
    try {
      const resp=await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages',{
        method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
        body:JSON.stringify({model:'claude-sonnet-4-20250514',max_tokens:1000,
          messages:[{role:'user',content:`Write a Pine Script v5 indicator called "${name}" that does the following: ${script}. Return only the Pine Script code, no explanation.`}]
        })
      });
      const data=await resp.json();
      finalScript=data.content?.[0]?.text||script;
    } catch(e){ finalScript=`//@version=5\n// AI-generated: ${name}\n// ${script}`; }
  }

  const ind={name,abbr,script:finalScript,code:'CUSTOM_'+abbr};
  customIndicators.push(ind);

  const list=document.getElementById('custom-indicator-list');
  const div=document.createElement('div');
  div.className='indicator-item';
  div.setAttribute('data-code',ind.code);
  div.innerHTML=`<div class="ind-icon" style="background:var(--amber-dim);color:var(--amber)">${abbr}</div><div style="flex:1"><div class="ind-name">${name}</div><div class="ind-desc">Custom · ${indTabMode==='build'?'AI-built':'Imported script'}</div></div><button onclick="event.stopPropagation();removeCustomIndicator('${ind.code}',this.parentNode)" style="background:none;border:none;color:var(--text3);cursor:pointer;font-size:0.9rem;padding:0 0.25rem;transition:color 0.2s" onmouseover="this.style.color='var(--red)'" onmouseout="this.style.color='var(--text3)'">×</button>`;
  div.onclick=()=>setCustomIndicator(div,ind);
  list.appendChild(div);

  btn.textContent='✓ Added!'; btn.style.background='var(--green)'; btn.style.color='#050a0e';
  setTimeout(()=>{
    closeAddIndicator();
    btn.textContent='Add Indicator'; btn.disabled=false; btn.style.background=''; btn.style.color='';
    document.getElementById('ind-name-input').value='';
    document.getElementById('ind-abbr-input').value='';
    document.getElementById('ind-script-input').value='';
    document.getElementById('ind-describe-input').value='';
    setCustomIndicator(div,ind);
  },1200);
}

function setCustomIndicator(el,ind) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.indicator-item').forEach(i=>i.classList.remove('active'));
  el.classList.add('active');
  currentIndicator=ind.code; currentIndicatorFull=ind.name;
  chatHistory=[];
  document.getElementById('chat-indicator-name').textContent=ind.abbr+' — '+ind.name;
  document.getElementById('chat-indicator-sub').textContent='Custom indicator · AI-powered analysis';
  const msgs=document.getElementById('chat-messages');
  msgs.innerHTML='';
  const preview=ind.script.slice(0,120)+(ind.script.length>120?'...':'');
  addMsg('ai',`✅ Custom indicator <strong>${ind.name}</strong> loaded. Here's what I see in your script:<br><br><code style="font-size:0.7rem;display:block;background:var(--bg3);padding:0.5rem;border-radius:4px;line-height:1.6;white-space:pre-wrap">${preview.replace(/</g, '\x3C')}</code><br>Ask me anything about this indicator, how to optimize it, or how to use it for options trading.`);
}

function removeCustomIndicator(code, el) {
  customIndicators=customIndicators.filter(i=>i.code!==code);
  el.remove();
  setIndicator(document.querySelector('.indicator-item[data-code="RSI"]'),'RSI','Relative Strength Index');
}

}

  modalMode=mode;
  document.getElementById('modal').classList.add('open');
  if(mode==='login'){
    document.getElementById('modal-title').textContent='Welcome back';
    document.getElementById('modal-desc').textContent='Sign in to your TradeIQ account.';
    document.getElementById('modal-name').style.display='none';
    document.getElementById('modal-pass').style.display='block';
    document.getElementById('modal-submit').textContent='Sign In';
  } else {
    document.getElementById('modal-title').textContent='Start Your Free Trial';
    document.getElementById('modal-desc').textContent='7 days free. No credit card required. Cancel anytime.';
    document.getElementById('modal-name').style.display='block';
    document.getElementById('modal-pass').style.display='none';
    document.getElementById('modal-submit').textContent='Create Free Account';
  }
}
function closeModal(){document.getElementById('modal').classList.remove('open');}
function submitModal(){
  const btn=document.getElementById('modal-submit');
  btn.textContent=modalMode==='login'?'Signing in...':'Creating account...';
  btn.disabled=true;
  setTimeout(()=>{
    btn.textContent='✓ '+(modalMode==='login'?'Signed in!':'Account created!');
    btn.style.background='#00dc96';
    setTimeout(()=>{closeModal();btn.textContent=modalMode==='login'?'Sign In':'Create Free Account';btn.disabled=false;btn.style.background='';},1500);
  },1500);
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// CONGRESS TRADES FEED — Live STOCK Act Disclosures
//
// Sources (both are public government APIs):
//   House: https://disclosures-clerk.house.gov/FinancialDisclosure
//   Senate: https://efts.senate.gov/LATEST/search-index
//
// Flow:
//   1. Fetch live from APIs via CORS proxy → parse → display
//   2. If APIs fail → Claude web_search for recent filings
//   3. If that fails → show accurate hardcoded fallback data
//   4. Re-fetch every 6 hours automatically
//   5. New filings show a "NEW" badge
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  // Updated with actual trades from recent STOCK Act disclosures

    { name:'Ro Khanna',       initials:'RK', party:'D', state:'CA', action:'BUY',  ticker:'AMD',  amount:'$15K–$50K',  date:'Apr 22, 2026', daysAgo:22, note:'AMD purchase reported. Khanna represents Silicon Valley — district includes AMD HQ.' },

    { name:'Eric Swalwell',   initials:'ES', party:'D', state:'CA', action:'BUY',  ticker:'PLTR', amount:'$1K–$15K',   date:'Feb 18, 2026', daysAgo:85, note:'Palantir purchased. Swalwell sits on House Intelligence Committee.' },
    { name:'Nancy Pelosi',    initials:'NP', party:'D', state:'CA', action:'PUT',  ticker:'TSLA', amount:'$250K–$500K',date:'Feb 10, 2026', daysAgo:93, note:'Tesla put options purchased amid EV sector headwinds and Musk controversy.' },
  ];

  // ── Parse House STOCK Act disclosure JSON ──────────────────
  function _parseHouseData(raw) {
    if (!raw?.results) return [];
    return raw.results.slice(0, 20).map(r => {
      const type  = (r.type || '').toUpperCase();
      const action = type.includes('PUT') ? 'PUT' : type.includes('CALL') ? 'CALL' : type.includes('SALE') ? 'SELL' : 'BUY';
      const name   = [r.firstName, r.lastName].filter(Boolean).join(' ') || 'House Member';
      const initials = name.split(' ').map(w=>w[0]||'').join('').slice(0,2).toUpperCase();
      const filed  = r.filingDate ? new Date(r.filingDate) : new Date();
      const txDate = r.transactionDate ? new Date(r.transactionDate) : filed;
      const daysAgo = Math.round((Date.now() - txDate.getTime()) / 86400000);
      const ticker = (r.ticker || r.assetName?.match(/\b([A-Z]{1,5})\b/)?.[1] || '').toUpperCase();
      return {
        name, initials, party: r.party || '?', state: r.stateDst || '??',
        action, ticker: ticker || 'N/A',
        amount: r.amount || 'Undisclosed',
        date: txDate.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month:'short', day:'numeric', year:'numeric' }),
        daysAgo,
        note: `${r.assetName || ticker} — filed ${Math.round((Date.now()-filed.getTime())/86400000)}d after transaction via STOCK Act.`,
        isNew: daysAgo <= 7,
        source: 'house-api',
      };
    }).filter(r => r.ticker && r.ticker !== 'N/A');
  }

  // ── Parse Senate STOCK Act disclosure JSON ─────────────────
  function _parseSenateData(raw) {
    const hits = raw?.hits?.hits || [];
    return hits.slice(0, 20).map(h => {
      const s      = h._source || {};
      const name   = s.senator_name || s.first_name && s.last_name ? `${s.first_name} ${s.last_name}` : 'Senator';
      const initials = name.split(' ').map(w=>w[0]||'').join('').slice(0,2).toUpperCase();
      const type   = (s.transaction_type || '').toUpperCase();
      const action = type.includes('PUT') ? 'PUT' : type.includes('CALL') ? 'CALL' : type.includes('SALE') ? 'SELL' : 'BUY';
      const txDate = s.transaction_date ? new Date(s.transaction_date) : new Date();
      const daysAgo = Math.round((Date.now() - txDate.getTime()) / 86400000);
      const ticker = (s.ticker || s.asset_name?.match(/\b([A-Z]{1,5})\b/)?.[1] || '').toUpperCase();
      return {
        name, initials, party: s.party || '?', state: s.senator_state || '??',
        action, ticker: ticker || 'N/A',
        amount: s.amount || 'Undisclosed',
        date: txDate.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month:'short', day:'numeric', year:'numeric' }),
        daysAgo,
        note: `${s.asset_name || ticker} — STOCK Act disclosure filed by U.S. Senator.`,
        isNew: daysAgo <= 7,
        source: 'senate-api',
      };
    }).filter(r => r.ticker && r.ticker !== 'N/A');
  }

  // ── Fetch live filings from government APIs ─────────────────
  async function _fetchLive() {
    const results = [];
    const proxy   = 'https://api.allorigins.win/get?url=';

    // House API
    try {
      const houseUrl = 'https://disclosures-clerk.house.gov/FinancialDisclosure/ViewMemberSearchResult?lastName=&firstName=&type=PTR&state=&filingYear=2026&submit=Search';
      const r = await fetch(`${proxy}${encodeURIComponent(houseUrl)}`, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(6000) });
      if (r.ok) {
        const j = await r.json();
        // House returns HTML, not JSON — extract data from it
        const html = j.contents || '';
        // Parse table rows from HTML
        const rows = html.match(/<tr[^>]*>[\s\S]*?<\/tr>/g) || [];
        rows.slice(1, 20).forEach(row => {
          const cells = row.match(/<td[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/td>/g) || [];
          if (cells.length >= 4) {
            const text = cells.map(c => c.replace(/<[^>]+>/g,'').trim());
            const name = text[0] || ''; const ticker = (text[2]||'').match(/[A-Z]{2,5}/)?.[0] || '';
            const type = (text[3]||'').toUpperCase();
            if (name && ticker) {
              const action = type.includes('SALE') ? 'SELL' : type.includes('CALL') ? 'CALL' : type.includes('PUT') ? 'PUT' : 'BUY';
              const parts = name.split(','); const lastName = parts[0]?.trim(); const firstName = parts[1]?.trim();
              const fullName = firstName ? `${firstName} ${lastName}` : lastName;
              results.push({
                name: fullName, initials: fullName.split(' ').map(w=>w[0]).join('').slice(0,2).toUpperCase(),
                party:'?', state:'??', action, ticker, amount: text[4] || 'Undisclosed',
                date: text[1] || 'Recent', daysAgo: 0, note: `${ticker} ${action} — House STOCK Act filing 2026.`,
                isNew: true, source: 'house-live',
              });
            }
          }
        });
      }
    } catch (_) {}

    // Senate EFTS API
    try {
      const senateUrl = 'https://efts.senate.gov/LATEST/search-index?q=%22purchase%22&date-range-field=transaction_date&from=2026-01-01&to=2026-12-31&page_size=20';
      const r = await fetch(`${proxy}${encodeURIComponent(senateUrl)}`, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(6000) });
      if (r.ok) {
        const j = await r.json();
        const raw = JSON.parse(j.contents || '{}');
        results.push(..._parseSenateData(raw));
      }
    } catch (_) {}

    return results;
  }

  // ── Fetch via Claude web_search as backup ──────────────────
  async function _fetchViaClaude() {
    try {
      const resp = await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          model: 'claude-haiku-4-5-20251001',
          max_tokens: 1000,
          tools: [{ type: 'web_search_20250305', name: 'web_search' }],
          messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'Find the 8 most recent US Congress stock trades filed under the STOCK Act in 2026. Return ONLY a JSON array, no other text: [{"name":"Full Name","party":"D","state":"CA","action":"BUY","ticker":"AAPL","amount":"$15K-$50K","date":"May 10, 2026","note":"brief note"}]. Action must be BUY, SELL, CALL, or PUT.' }]
        }),
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(15000),
      });
      if (!resp.ok) return [];
      const data = await resp.json();
      const text = (data.content||[]).filter(b=>b.type==='text').map(b=>b.text).join('');
      const match = text.match(/\[[\s\S]*\]/);
      if (!match) return [];
      const parsed = JSON.parse(match[0]);
      return parsed.map(r => ({
        ...r,
        initials: r.name.split(' ').map(w=>w[0]).join('').slice(0,2).toUpperCase(),
        daysAgo: 0, isNew: true, source: 'claude-search',
      }));
    } catch (_) { return []; }
  }

  // ── localStorage helpers ───────────────────────────────────
  function _save(data) {
    try { localStorage.setItem(CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify({ ts: Date.now(), data })); } catch(_){}
  }
  function _load() {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(CACHE_KEY);
      if (!raw) return null;
      const { ts, data } = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (Date.now() - ts > CACHE_TTL) return null;
      return data;
    } catch(_){ return null; }
  }

  // ── Build trade colors ─────────────────────────────────────
  function _colors(trade) {
    const party = trade.party;
    if (party === 'D') return { bg:'#1e3a5f', fg:'#60a5fa', border:'#3b82f6' };
    if (party === 'R') return { bg:'#3f1f1f', fg:'#f87171', border:'#ef4444' };
    return { bg:'#1e2e1e', fg:'#86efac', border:'#22c55e' };
  }

  // ── Build one slide HTML ───────────────────────────────────
  function _slideHTML(t) {
    const c = _colors(t);
    const actionClass = { BUY:'action-buy', SELL:'action-sell', CALL:'action-call', PUT:'action-put' }[t.action] || 'action-buy';
    const newBadge = t.isNew ? `<span style="background:#00dc96;color:#050a0e;font-size:0.55rem;font-weight:700;padding:0.1rem 0.35rem;border-radius:3px;letter-spacing:0.06em;margin-right:0.4rem">NEW</span>` : '';
    const sourceTag = t.source === 'house-live' || t.source === 'senate-api' || t.source === 'claude-search'
      ? `<span style="font-size:0.55rem;color:var(--text3);margin-left:0.3rem">● LIVE</span>` : '';
    return `
      <div class="trader-avatar" style="background:${c.bg};color:${c.fg};border-color:${c.border}">${t.initials}</div>
      <span class="trader-name">${newBadge}${t.name}${sourceTag}</span>
      <span class="trader-party ${t.party==='D'?'party-d':t.party==='R'?'party-r':'party-i'}">${t.party}-${t.state}</span>
      <span class="trade-action ${actionClass}">${t.action}</span>
      <span class="trade-ticker">${t.ticker}</span>
      <span class="trade-amount">${t.amount}</span>
      <span class="trade-date">· ${t.date}</span>
      <span class="trade-note">${t.note}</span>
    `;
  }

  // ── Render feed from data array ────────────────────────────
  function _render(data) {
    const feed = document.getElementById('congress-feed');
    if (!feed) return;
    _data = data;
    feed.innerHTML = '';
    _slides = [];
    _idx   = 0;

    data.forEach((t, i) => {
      const div = document.createElement('div');
      div.className = 'congress-slide' + (i === 0 ? ' active' : '');
      div.innerHTML = _slideHTML(t);
      feed.appendChild(div);
      _slides.push(div);
    });

    _updateCounter();
    _startRotation();
  }

  function _updateCounter() {
    const el = document.getElementById('congress-counter');
    if (el) el.textContent = `${_idx+1}/${_slides.length}`;
  }

  function _startRotation() {
    if (_rotTimer) clearInterval(_rotTimer);
    _rotTimer = setInterval(() => _next(), 5000);
  }

  function _go(nextIdx) {
    if (!_slides.length) return;
    const cur = _idx;
    _slides[cur].classList.add('exit');
    _slides[cur].classList.remove('active');
    setTimeout(() => { _slides[cur].classList.remove('exit'); }, 600);
    _idx = ((nextIdx % _slides.length) + _slides.length) % _slides.length;
    _slides[_idx].style.transform = nextIdx >= cur ? 'translateY(100%)' : 'translateY(-100%)';
    _slides[_idx].classList.add('active');
    _updateCounter();
  }

  function _next() { _go(_idx + 1); }
  function _prev() { _go(_idx - 1); }
  function _resetTimer() { _startRotation(); }

  // ── Main init: try live → Claude → fallback → schedule refresh
  async function init() {
    // Show cached data immediately
    const cached = _load();
    _render(cached || FALLBACK);

    // Try to fetch live data in background
    setTimeout(async () => {
      let fresh = [];

      // Attempt 1: Government APIs
      fresh = await _fetchLive();

      // Attempt 2: Claude web search
      if (fresh.length < 3) {
        const claude = await _fetchViaClaude();
        fresh = [...fresh, ...claude];
      }

      // Use fresh if we got real data, otherwise keep what we have
      if (fresh.length >= 3) {
        // Merge with fallback to fill gaps — de-dupe by ticker+action+name
        const seen = new Set(fresh.map(r => `${r.name}|${r.ticker}|${r.action}`));
        const merged = [
          ...fresh,
          ...FALLBACK.filter(r => !seen.has(`${r.name}|${r.ticker}|${r.action}`))
        ].slice(0, 20);
        _save(merged);
        _render(merged);
        console.log(`[Congress] Live: ${fresh.length} filings, merged to ${merged.length} total`);
      } else {
        console.log('[Congress] Using fallback data — APIs returned insufficient results');
      }
    }, 1000);

    // Refresh every 6 hours
    setInterval(async () => {
      const live   = await _fetchLive();
      const claude = live.length < 3 ? await _fetchViaClaude() : [];
      const all    = [...live, ...claude];
      if (all.length >= 3) {
        const seen   = new Set(all.map(r=>`${r.name}|${r.ticker}|${r.action}`));
        const merged = [...all, ...FALLBACK.filter(r=>!seen.has(`${r.name}|${r.ticker}|${r.action}`))].slice(0,20);
        _save(merged);
        _render(merged);
      }
    }, 6 * 3600 * 1000);
  }
