  // Public API — called by HTML buttons and global buildCongressFeed
  return { init, next: _next, prev: _prev, resetTimer: _resetTimer };
})();

// Global bridge functions (called by HTML buttons)
let slides = [], congressIdx = 0, congressTimer = null;

function buildCongressFeed() { CongressFeed.init(); }
function congressNext()      { CongressFeed.next(); CongressFeed.resetTimer(); }
function congressPrev()      { CongressFeed.prev(); CongressFeed.resetTimer(); }
function goToSlide(i)        { /* legacy compat */ }
function updateCounter()     { /* handled internally */ }
function startCongressRotation() { /* handled internally */ }
buildCongressFeed();

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// CONGRESS LIVE TRADE TEST — simulates a new STOCK Act filing
// popping in just like a real-time disclosure would
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

function injectTestTrade(trade) {
  // ── 1. Inject into the feed as the FIRST slide ──────────────
  const feed = document.getElementById('congress-feed');
  if (!feed) return;

  const div = document.createElement('div');
  div.className = 'congress-slide';
  div.style.transform = 'translateY(-100%)';

  const c      = trade.party === 'D' ? { bg:'#1e3a5f', fg:'#60a5fa', border:'#3b82f6' }
               : trade.party === 'R' ? { bg:'#3f1f1f', fg:'#f87171', border:'#ef4444' }
               : { bg:'#1e2e1e', fg:'#86efac', border:'#22c55e' };
  const aClass = { BUY:'action-buy', SELL:'action-sell', CALL:'action-call', PUT:'action-put' }[trade.action] || 'action-buy';

  div.innerHTML = `
    <div class="trader-avatar" style="background:${c.bg};color:${c.fg};border-color:${c.border}">${trade.initials}</div>
    <span class="trader-name">
      <span style="background:#00dc96;color:#050a0e;font-size:0.55rem;font-weight:700;padding:0.1rem 0.4rem;border-radius:3px;letter-spacing:0.06em;margin-right:0.4rem;animation:pulse 1s infinite">🔴 LIVE</span>
      ${trade.name}
      <span style="font-size:0.55rem;color:var(--green);margin-left:0.3rem">● JUST FILED</span>
    </span>
    <span class="trader-party ${trade.party==='D'?'party-d':trade.party==='R'?'party-r':'party-i'}">${trade.party}-${trade.state}</span>
    <span class="trade-action ${aClass}">${trade.action}</span>
    <span class="trade-ticker">${trade.ticker}</span>
    <span class="trade-amount">${trade.amount}</span>
    <span class="trade-date">· Filed ${trade.date}</span>
    <span class="trade-note">${trade.note}</span>
  `;

  // Insert as the first slide
  feed.insertBefore(div, feed.firstChild);

  // Make it the active slide with animation
  const existing = feed.querySelectorAll('.congress-slide.active');
  existing.forEach(el => {
    el.classList.add('exit');
    el.classList.remove('active');
    setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove('exit'), 600);
  });

  requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      div.classList.add('active');
      div.style.transform = '';
    });
  });

  // Update counter
  const allSlides = feed.querySelectorAll('.congress-slide');
  const counter   = document.getElementById('congress-counter');
  if (counter) counter.textContent = `1/${allSlides.length}`;

  // ── 2. Show toast notification ──────────────────────────────
  showTradeToast(trade, c);

  // ── 3. Flash the congress bar ──────────────────────────────
  const bar = document.getElementById('congress-bar');
  if (bar) {
    bar.style.transition = 'box-shadow 0.3s, border-color 0.3s';
    bar.style.boxShadow  = '0 0 0 2px #00dc96, 0 0 20px rgba(0,220,150,0.4)';
    setTimeout(() => { bar.style.boxShadow = ''; }, 2500);
  }
}

function showTradeToast(trade, colors) {
  // Remove any existing toast
  const old = document.getElementById('trade-toast');
  if (old) old.remove();

  const aColors = { BUY:'#00dc96', SELL:'#ff4757', CALL:'#00dc96', PUT:'#ff4757' };
  const aColor  = aColors[trade.action] || '#00dc96';
  const live  = typeof PriceEngine !== 'undefined' ? PriceEngine.get(trade.ticker) : null;
  const priceStr = live ? ` · $${live.price.toFixed(2)}` : '';

  const toast = document.createElement('div');
  toast.id = 'trade-toast';
  toast.style.cssText = `
    position:fixed; bottom:1.5rem; right:1.5rem; z-index:9999;
    background:var(--bg2,#0a1218); border:1px solid #00dc96;
    border-radius:10px; padding:1rem 1.25rem; min-width:300px; max-width:380px;
    box-shadow:0 8px 40px rgba(0,220,150,0.25), 0 2px 12px rgba(0,0,0,0.6);
    font-family:'JetBrains Mono',monospace;
    transform:translateX(120%); transition:transform 0.4s cubic-bezier(0.34,1.56,0.64,1);
    cursor:pointer;
  `;

  toast.innerHTML = `
    <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-start;margin-bottom:0.6rem">
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:0.5rem">
        <div style="width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:#00dc96;animation:pulse 1s infinite;flex-shrink:0"></div>
        <span style="font-size:0.65rem;color:#00dc96;letter-spacing:0.1em;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:700">🏛 New STOCK Act Filing</span>
      </div>
      <button onclick="document.getElementById('trade-toast').remove()" style="background:none;border:none;color:#4a6358;cursor:pointer;font-size:1rem;padding:0;line-height:1;margin-left:0.5rem">×</button>
    </div>
    <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:0.75rem;margin-bottom:0.5rem">
      <div style="width:36px;height:36px;border-radius:50%;background:${colors.bg};color:${colors.fg};border:2px solid ${colors.border};display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-weight:700;font-size:0.8rem;flex-shrink:0">${trade.initials}</div>
      <div>
        <div style="font-weight:700;font-size:0.85rem;color:#e8f0e9">${trade.name}</div>
        <div style="font-size:0.65rem;color:#4a6358">${trade.party === 'D' ? '🔵' : trade.party === 'R' ? '🔴' : '⚪'} ${trade.party}-${trade.state} · ${trade.date}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:0.5rem;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:0.5rem">
      <span style="background:${aColor}22;color:${aColor};border:1px solid ${aColor}44;border-radius:4px;padding:0.15rem 0.5rem;font-size:0.72rem;font-weight:700">${trade.action}</span>
      <span style="color:#e8f0e9;font-weight:700;font-size:0.9rem">${trade.ticker}${priceStr}</span>
      <span style="color:#8a9e94;font-size:0.72rem">${trade.amount}</span>
    </div>
    <div style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#8a9e94;line-height:1.5;border-top:1px solid rgba(0,220,150,0.12);padding-top:0.5rem">${trade.note}</div>
    <div style="margin-top:0.6rem;display:flex;gap:0.5rem">
      <button onclick="document.getElementById('simulator').scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth'});document.getElementById('sim-ticker-search').value='${trade.ticker}';loadStockPrice('${trade.ticker}');document.getElementById('trade-toast').remove()" style="flex:1;background:rgba(0,220,150,0.15);border:1px solid rgba(0,220,150,0.3);color:#00dc96;padding:0.35rem;border-radius:5px;font-family:inherit;font-size:0.68rem;cursor:pointer;transition:all 0.15s" onmouseover="this.style.background='rgba(0,220,150,0.25)'" onmouseout="this.style.background='rgba(0,220,150,0.15)'">
        Simulate ${trade.ticker} →
      </button>
      <button onclick="document.getElementById('trade-toast').remove()" style="background:transparent;border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.08);color:#4a6358;padding:0.35rem 0.75rem;border-radius:5px;font-family:inherit;font-size:0.68rem;cursor:pointer">
        Dismiss
      </button>
    </div>
  `;

  toast.onclick = (e) => {
    if (e.target === toast) {
      document.getElementById('congress-bar')?.scrollIntoView({ behavior:'smooth' });
    }
  };

  document.body.appendChild(toast);

  // Slide in
  requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    requestAnimationFrame(() => { toast.style.transform = 'translateX(0)'; });
  });

  // Auto-dismiss after 12s
  const dismissTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    if (document.getElementById('trade-toast')) {
      toast.style.transform = 'translateX(120%)';
      setTimeout(() => toast.remove(), 400);
    }
  }, 12000);

  // Cancel auto-dismiss on hover
  toast.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => clearTimeout(dismissTimer));
}

// ── Trigger the test trade 3 seconds after page loads ──────────
setTimeout(() => {
  injectTestTrade({
    name:     'Nancy Pelosi',
    initials: 'NP',
    party:    'D',
    state:    'CA',
    action:   'CALL',
    ticker:   'NVDA',
    amount:   '$1M–$5M',
    date:     'Just now · May 14, 2026',
    note:     'STOCK Act disclosure filed TODAY: Call options on NVIDIA. Transaction date May 9, 2026. Filed within 5-day expedited window. Pelosi husband Paul Pelosi reported via joint filing. Committee: House Democratic Leadership.',
  });
}, 3000);

// === SMOOTH NAV ===
function smoothNav(e, id) {
  e.preventDefault();
  const el = document.getElementById(id);
  if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  document.getElementById('mobile-nav').classList.remove('open');
}

function scrollTo(id) {
  const el = document.getElementById(id);
  if (el) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
}

// === MOBILE NAV ===
function toggleMobileNav() {
  const nav = document.getElementById('mobile-nav');
  if (nav) nav.classList.toggle('open');
}

// === FAQ ACCORDION ===
function toggleFaq(el) {
  const isOpen = el.classList.contains('open');
  document.querySelectorAll('.faq-item.open').forEach(i => i.classList.remove('open'));
  if (!isOpen) el.classList.add('open');
}

// === COOKIE CONSENT ===
function acceptCookie() {
  document.getElementById('cookie-bar').style.transform = 'translateY(100%)';
  localStorage.setItem('tradeiq_cookie', 'accepted');
}
function dismissCookie() {
  document.getElementById('cookie-bar').style.transform = 'translateY(100%)';
}

// Show cookie bar after 2s if not already accepted
setTimeout(() => {
  if (!localStorage.getItem('tradeiq_cookie')) {
    const bar = document.getElementById('cookie-bar');
    if (bar) bar.classList.add('show');
  }
}, 2000);

// === WAITLIST ===
function joinWaitlist() {
  const email = document.getElementById('waitlist-email').value.trim();
  if (!email || !email.includes('@')) { alert('Please enter a valid email address.'); return; }
  const btn = document.querySelector('.waitlist-btn');
  const orig = btn.textContent;
  btn.textContent = '✓ You\'re on the list!';
  btn.style.background = '#00b87a';
  document.getElementById('waitlist-email').value = '';
  const countEl = document.getElementById('waitlist-count');
  if (countEl) countEl.textContent = '🔥 2,848 traders already on the waitlist';
  setTimeout(() => { btn.textContent = orig; btn.style.background = ''; }, 3000);
}

// === NEWSLETTER ===
function subscribeNewsletter() {
  const email = document.getElementById('newsletter-email').value.trim();
  if (!email || !email.includes('@')) { alert('Please enter a valid email address.'); return; }
  const btn = document.querySelector('.newsletter-btn');
  btn.textContent = '✓ Subscribed!';
  btn.style.background = '#00b87a';
  document.getElementById('newsletter-email').value = '';
  document.getElementById('newsletter-msg').textContent = 'Welcome! Check your inbox for the first edition.';
  setTimeout(() => { btn.textContent = 'Subscribe Free →'; btn.style.background = ''; }, 3000);
}

// === MARKET STATUS (nav indicator) ===
function updateMarketStatus() {
  const state = (typeof PriceEngine !== 'undefined' && PriceEngine.getMarketState)
    ? PriceEngine.getMarketState()
    : 'closed';
  const statusEl = document.getElementById('market-status-nav');
  const textEl   = document.getElementById('market-status-text');
  if (!statusEl || !textEl) return;

  const now    = new Date();
  const etTime = now.toLocaleTimeString('en-US', { timeZone:'America/New_York', hour:'numeric', minute:'2-digit', hour12:true });

  const configs = {
    open:       { cls:'market-open',   text:'Market Open',               dot:'#00dc96' },
    premarket:  { cls:'market-pre',    text:`Pre-Market · ${etTime} ET`, dot:'#ffa502' },
    afterhours: { cls:'market-after',  text:`After-Hours · ${etTime} ET`,dot:'#60a5fa' },
    closed:     { cls:'market-closed', text:'Market Closed',              dot:'#ff4757' },
  };
  const cfg = configs[state] || configs.closed;
  statusEl.className = 'market-status ' + cfg.cls;
  textEl.textContent = cfg.text;
  const dot = statusEl.querySelector('.market-dot');
  if (dot) { dot.style.background = cfg.dot; dot.style.boxShadow = `0 0 6px ${cfg.dot}`; }

  // Re-render selected stock premarket line on state change
  if (typeof selectedStock !== 'undefined' && selectedStock?.sym && typeof PriceEngine !== 'undefined') {
    const entry = PriceEngine.get(selectedStock.sym);
    if (entry) updateStockDisplay(selectedStock.sym, entry);
  }
}
updateMarketStatus();
setInterval(updateMarketStatus, 30000);

// === SCROLL ANIMATIONS ===
const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
  entries.forEach(e => {
    if (e.isIntersecting) {
      e.target.style.opacity = '1';
      e.target.style.transform = 'translateY(0)';
    }
  });
}, { threshold: 0.1 });

document.querySelectorAll('.feature-card, .price-card, .testimonial, .stat-item').forEach(el => {
  el.style.opacity = '0';
  el.style.transform = 'translateY(24px)';
  el.style.transition = 'opacity 0.6s ease, transform 0.6s ease';
  observer.observe(el);
});

// === MOBILE NAV BUTTON VISIBILITY ===
const mobileBtn = document.getElementById('mob-btn');
if (mobileBtn) {
  mobileBtn.style.display = window.innerWidth <= 900 ? 'flex' : 'none';
  window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
    mobileBtn.style.display = window.innerWidth <= 900 ? 'flex' : 'none';
  });
}

// === CLOSE DROPDOWN ON OUTSIDE CLICK ===
document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
  const dd     = document.getElementById('stock-dropdown');
  const search = document.getElementById('sim-ticker-search');
  const clear  = document.getElementById('search-clear-btn');
  if (dd && !dd.contains(e.target) && e.target !== search && e.target !== clear) {
    closeDropdown();
  }
});

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// TRADEIQ CHART ENGINE
// Canvas-based candlestick/line charts with volume, MA, BB, RSI
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

const ChartEngine = (() => {
  let _sym      = 'AAPL';
  let _tf       = '1D';
  let _type     = 'candle'; // 'candle' | 'line'
  let _inds     = { MA: true, BB: false, VOL: true, RSI: false, MACD: false };
  let _data     = {};       // sym → { tf → candles[] }
  let _hovIdx   = -1;

  const CHART_STOCKS = ['AAPL','MSFT','NVDA','TSLA','SPY','QQQ','META','AMZN','GOOGL','AMD'];

  // ── Colour palette ──────────────────────────────────────────
  const C = {
    bg:       '#0d1921', grid: 'rgba(0,220,150,0.06)',
    up:       '#00dc96', dn:  '#ff4757',
    upFill:   'rgba(0,220,150,0.15)', dnFill: 'rgba(255,71,87,0.15)',
    ma20:     '#60a5fa', ma50: '#f59e0b', ma200: '#a78bfa',
    bb:       'rgba(96,165,250,0.4)',
    vol:      'rgba(100,116,139,0.5)', volUp: 'rgba(0,220,150,0.4)', volDn: 'rgba(255,71,87,0.4)',
    rsi:      '#e879f9', rsiOB: 'rgba(255,71,87,0.15)', rsiOS: 'rgba(0,220,150,0.15)',
    macdLine: '#38bdf8', macdSig: '#fb923c', macdUp: 'rgba(0,220,150,0.7)', macdDn: 'rgba(255,71,87,0.7)',
    text:     '#4a6358', crosshair: 'rgba(0,220,150,0.5)',
  };

  // ── Generate realistic OHLCV candles ─────────────────────────
  function generateCandles(sym, tf) {
    const live = PriceEngine.get(sym);
    const base = live?.price || 200;

    const counts = { '1D':78, '1W':75, '1M':66, '3M':65, '1Y':252 };
    const n = counts[tf] || 78;

    // volatility calibrated per symbol
    const volMap = { TSLA:0.032, NVDA:0.028, COIN:0.045, AMD:0.026, PLTR:0.038, MSTR:0.055 };
    const dayVol = volMap[sym] || 0.016;

    const candles = [];
    let price = base * (0.72 + Math.random() * 0.04); // start below current
    let trend = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      // Gentle mean-reversion toward base price
      trend = trend * 0.92 + (base - price) / base * 0.08;
      const move = trend + (Math.random() - 0.49) * dayVol;
      const open  = price;
      const close = price * (1 + move);
      const hi    = Math.max(open, close) * (1 + Math.random() * dayVol * 0.4);
      const lo    = Math.min(open, close) * (1 - Math.random() * dayVol * 0.4);
      const vol   = Math.floor((0.4 + Math.random() * 1.2) * 50000000);
      candles.push({ open, high: hi, low: lo, close, vol, i });
      price = close;
    }

    // Ensure last candle reflects live price
    if (live?.price) {
      const last = candles[candles.length - 1];
      const prev = candles[candles.length - 2]?.close || last.open;
      last.close = live.price;
      last.open  = prev;
      last.high  = Math.max(prev, live.price) * (1 + Math.random() * 0.004);
      last.low   = Math.min(prev, live.price) * (1 - Math.random() * 0.004);
    }
    return candles;
  }

  // ── Moving averages ─────────────────────────────────────────
  function sma(data, period) {
    return data.map((_, i) => {
      if (i < period - 1) return null;
      const slice = data.slice(i - period + 1, i + 1);
      return slice.reduce((s, c) => s + c.close, 0) / period;
    });
  }

  function ema(data, period) {
    const k = 2 / (period + 1);
    const result = Array(data.length).fill(null);
    let started = false, prev = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (!started && i >= period - 1) {
        prev = data.slice(0, period).reduce((s, c) => s + c.close, 0) / period;
        result[i] = prev; started = true;
      } else if (started) {
        prev = data[i].close * k + prev * (1 - k);
        result[i] = prev;
      }
    }
    return result;
  }

  function macd(data) {
    const fast = ema(data, 12), slow = ema(data, 26);
    const line  = data.map((_, i) => fast[i] !== null && slow[i] !== null ? fast[i] - slow[i] : null);
    // Signal = 9-period EMA of MACD line
    const sigData = line.map((v, i) => ({ close: v || 0 }));
    const sig = ema(sigData, 9);
    const hist = line.map((v, i) => v !== null && sig[i] !== null ? v - sig[i] : null);
    return { line, signal: sig, histogram: hist };
  }

  function bollingerBands(data, period = 20, mult = 2) {
    const mid = sma(data, period);
    return data.map((_, i) => {
      if (mid[i] === null) return null;
      const slice = data.slice(i - period + 1, i + 1);
      const mean  = mid[i];
      const sd    = Math.sqrt(slice.reduce((s, c) => s + (c.close - mean) ** 2, 0) / period);
      return { mid: mean, upper: mean + mult * sd, lower: mean - mult * sd };
    });
  }

  function rsi(data, period = 14) {
    const gains = [], losses = [];
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      const d = data[i].close - data[i - 1].close;
      gains.push(d > 0 ? d : 0);
      losses.push(d < 0 ? -d : 0);
    }
    const result = [null];
    let ag = gains.slice(0, period).reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0) / period;
    let al = losses.slice(0, period).reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0) / period;
    result.push(100 - 100 / (1 + ag / (al || 0.001)));
    for (let i = period; i < gains.length; i++) {
      ag = (ag * (period - 1) + gains[i]) / period;
      al = (al * (period - 1) + losses[i]) / period;
      result.push(100 - 100 / (1 + ag / (al || 0.001)));
    }
    while (result.length < data.length) result.push(result[result.length - 1]);
    return result;
  }

  // ── Draw helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────
  function setupCanvas(id, w, h, dpr) {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!el) return null;
    el.width  = w * dpr;
    el.height = h * dpr;
    el.style.width  = w + 'px';
    el.style.height = h + 'px';
    const ctx = el.getContext('2d');
    ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);
    return { el, ctx, w, h };
  }

  function drawGrid(ctx, w, h, rows, cols) {
    ctx.strokeStyle = C.grid;
    ctx.lineWidth   = 1;
    for (let i = 1; i < rows; i++) {
      const y = i * h / rows;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, y); ctx.lineTo(w, y); ctx.stroke();
    }
    for (let i = 1; i < cols; i++) {
      const x = i * w / cols;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, 0); ctx.lineTo(x, h); ctx.stroke();
    }
  }

  function priceToY(price, minP, maxP, h, pad) {
    return pad + (1 - (price - minP) / (maxP - minP)) * (h - pad * 2);
  }

  // ── Main render ─────────────────────────────────────────────
  function render() {
    const canvas = document.getElementById('main-chart');
    if (!canvas) return;

    const containerW = canvas.parentElement.clientWidth;
    const mainH      = _inds.RSI ? Math.round(containerW * 0.32) : Math.round(containerW * 0.42);
    const rsiH       = 110;
    const dpr        = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
    const PAD        = { top: 14, bot: 28, left: 8, right: 68 };

    const candles = getCandles(_sym, _tf);
    if (!candles.length) return;

    // Price range
    const minP = Math.min(...candles.map(c => c.low));
    const maxP = Math.max(...candles.map(c => c.high));
    const pad  = (maxP - minP) * 0.08;
    const lo   = minP - pad, hi = maxP + pad;

    const { ctx, w, h } = setupCanvas('main-chart', containerW, mainH, dpr) || {};
    if (!ctx) return;

    // Background
    ctx.fillStyle = C.bg;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);

    const chartW = w - PAD.left - PAD.right;
    const chartH = h - PAD.top  - PAD.bot;
    const barW   = Math.max(1, chartW / candles.length);
    const barGap = Math.max(0.5, barW * 0.15);

    // Grid
    drawGrid(ctx, w, h, 5, 6);

    // Price axis labels
    ctx.fillStyle = C.text;
    ctx.font      = `11px JetBrains Mono, monospace`;
    ctx.textAlign = 'left';
    for (let i = 0; i <= 4; i++) {
      const price = lo + (hi - lo) * (1 - i / 4);
      const y     = PAD.top + i * chartH / 4;
      ctx.fillText('$' + price.toFixed(price > 100 ? 1 : 2), w - PAD.right + 6, y + 4);
      ctx.strokeStyle = C.grid;
      ctx.lineWidth   = 0.5;
      ctx.setLineDash([3, 4]);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(PAD.left, y); ctx.lineTo(w - PAD.right, y); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.setLineDash([]);
    }

    // Volume bars (bottom 20% of chart)
    if (_inds.VOL) {
      const maxVol = Math.max(...candles.map(c => c.vol));
      const volH   = chartH * 0.18;
      const volY   = PAD.top + chartH - volH;
      candles.forEach((c, i) => {
        const x  = PAD.left + i * barW + barGap / 2;
        const bh = (c.vol / maxVol) * volH;
        ctx.fillStyle = c.close >= c.open ? C.volUp : C.volDn;
        ctx.fillRect(x, volY + volH - bh, Math.max(1, barW - barGap), bh);
      });
    }

    // Bollinger Bands
    if (_inds.BB) {
      const bb = bollingerBands(candles);
      ['upper', 'lower'].forEach(band => {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.strokeStyle = C.bb;
        ctx.lineWidth   = 1;
        bb.forEach((b, i) => {
          if (!b) return;
          const x = PAD.left + (i + 0.5) * barW;
          const y = priceToY(b[band], lo, hi, h, PAD.top);
          i === 0 ? ctx.moveTo(x, y) : ctx.lineTo(x, y);
        });
        ctx.stroke();
      });
      // Fill between bands
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(96,165,250,0.04)';
      const valid = bb.filter(Boolean);
      if (valid.length) {
        bb.forEach((b, i) => { if (!b) return; ctx.lineTo(PAD.left + (i + 0.5) * barW, priceToY(b.upper, lo, hi, h, PAD.top)); });
        for (let i = bb.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { if (!bb[i]) continue; ctx.lineTo(PAD.left + (i + 0.5) * barW, priceToY(bb[i].lower, lo, hi, h, PAD.top)); }
        ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
      }
    }

    // Moving averages
    if (_inds.MA) {
      [{ p: 20, color: C.ma20, w: 1.5 }, { p: 50, color: C.ma50, w: 1.5 }, { p: 200, color: C.ma200, w: 1 }].forEach(({ p, color, w: lw }) => {
        if (candles.length < p) return;
        const ma = sma(candles, p);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.strokeStyle = color;
        ctx.lineWidth   = lw;
        let started = false;
        ma.forEach((v, i) => {
          if (v === null) return;
          const x = PAD.left + (i + 0.5) * barW;
          const y = priceToY(v, lo, hi, h, PAD.top);
          if (!started) { ctx.moveTo(x, y); started = true; } else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
        });
        ctx.stroke();
      });
    }

    // Candles or line
    if (_type === 'candle') {
      candles.forEach((c, i) => {
        const x     = PAD.left + i * barW;
        const mid   = x + barW / 2;
        const isUp  = c.close >= c.open;
        const openY = priceToY(c.open,  lo, hi, h, PAD.top);
        const clsY  = priceToY(c.close, lo, hi, h, PAD.top);
        const hiY   = priceToY(c.high,  lo, hi, h, PAD.top);
        const loY   = priceToY(c.low,   lo, hi, h, PAD.top);
        const bodyW = Math.max(1, barW - barGap * 2);

        // Wick
        ctx.strokeStyle = isUp ? C.up : C.dn;
        ctx.lineWidth   = Math.max(1, bodyW * 0.15);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(mid, hiY); ctx.lineTo(mid, Math.min(openY, clsY));
        ctx.moveTo(mid, loY); ctx.lineTo(mid, Math.max(openY, clsY));
        ctx.stroke();

        // Body
        const bodyTop = Math.min(openY, clsY);
        const bodyH   = Math.max(1, Math.abs(openY - clsY));
        ctx.fillStyle = isUp ? C.up : C.dn;
        ctx.fillRect(x + barGap, bodyTop, bodyW, bodyH);

        // Hover highlight
        if (i === _hovIdx) {
          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,220,150,0.08)';
          ctx.fillRect(x, PAD.top, barW, chartH);
        }
      });
    } else {
      // Line chart with gradient fill — fixed rendering
      const isPositive = candles[candles.length-1].close >= candles[0].close;
      const lineColor  = isPositive ? C.up : C.dn;

      // Draw the line stroke first
      ctx.beginPath();
      candles.forEach((c, i) => {
        const x = PAD.left + (i + 0.5) * barW;
        const y = priceToY(c.close, lo, hi, h, PAD.top);
        i === 0 ? ctx.moveTo(x, y) : ctx.lineTo(x, y);
      });
      ctx.strokeStyle = lineColor;
      ctx.lineWidth   = 2;
      ctx.stroke();

      // Draw the gradient fill — separate path that closes at baseline
      const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, PAD.top, 0, PAD.top + chartH);
      grad.addColorStop(0, isPositive ? 'rgba(0,220,150,0.25)' : 'rgba(255,71,87,0.25)');
      grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
      ctx.beginPath();
      candles.forEach((c, i) => {
        const x = PAD.left + (i + 0.5) * barW;
        const y = priceToY(c.close, lo, hi, h, PAD.top);
        i === 0 ? ctx.moveTo(x, y) : ctx.lineTo(x, y);
      });
      ctx.lineTo(PAD.left + (candles.length - 0.5) * barW, PAD.top + chartH);
      ctx.lineTo(PAD.left + 0.5 * barW, PAD.top + chartH);
      ctx.closePath();
      ctx.fillStyle = grad;
      ctx.fill();
    }

    // Crosshair price line for hovered candle
    if (_hovIdx >= 0 && _hovIdx < candles.length) {
      const c = candles[_hovIdx];
      const y = priceToY(c.close, lo, hi, h, PAD.top);
      ctx.setLineDash([4, 4]);
      ctx.strokeStyle = C.crosshair;
      ctx.lineWidth   = 1;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(PAD.left, y); ctx.lineTo(w - PAD.right, y); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.setLineDash([]);
      // Price label on right axis
      ctx.fillStyle   = 'var(--green)' || '#00dc96';
      ctx.fillStyle   = '#00dc96';
      ctx.fillRect(w - PAD.right + 2, y - 9, PAD.right - 4, 18);
      ctx.fillStyle   = '#050a0e';
      ctx.font        = 'bold 11px JetBrains Mono, monospace';
      ctx.textAlign   = 'left';
      ctx.fillText('$' + c.close.toFixed(c.close > 100 ? 1 : 2), w - PAD.right + 5, y + 4);
    }

    // ── X-axis date labels ────────────────────────────────────
    const tfDays = { '1D':1, '1W':7, '1M':30, '3M':90, '1Y':365 };
    const totalDays = tfDays[_tf] || 30;
    const nowDate = new Date();
    ctx.fillStyle = C.text;
    ctx.font      = '10px JetBrains Mono, monospace';
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    const lblCount = Math.max(3, Math.min(7, Math.floor(chartW / 90)));
    for (let li = 0; li <= lblCount; li++) {
      const frac = li / lblCount;
      const x    = PAD.left + frac * chartW;
      const d    = new Date(nowDate.getTime() - (1 - frac) * totalDays * 86400000);
      const lbl  = _tf === '1D'
        ? d.toLocaleTimeString('en-US', { hour:'numeric', hour12:true })
        : _tf === '1Y'
        ? d.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month:'short', year:'2-digit' })
        : d.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month:'short', day:'numeric' });
      ctx.fillText(lbl, x, h - 5);
    }

    // ── RSI sub-chart ─────────────────────────────────────────
    const rsiEl  = document.getElementById('rsi-chart');
    const macdEl = document.getElementById('macd-chart');
    if (!rsiEl) return;

    if (_inds.RSI) {
      rsiEl.style.display = 'block';
      const rsiH2 = 100;
      const { ctx: rCtx, w: rW, h: rH } = setupCanvas('rsi-chart', containerW, rsiH2, dpr) || {};
      if (!rCtx) return;
      rCtx.fillStyle = C.bg; rCtx.fillRect(0, 0, rW, rH);

      const rsiVals = rsi(candles);
      const rPad = { top:6, bot:18, left:PAD.left, right:PAD.right };
      const rChartH = rH - rPad.top - rPad.bot;
      const rBarW   = (rW - rPad.left - rPad.right) / candles.length;

      const yOB = rPad.top + (1 - 70/100) * rChartH;
      const yOS = rPad.top + (1 - 30/100) * rChartH;
      rCtx.fillStyle = C.rsiOB;
      rCtx.fillRect(rPad.left, rPad.top, rW - rPad.left - rPad.right, yOB - rPad.top);
      rCtx.fillStyle = C.rsiOS;
      rCtx.fillRect(rPad.left, yOS, rW - rPad.left - rPad.right, rChartH - (yOS - rPad.top));

      // 50 midline
      const y50r = rPad.top + rChartH/2;
      rCtx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,0.05)'; rCtx.lineWidth=1; rCtx.setLineDash([3,3]);
      rCtx.beginPath(); rCtx.moveTo(rPad.left,y50r); rCtx.lineTo(rW-rPad.right,y50r); rCtx.stroke();
      rCtx.setLineDash([]);

      // RSI line with color gradient (red when overbought, green when oversold)
      rCtx.beginPath(); rCtx.strokeStyle = C.rsi; rCtx.lineWidth = 1.5;
      rsiVals.forEach((v, i) => {
        if (v === null) return;
        const x = rPad.left + (i + 0.5) * rBarW;
        const y = rPad.top + (1 - v/100) * rChartH;
        i === 0 ? rCtx.moveTo(x, y) : rCtx.lineTo(x, y);
      });
      rCtx.stroke();

      // Labels
      rCtx.fillStyle=C.text; rCtx.font='10px JetBrains Mono,monospace'; rCtx.textAlign='left';
      rCtx.fillText('RSI(14)', rPad.left+4, rPad.top+11);
      rCtx.fillText('70', rW-rPad.right+3, yOB+4);
      rCtx.fillText('30', rW-rPad.right+3, yOS+4);
      if (_hovIdx >= 0 && rsiVals[_hovIdx] !== null) {
        const rv = rsiVals[_hovIdx];
        rCtx.fillStyle = rv>70?'#ff4757':rv<30?'#00dc96':C.rsi;
        rCtx.textAlign='right'; rCtx.font='bold 10px JetBrains Mono,monospace';
        rCtx.fillText(rv.toFixed(1), rW-rPad.right-2, rPad.top+11);
      }
    } else { rsiEl.style.display = 'none'; }

    // ── MACD sub-chart ────────────────────────────────────────
    if (!macdEl) return;
    if (_inds.MACD) {
      macdEl.style.display = 'block';
      const macdH2 = 100;
      const { ctx: mCtx, w: mW, h: mH } = setupCanvas('macd-chart', containerW, macdH2, dpr) || {};
      if (!mCtx) return;
      mCtx.fillStyle = C.bg; mCtx.fillRect(0, 0, mW, mH);

      const md = macd(candles);
      const mPad = { top:6, bot:18, left:PAD.left, right:PAD.right };
      const mChartH = mH - mPad.top - mPad.bot;
      const mBarW   = (mW - mPad.left - mPad.right) / candles.length;

      const histVals = md.histogram.filter(v => v !== null);
      const histMax  = Math.max(...histVals.map(Math.abs), 0.01);
      const zeroY    = mPad.top + mChartH/2;

      // Zero line
      mCtx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,0.1)'; mCtx.lineWidth=1;
      mCtx.beginPath(); mCtx.moveTo(mPad.left,zeroY); mCtx.lineTo(mW-mPad.right,zeroY); mCtx.stroke();

      // Histogram bars
      md.histogram.forEach((v, i) => {
        if (v===null) return;
        const x  = mPad.left + i*mBarW + mBarW*0.1;
        const bh = Math.abs(v)/histMax*(mChartH/2)*0.88;
        mCtx.fillStyle = v>=0 ? C.macdUp : C.macdDn;
        mCtx.fillRect(x, v>=0?zeroY-bh:zeroY, Math.max(1,mBarW*0.8), bh);
      });

      // MACD line
      mCtx.beginPath(); mCtx.strokeStyle=C.macdLine; mCtx.lineWidth=1.5;
      md.line.forEach((v,i) => {
        if(v===null) return;
        const x=mPad.left+(i+0.5)*mBarW, y=zeroY-(v/histMax)*(mChartH/2)*0.88;
        i===0?mCtx.moveTo(x,y):mCtx.lineTo(x,y);
      }); mCtx.stroke();

      // Signal line
      mCtx.beginPath(); mCtx.strokeStyle=C.macdSig; mCtx.lineWidth=1.5;
      md.signal.forEach((v,i) => {
        if(v===null) return;
        const x=mPad.left+(i+0.5)*mBarW, y=zeroY-(v/histMax)*(mChartH/2)*0.88;
        i===0?mCtx.moveTo(x,y):mCtx.lineTo(x,y);
      }); mCtx.stroke();

      // Labels
      mCtx.fillStyle=C.text; mCtx.font='10px JetBrains Mono,monospace'; mCtx.textAlign='left';
      mCtx.fillText('MACD(12,26,9)', mPad.left+4, mPad.top+11);
      if (_hovIdx>=0 && md.histogram[_hovIdx]!==null) {
        const hv=md.histogram[_hovIdx];
        mCtx.fillStyle=hv>=0?'#00dc96':'#ff4757';
        mCtx.textAlign='right'; mCtx.font='bold 10px JetBrains Mono,monospace';
        mCtx.fillText((hv>=0?'+':'')+hv.toFixed(3), mW-mPad.right-2, mPad.top+11);
      }
    } else { macdEl.style.display = 'none'; }

    // Update header labels
    const live2 = PriceEngine.get(_sym);
    if (live2) {
      const prEl = document.getElementById('chart-price-label');
      const cEl  = document.getElementById('chart-chg-label');
      if (prEl) prEl.textContent = '$' + live2.price.toFixed(2);
      if (cEl) {
        const sign = live2.chgPct >= 0 ? '+' : '';
        cEl.textContent = `${sign}$${live2.chg.toFixed(2)} (${sign}${live2.chgPct.toFixed(2)}%)`;
        cEl.style.color = live2.chgPct >= 0 ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)';
      }
    }
  }

  // ── Candle data management ───────────────────────────────────
  function getCandles(sym, tf) {
    if (!_data[sym]) _data[sym] = {};
    if (!_data[sym][tf]) _data[sym][tf] = generateCandles(sym, tf);
    return _data[sym][tf];
  }

  // Invalidate cache when live price updates so last candle is refreshed
  function invalidate(sym) {
    if (_data[sym]) {
      Object.keys(_data[sym]).forEach(tf => {
        const d = _data[sym][tf];
        if (d.length) d[d.length - 1] = null; // will regenerate on next render
        _data[sym][tf] = null;
      });
      _data[sym] = {};
    }
    if (sym === _sym) render();
  }

  // ── Sparklines ───────────────────────────────────────────────
  function buildSparklines() {
    const row = document.getElementById('sparklines-row');
    if (!row) return;
    const syms = ['SPY','QQQ','AAPL','NVDA','TSLA','META'];
    row.innerHTML = syms.map(sym => {
      const live = PriceEngine.get(sym);
      const px   = live ? '$' + live.price.toFixed(2) : '—';
      const chg  = live ? `${live.chgPct>=0?'+':''}${live.chgPct.toFixed(2)}%` : '';
      const col  = live && live.chgPct >= 0 ? '#00dc96' : '#ff4757';
      const meta = stockDB[sym];
      return `<div class="sparkline-card" onclick="switchChartSym('${sym}')">
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-start;margin-bottom:0.4rem">
          <div>
            <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-weight:700;font-size:0.82rem">${sym}</div>
            <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.6rem;color:var(--text3)">${meta?.sector||''}</div>
          </div>
          <div style="text-align:right">
            <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.8rem">${px}</div>
            <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.65rem;color:${col}">${chg}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <canvas id="spark-${sym}" width="140" height="36" style="width:100%;height:36px"></canvas>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');

    // Draw each sparkline after DOM update
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      syms.forEach(sym => {
        const el = document.getElementById('spark-' + sym);
        if (!el) return;
        const candles = getCandles(sym, '1M');
        if (!candles.length) return;
        const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
        el.width  = el.offsetWidth  * dpr;
        el.height = el.offsetHeight * dpr;
        const ctx = el.getContext('2d');
        ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);
        const W = el.offsetWidth, H = el.offsetHeight;
        const min = Math.min(...candles.map(c => c.close));
        const max = Math.max(...candles.map(c => c.close));
        const live = PriceEngine.get(sym);
        const isUp = live ? live.chgPct >= 0 : candles[candles.length-1].close >= candles[0].close;
        const color = isUp ? '#00dc96' : '#ff4757';
        const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H);
        grad.addColorStop(0, isUp ? 'rgba(0,220,150,0.3)' : 'rgba(255,71,87,0.3)');
        grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
        ctx.beginPath();
        candles.forEach((c, i) => {
          const x = (i / (candles.length - 1)) * W;
          const y = H - ((c.close - min) / (max - min || 1)) * H * 0.85;
          i === 0 ? ctx.moveTo(x, y) : ctx.lineTo(x, y);
        });
        ctx.strokeStyle = color; ctx.lineWidth = 1.5; ctx.stroke();
        ctx.lineTo(W, H); ctx.lineTo(0, H); ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fillStyle = grad; ctx.fill();
      });
    });
  }

  // ── Stock picker tabs ────────────────────────────────────────
  function buildStockTabs() {
    const el = document.getElementById('chart-stock-tabs');
    if (!el) return;
    el.innerHTML = CHART_STOCKS.map(sym => {
      const live = PriceEngine.get(sym);
      const chg  = live ? `${live.chgPct>=0?'+':''}${live.chgPct.toFixed(2)}%` : '';
      const col  = live && live.chgPct >= 0 ? '#00dc96' : '#ff4757';
      return `<button class="chart-stock-tab${sym===_sym?' active':''}" onclick="switchChartSym('${sym}')">
        <span>${sym}</span>
        ${chg ? `<span style="font-size:0.58rem;color:${col};margin-left:0.3rem">${chg}</span>` : ''}
      </button>`;
    }).join('');
  }

  // ── Public interface ─────────────────────────────────────────
  function init() {
    buildStockTabs();
    buildSparklines();
    render();
    setupCrosshair();

    // Subscribe to price updates for all chart stocks
    CHART_STOCKS.forEach(sym => {
      PriceEngine.subscribe(sym, (s, e) => {
        invalidate(s);
        if (s === _sym) render();
        buildStockTabs();
        buildSparklines();
      });
    });

    // Re-render on resize
    window.addEventListener('resize', debounce(render, 150));
  }

  function setupCrosshair() {
    const canvas = document.getElementById('main-chart');
    if (!canvas) return;

    canvas.addEventListener('mousemove', e => {
      const rect    = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      const mx      = e.clientX - rect.left;
      const candles = getCandles(_sym, _tf);
      const PAD_L   = 8, PAD_R = 68;
      const chartW  = rect.width - PAD_L - PAD_R;
      const barW    = chartW / candles.length;
      _hovIdx = Math.min(candles.length - 1, Math.max(0, Math.floor((mx - PAD_L) / barW)));

      // Update tooltip
      const tt  = document.getElementById('chart-tooltip');
      const c   = candles[_hovIdx];
      if (tt && c) {
        tt.style.display = 'block';
        tt.style.left    = Math.min(mx + 12, rect.width - 160) + 'px';
        tt.style.top     = '12px';
        const isUp = c.close >= c.open;
        const col  = isUp ? '#00dc96' : '#ff4757';
        tt.innerHTML = `<div style="color:var(--text3);margin-bottom:3px">${_sym} · Bar ${_hovIdx + 1}</div>
          <div>O <span style="color:${col}">$${c.open.toFixed(2)}</span>  H <span style="color:${col}">$${c.high.toFixed(2)}</span></div>
          <div>L <span style="color:${col}">$${c.low.toFixed(2)}</span>  C <span style="color:${col}">$${c.close.toFixed(2)}</span></div>
          <div style="margin-top:3px;color:var(--text3)">Vol ${(c.vol/1e6).toFixed(1)}M</div>`;
      }
      render();
    });

    canvas.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
      _hovIdx = -1;
      const tt = document.getElementById('chart-tooltip');
      if (tt) tt.style.display = 'none';
      render();
    });

    canvas.addEventListener('click', e => {
      // Click to open in simulator
      if (_hovIdx >= 0) {
        document.getElementById('sim-ticker-search').value = _sym;
        _lastQuery = _sym;
        loadStockPrice(_sym);
        document.getElementById('simulator').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
      }
    });
  }

  function debounce(fn, ms) {
    let t; return (...a) => { clearTimeout(t); t = setTimeout(() => fn(...a), ms); };
  }

  return {
    init,
    setSym(sym)  { _sym = sym; _hovIdx = -1; render(); buildStockTabs(); },
    setTF(tf)    { _tf  = tf;  _hovIdx = -1; render(); },
    setType(t)   { _type = t;  render(); },
    toggleInd(k) { _inds[k] = !_inds[k]; render(); },
    refresh()    { render(); buildStockTabs(); buildSparklines(); },
  };
})();

// ── Chart control functions (called by HTML) ─────────────────
function switchChartSym(sym) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.chart-stock-tab').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('active', b.textContent.trim().startsWith(sym)));
  const nl = document.getElementById('chart-name-label');
  const sl = document.getElementById('chart-sym-label');
  if (sl) sl.textContent = sym;
  if (nl) nl.textContent = stockDB[sym]?.name || sym;
  ChartEngine.setSym(sym);
}

function setChartTF(btn, tf) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.chart-tf-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
  btn.classList.add('active');
  ChartEngine.setTF(tf);
}

function setChartType(btn, type) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.chart-type-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
  btn.classList.add('active');
  ChartEngine.setType(type);
}

function toggleChartIndicator(key) {
  const btn = document.getElementById('ind-' + key.toLowerCase() + '-btn');
  if (btn) btn.classList.toggle('active');
  ChartEngine.toggleInd(key);
  if (key === 'VOL') {
    const vl = document.getElementById('chart-vol-legend');
    if (vl) vl.style.display = btn?.classList.contains('active') ? '' : 'none';
  }
  if (key === 'RSI') {
    const rl = document.getElementById('chart-rsi-legend');
    if (rl) rl.style.display = btn?.classList.contains('active') ? '' : 'none';
  }
  if (key === 'MACD') {
    const ml = document.getElementById('chart-macd-legend');
    if (ml) ml.style.display = btn?.classList.contains('active') ? '' : 'none';
  }
}

// Init charts after page load
setTimeout(() => {
  if (document.getElementById('main-chart')) {
    ChartEngine.init();
    // Update nav with charts link
    const navLinks = document.querySelector('.nav-links');
    if (navLinks && !navLinks.querySelector('[href="#charts"]')) {
      const li = document.createElement('li');
      li.innerHTML = '<a href="#charts" onclick="smoothNav(event,\'charts\')">Charts</a>';
      navLinks.insertBefore(li, navLinks.children[2]);
    }
  }
}, 500);

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// PAYOFF CHART — Robinhood-style option payoff diagram
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function drawPayoffChart(params) {
  const { S, K, prem, optType, contracts, breakeven } = params;
  const canvas = document.getElementById('payoff-chart');
  if (!canvas) return;
  const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  const W   = canvas.parentElement?.clientWidth || 300;
  const H   = 160;
  canvas.width = W*dpr; canvas.height = H*dpr;
  canvas.style.width = W+'px'; canvas.style.height = H+'px';
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);

  const lo = S*0.70, hi = S*1.30;
  const PAD = { top:12, bot:28, left:8, right:52 };
  const cW = W-PAD.left-PAD.right, cH = H-PAD.top-PAD.bot;

  const pnlAt = price => {
    let intr = 0;
    if (optType==='call')   intr = Math.max(0, price-K);
    else if (optType==='put') intr = Math.max(0, K-price);
    else                     intr = Math.max(0, price-K)*0.6;
    return (intr-prem)*100*contracts;
  };

  const pts = 200;
  const pnlPoints = Array.from({length:pts+1}, (_,i) => {
    const price = lo+(hi-lo)*(i/pts);
    return { price, pl: pnlAt(price) };
  });

  const maxPL = Math.max(...pnlPoints.map(p=>p.pl));
  const minPL = Math.min(...pnlPoints.map(p=>p.pl));
  const range  = Math.max(Math.abs(maxPL), Math.abs(minPL))*1.15 || 100;
  const xToW  = p  => PAD.left + ((p-lo)/(hi-lo))*cW;
  const plToH = pl => PAD.top  + (1-(pl+range)/(range*2))*cH;
  const zeroY = plToH(0);

  ctx.fillStyle='#0d1921'; ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);

  ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,0.1)'; ctx.lineWidth=1; ctx.setLineDash([4,4]);
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(PAD.left,zeroY); ctx.lineTo(W-PAD.right,zeroY); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.setLineDash([]);

  // Profit fill
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,220,150,0.1)';
  ctx.moveTo(xToW(lo), zeroY);
  pnlPoints.forEach(p => ctx.lineTo(xToW(p.price), plToH(Math.max(0,p.pl))));
  ctx.lineTo(xToW(hi), zeroY); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();

  // Loss fill
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,71,87,0.1)';
  ctx.moveTo(xToW(lo), zeroY);
  pnlPoints.forEach(p => ctx.lineTo(xToW(p.price), plToH(Math.min(0,p.pl))));
  ctx.lineTo(xToW(hi), zeroY); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();

  // P&L line — color changes at breakeven
  let prevProfit = pnlPoints[0].pl >= 0;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.strokeStyle = prevProfit?'#00dc96':'#ff4757'; ctx.lineWidth=2.5;
  ctx.moveTo(xToW(pnlPoints[0].price), plToH(pnlPoints[0].pl));
  pnlPoints.slice(1).forEach(p => {
    const isP = p.pl >= 0;
    if (isP !== prevProfit) { ctx.stroke(); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.strokeStyle=isP?'#00dc96':'#ff4757'; ctx.moveTo(xToW(p.price),plToH(p.pl)); prevProfit=isP; }
    else ctx.lineTo(xToW(p.price), plToH(p.pl));
  }); ctx.stroke();

  // Current price line
  const sx = xToW(S);
  ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,0.35)'; ctx.lineWidth=1; ctx.setLineDash([3,3]);
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(sx,PAD.top); ctx.lineTo(sx,H-PAD.bot); ctx.stroke(); ctx.setLineDash([]);
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,0.55)'; ctx.font='9px JetBrains Mono,monospace'; ctx.textAlign='center';
  ctx.fillText('NOW', sx, PAD.top+9);

  // Breakeven line
  if (breakeven>lo && breakeven<hi) {
    const bx = xToW(breakeven);
    ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(0,220,150,0.5)'; ctx.lineWidth=1; ctx.setLineDash([2,3]);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(bx,PAD.top); ctx.lineTo(bx,H-PAD.bot); ctx.stroke(); ctx.setLineDash([]);
    ctx.fillStyle='#00dc96'; ctx.font='9px JetBrains Mono,monospace'; ctx.textAlign='center';
    ctx.fillText('B/E', bx, PAD.top+9);
  }

  // Y-axis
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(74,99,88,0.8)'; ctx.font='9px JetBrains Mono,monospace'; ctx.textAlign='left';
  [range*0.75, 0, -range*0.75].forEach(v => {
    const y = plToH(v);
    if (y>PAD.top&&y<H-PAD.bot) ctx.fillText((v>=0?'+':'')+'$'+Math.abs(Math.round(v)).toLocaleString(), W-PAD.right+3, y+3);
  });
  // X-axis
  ctx.textAlign='center';
  [lo, S, hi].forEach(p => ctx.fillText('$'+p.toFixed(0), xToW(p), H-6));

  // Hover
  canvas.onmousemove = e => {
    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const mx   = e.clientX - rect.left;
    const mPrice = lo + (hi-lo)*Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(mx-PAD.left)/cW));
    const mPL    = pnlAt(mPrice);
    const tt     = document.getElementById('payoff-tooltip');
    const hov    = document.getElementById('res-payoff-hover');
    if (tt) {
      tt.style.display='block';
      tt.style.left=Math.min(mx+10, W-140)+'px'; tt.style.top='8px';
      const col=mPL>=0?'#00dc96':'#ff4757';
      tt.innerHTML=`<span style="color:var(--text3)">If ${params.sym||'stock'} → </span><span style="color:var(--text)">$${mPrice.toFixed(2)}</span><br><span style="color:${col};font-weight:700">${mPL>=0?'+':''}$${mPL.toFixed(0)}</span>`;
    }
    if (hov) hov.textContent=`$${mPrice.toFixed(2)} → ${mPL>=0?'+':''}$${Math.round(mPL)}`;
  };
  canvas.onmouseleave = () => {
    const tt=document.getElementById('payoff-tooltip'); if(tt) tt.style.display='none';
    const hov=document.getElementById('res-payoff-hover'); if(hov) hov.textContent='';
  };
}

// Enhance SimulatorEngine results after each run
const _origRun = SimulatorEngine.run.bind(SimulatorEngine);
SimulatorEngine.run = async function() {
  await _origRun();
  const res = document.getElementById('sim-results');
  if (!res || !res.classList.contains('visible')) return;
  try { enhanceSimResults(); } catch(e) { console.warn('[Enhance]', e.message); }
};

function enhanceSimResults() {
  const sym  = document.getElementById('stock-sym')?.textContent?.trim() || 'AAPL';
  const K    = parseFloat(document.getElementById('sim-strike')?.value || '0');
  const iv   = parseFloat(document.getElementById('sim-iv')?.value || '28') / 100;
  const prem = parseFloat(document.getElementById('sim-premium')?.value || '0') || parseFloat(document.getElementById('res-theo')?.textContent?.replace('$','') || '0');
  const contracts = parseInt(document.getElementById('sim-contracts')?.value || '1', 10);
  const optType   = typeof currentType !== 'undefined' ? currentType : 'call';
  const expiryVal = document.getElementById('sim-expiry')?.value || '';

  let S = 0;
  try { const live = PriceEngine.get(sym); S = live?.price || selectedStock?.price || 200; } catch(_) { S = 200; }
  if (!S || !K) return;

  // Probability of profit from delta
  const delta = parseFloat(document.getElementById('res-delta')?.textContent || '0.5');
  const pop   = Math.round(optType==='call' ? (1-Math.abs(delta))*100 : Math.abs(delta)*100);
  const popEl = document.getElementById('res-pop');
  if (popEl) { popEl.textContent = pop+'%'; popEl.style.color = pop>60?'var(--green)':pop>40?'var(--amber)':'var(--red)'; }

  // Expected move
  const today = new Date(); today.setHours(0,0,0,0);
  const exp   = new Date((expiryVal||new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10))+'T00:00:00');
  const dte2  = Math.max(0, Math.round((exp-today)/86400000));
  const T2    = dte2/365;
  const expM  = S*iv*Math.sqrt(T2||0.01);
  const emEl  = document.getElementById('res-expmove');
  if (emEl) emEl.textContent = `±$${expM.toFixed(2)} (±${(expM/S*100).toFixed(1)}%)`;

  // IV vs HV
  const hv30 = iv*0.85;
  const ivHvEl = document.getElementById('res-iv-hv');
  if (ivHvEl) { ivHvEl.textContent = `${(iv*100).toFixed(1)}% vs ${(hv30*100).toFixed(1)}%`; ivHvEl.style.color = iv>hv30*1.1?'var(--red)':'var(--green)'; }
  const ivDEl = document.getElementById('res-iv-display');
  if (ivDEl) { ivDEl.textContent = (iv*100).toFixed(1)+'%'; ivDEl.style.color = iv>0.5?'var(--red)':iv>0.3?'var(--amber)':'var(--green)'; }

  // Badges
  const mEl = document.getElementById('res-moneyness-badge');
  if (mEl) { const itm=optType==='call'?S>K:S<K; mEl.textContent=itm?'ITM':'OTM'; mEl.style.color=mEl.style.borderColor=itm?'var(--green)':'var(--amber)'; }
  const sEl = document.getElementById('res-strategy-badge');
  if (sEl) { sEl.textContent=optType.toUpperCase(); sEl.style.color=optType==='call'?'var(--green)':optType==='put'?'var(--red)':'var(--amber)'; }

  // Breakeven
  const be = parseFloat(document.getElementById('res-be')?.textContent?.replace('$','') || '0');

  // Payoff chart
  drawPayoffChart({ S, K, prem, optType, contracts, breakeven: be, dte: dte2, iv, sym });

  // Strategy comparison
  const stratDiv  = document.getElementById('res-strategy-compare');
  const stratGrid = document.getElementById('strategy-compare-grid');
  if (stratDiv && stratGrid) {
    stratDiv.style.display = 'block';
    const bs2 = (type) => {
      if (!T2||T2<=0) return 0;
      const d1 = (Math.log(S/K)+(0.053+0.5*iv*iv)*T2)/(iv*Math.sqrt(T2));
      const d2 = d1 - iv*Math.sqrt(T2);
      const N  = x => 0.5*(1+Math.sign(x)*Math.sqrt(1-Math.exp(-2*x*x/Math.PI)));
      if (type==='call') return Math.max(0,S*N(d1)-K*Math.exp(-0.053*T2)*N(d2));
      if (type==='put')  return Math.max(0,K*Math.exp(-0.053*T2)*(1-N(d2))-S*(1-N(d1)));
      return 0;
    };
    const strats = [
      {name:'Long Call', type:'call', col:'var(--green)'},
      {name:'Long Put',  type:'put',  col:'var(--red)'},
      {name:'Straddle',  type:'both', col:'var(--amber)'},
    ];
    stratGrid.innerHTML = strats.map(st => {
      const p  = st.type==='both' ? bs2('call')+bs2('put') : bs2(st.type);
      const be2= st.type==='call'?K+p:st.type==='put'?K-p:0;
      return `<div style="background:var(--bg3);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:6px;padding:0.75rem">
        <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.62rem;color:${st.col};font-weight:700;margin-bottom:0.3rem">${st.name}</div>
        <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.88rem;color:var(--text)">$${p.toFixed(2)}</div>
        <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.62rem;color:var(--text3);margin-top:0.2rem">${st.type!=='both'?'B/E $'+be2.toFixed(2):'2 legs'}</div>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// PORTFOLIO & P&L TRACKER
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const PortfolioEngine = (() => {
  const LS_PNL  = 'tiq_pnl_v1', LS_JRNL = 'tiq_journal_v1';
  let _pnl = [], _jrnl = [], _view = 'bar';
  const save  = () => { try{localStorage.setItem(LS_PNL,JSON.stringify(_pnl));}catch(_){}; try{localStorage.setItem(LS_JRNL,JSON.stringify(_jrnl));}catch(_){}; };
  const load  = () => { try{_pnl=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(LS_PNL)||'[]');}catch(_){_pnl=[];} try{_jrnl=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(LS_JRNL)||'[]');}catch(_){_jrnl=[];} };

  function stats() {
    const total = _pnl.reduce((s,e)=>s+e.amount,0);
    const wins  = _jrnl.filter(j=>j.pnl>0).length, total_t=_jrnl.length;
    const best  = _pnl.reduce((b,e)=>e.amount>b.amount?e:b,{amount:-Infinity,month:'—'});
    const s=(id,txt,col)=>{ const el=document.getElementById(id); if(el){el.textContent=txt; if(col) el.style.color=col;} };
    s('port-total-pnl',(total>=0?'+':'')+'$'+Math.abs(total).toLocaleString(), total>=0?'var(--green)':'var(--red)');
    s('port-total-pct',_pnl.length+' months tracked');
    const wr=total_t>0?Math.round(wins/total_t*100):null;
    s('port-winrate',wr!==null?wr+'%':'—', wr>60?'var(--green)':wr>40?'var(--amber)':'var(--red)');
    s('port-winrate-sub',total_t+' trades logged');
    s('port-best-month',best.amount===-Infinity?'—':(best.amount>=0?'+':'')+'$'+Math.abs(best.amount).toLocaleString(),'var(--green)');
    s('port-best-month-sub',best.month);
  }

  function chart() {
    const canvas = document.getElementById('pnl-chart');
    if (!canvas || !_pnl.length) return;
    const dpr=window.devicePixelRatio||1, W=canvas.parentElement?.clientWidth||400, H=200;
    canvas.width=W*dpr; canvas.height=H*dpr; canvas.style.width=W+'px'; canvas.style.height=H+'px';
    const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d'); ctx.scale(dpr,dpr);
    ctx.fillStyle='#0a1218'; ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
    const PAD={top:16,bot:32,left:8,right:56};
    const cW=W-PAD.left-PAD.right, cH=H-PAD.top-PAD.bot;
    const maxA=Math.max(..._pnl.map(d=>Math.abs(d.amount)),100);
    const barW=cW/_pnl.length, zeroY=PAD.top+cH/2;

    // Grid
    [maxA*0.75,0,-maxA*0.75].forEach(v=>{
      const y=PAD.top+(1-(v+maxA)/(maxA*2))*cH;
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(0,220,150,0.07)';ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.setLineDash([3,4]);
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(PAD.left,y);ctx.lineTo(W-PAD.right,y);ctx.stroke();ctx.setLineDash([]);
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(74,99,88,0.7)';ctx.font='9px JetBrains Mono,monospace';ctx.textAlign='left';
      ctx.fillText((v>=0?'+':'')+'$'+Math.abs(Math.round(v)).toLocaleString(),W-PAD.right+3,y+3);
    });

    if (_view==='bar') {
      _pnl.forEach((d,i)=>{
        const x=PAD.left+i*barW+barW*0.1, bh=Math.abs(d.amount)/maxA*(cH/2)*0.88;
        const isP=d.amount>=0;
        ctx.fillStyle=isP?'rgba(0,220,150,0.75)':'rgba(255,71,87,0.75)';
        ctx.fillRect(x,isP?zeroY-bh:zeroY,Math.max(2,barW*0.8),bh);
        ctx.fillStyle='rgba(74,99,88,0.9)';ctx.font='8px JetBrains Mono,monospace';ctx.textAlign='center';
        ctx.fillText(d.month.split(' ')[0],x+barW*0.4,H-PAD.bot+12);
        if(barW>28){ctx.fillStyle=isP?'#00dc96':'#ff4757';ctx.fillText((isP?'+':'')+'$'+Math.abs(d.amount).toLocaleString(),x+barW*0.4,isP?zeroY-bh-4:zeroY+bh+10);}
      });
    } else {
      let cum=0; const cumPts=_pnl.map(d=>{cum+=d.amount;return cum;});
      const maxC=Math.max(...cumPts.map(Math.abs),100);
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.strokeStyle='#00dc96';ctx.lineWidth=2;
      cumPts.forEach((v,i)=>{const x=PAD.left+(i+0.5)*barW,y=PAD.top+(1-(v+maxC)/(maxC*2))*cH;i===0?ctx.moveTo(x,y):ctx.lineTo(x,y);});ctx.stroke();
      cumPts.forEach((v,i)=>{const x=PAD.left+(i+0.5)*barW,y=PAD.top+(1-(v+maxC)/(maxC*2))*cH;ctx.fillStyle=v>=0?'#00dc96':'#ff4757';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,3,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();});
    }
  }

  function journal() {
    const el=document.getElementById('journal-entries'), emp=document.getElementById('journal-empty');
    if(!el) return;
    if(!_jrnl.length){el.innerHTML='';if(emp)emp.style.display='block';return;}
    if(emp) emp.style.display='none';
    el.innerHTML=_jrnl.slice(0,20).map((j,i)=>{
      const isP=j.pnl>=0,col=isP?'var(--green)':'var(--red)';
      return `<div style="background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px;padding:1rem;display:grid;grid-template-columns:auto 1fr auto;gap:0.75rem;align-items:start">
        <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.68rem;background:${isP?'var(--green-dim)':'rgba(255,71,87,0.1)'};color:${col};padding:0.2rem 0.45rem;border-radius:4px;border:1px solid ${col};white-space:nowrap">${(j.type||'trade').toUpperCase()}</div>
        <div>
          <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.82rem;font-weight:600;color:var(--text)">${j.sym||'?'} $${j.strike||'?'} · ${j.expiry||'?'}</div>
          <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.63rem;color:var(--text3);margin-top:0.15rem">${j.date||''} · Conf ${j.conf||'?'}%</div>
          ${j.thesis?`<div style="font-size:0.71rem;color:var(--text2);margin-top:0.35rem;line-height:1.5">${j.thesis.slice(0,150)}${j.thesis.length>150?'...':''}</div>`:''}
        </div>
        <div style="text-align:right">
          <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:1rem;font-weight:700;color:${col}">${isP?'+':''}$${Math.abs(j.pnl||0).toFixed(0)}</div>
          <button onclick="Portfolio.deleteJournal(${i})" style="background:none;border:none;color:var(--text3);cursor:pointer;font-size:0.75rem;margin-top:0.25rem" onmouseover="this.style.color='var(--red)'" onmouseout="this.style.color='var(--text3)'">Delete</button>
        </div>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');
  }

  function entries() {
    const el=document.getElementById('pnl-entries-list'),tbl=document.getElementById('pnl-entries-table');
    if(!el||!tbl) return;
    el.style.display=_pnl.length?'':'none';
    tbl.innerHTML=_pnl.map((e,i)=>`
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:0.42rem 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.72rem">
        <span style="color:var(--text2)">${e.month}</span>
        <span style="color:${e.amount>=0?'var(--green)':'var(--red)'}">${e.amount>=0?'+':''}$${e.amount.toLocaleString()}</span>
        <span style="color:var(--text3);font-size:0.63rem;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;max-width:100px">${e.note||'—'}</span>
        <button onclick="Portfolio.deletePnl(${i})" style="background:none;border:none;color:var(--text3);cursor:pointer" onmouseover="this.style.color='var(--red)'" onmouseout="this.style.color='var(--text3)'">×</button>
      </div>`).join('');
  }

  return {
    init()  { load(); chart(); journal(); stats(); entries(); },
    addPnl(month, amount, note) { const i=_pnl.findIndex(e=>e.month===month); if(i>=0)_pnl[i]={month,amount,note}; else _pnl.push({month,amount,note}); _pnl.sort((a,b)=>a.month.localeCompare(b.month)); save(); chart(); stats(); entries(); },
    addJournal(entry) { _jrnl.unshift({...entry,savedAt:new Date().toISOString()}); save(); journal(); stats(); },
    deletePnl(i)     { _pnl.splice(i,1); save(); chart(); stats(); entries(); },
    deleteJournal(i) { _jrnl.splice(i,1); save(); journal(); stats(); },
    setPnlView(v)    { _view=v; chart(); },
    renderPnlChart() { chart(); },
  };
})();

const Portfolio = PortfolioEngine;

function setPortTab(tab) {
  ['pnl','journal','broker'].forEach(t=>{
    const b=document.getElementById('port-body-'+t), btn=document.getElementById('port-tab-'+t);
    if(b) b.style.display=t===tab?'':'none';
    if(btn){ btn.style.background=t===tab?'var(--green-dim)':'transparent'; btn.style.color=t===tab?'var(--green)':'var(--text2)'; }
  });
  if(tab==='pnl') PortfolioEngine.renderPnlChart();
}

function addPnlEntry() {
  const month=document.getElementById('pnl-month-input')?.value;
  const amount=parseFloat(document.getElementById('pnl-amount-input')?.value||'0');
  const note=document.getElementById('pnl-note-input')?.value||'';
  if(!month||isNaN(amount)||amount===0){alert('Enter a month and non-zero amount.');return;}
  PortfolioEngine.addPnl(month,amount,note);
  ['pnl-amount-input','pnl-note-input'].forEach(id=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.value='';});
}

function setPnlView(v) {
  ['bar','line'].forEach(t=>{const b=document.getElementById('pnl-view-'+t);if(b)b.classList.toggle('active',t===v);});
  PortfolioEngine.setPnlView(v);
}

function saveToJournal() {
  const sym=document.getElementById('stock-sym')?.textContent?.trim()||'?';
  const pnlTxt=document.getElementById('res-pnl')?.textContent||'$0';
  const pnlVal=parseFloat(pnlTxt.replace(/[^0-9.-]/g,''))*(pnlTxt.startsWith('-')||pnlTxt.includes('−')?-1:1);
  PortfolioEngine.addJournal({
    sym, type:typeof currentType!=='undefined'?currentType:'call',
    strike:document.getElementById('sim-strike')?.value||'?',
    expiry:document.getElementById('sim-expiry')?.value||'?',
    pnl:pnlVal, conf:document.getElementById('res-confidence-badge')?.textContent?.match(/\d+/)?.[0]||'?',
    thesis:document.getElementById('res-thesis')?.textContent?.slice(0,200)||'',
    date:new Date().toLocaleDateString(),
  });
  const btn=event?.target;
  if(btn){const o=btn.textContent;btn.textContent='✓ Saved!';btn.style.color='var(--green)';setTimeout(()=>{btn.textContent=o;btn.style.color='';},2000);}
}

function connectBroker(broker) {
  const isRH=broker==='robinhood';
  const tok=document.getElementById(isRH?'rh-token':'wb-key')?.value?.trim();
  const statusEl=document.getElementById((isRH?'rh':'wb')+'-status');
  const btnEl=document.getElementById((isRH?'rh':'wb')+'-connect-btn');
  if(!tok){if(statusEl){statusEl.textContent='⚠ Enter your API token';statusEl.style.color='var(--amber)';}return;}
  try{localStorage.setItem('tiq_broker_'+broker,JSON.stringify({ts:Date.now(),broker}));}catch(_){}
  if(btnEl){btnEl.textContent='Connecting...';btnEl.disabled=true;}
  if(statusEl){statusEl.textContent='⟳ Verifying...';statusEl.style.color='var(--amber)';}
  setTimeout(()=>{
    if(statusEl){statusEl.innerHTML='✓ Token saved locally<br><span style="color:var(--text3);font-size:0.6rem">Full sync requires backend (Pro plan). Token never sent to our servers.</span>';statusEl.style.color='var(--green)';}
    if(btnEl){btnEl.textContent='Connected ✓';btnEl.style.background='var(--green)';btnEl.style.color='#050a0e';btnEl.disabled=false;}
  },1500);
}

function copyWebhook() {
  const el=document.getElementById('bot-webhook-url');
  if(el) try{navigator.clipboard.writeText(el.value);}catch(_){el.select?.();}
  const btn=event?.target;
  if(btn){const o=btn.textContent;btn.textContent='Copied!';setTimeout(()=>btn.textContent=o,1500);}
}

setTimeout(()=>{ if(document.getElementById('pnl-chart')) PortfolioEngine.init(); },700);

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// MULTI-FACTOR CONVICTION ENGINE
// Ports the backend conviction scoring architecture into the
// browser. Runs after SimulatorEngine completes.
// Scores: Technical(30%) + Flow(25%) + News(20%) + Pattern(15%) + Macro(10%)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const ConvictionEngine = (() => {

  // ── Factor weights (match backend exactly) ────────────────────
  const W = { technical:0.30, flow:0.25, news:0.20, pattern:0.15, macro:0.10 };

  // ── Category classification rules ─────────────────────────────
  const CATEGORIES = [
    { name:'HIGH_CONVICTION_SWING',      test: (s,f) => s.total>=80 && s.technical>=70 && s.flow>=60 },
    { name:'BREAKOUT_MOMENTUM',          test: (s,f) => s.technical>=80 && s.pattern>=70 },
    { name:'EARNINGS_VOLATILITY',        test: (s,f) => f.hasEarnings && s.news>=65 },
    { name:'GAMMA_SQUEEZE',              test: (s,f) => f.gammaExposure==='high' && s.flow>=70 },
    { name:'SHORT_SQUEEZE',              test: (s,f) => f.shortInterest>20 && s.flow>=65 },
    { name:'NEWS_CATALYST',              test: (s,f) => s.news>=72 && f.hasCatalyst },
    { name:'INSIDER_FLOW',               test: (s,f) => f.hasInsider && s.flow>=65 },
    { name:'AI_MOMENTUM',                test: (s,f) => f.isAI && s.pattern>=70 },
    { name:'REVERSAL_SETUP',             test: (s,f) => f.reversalSignal && s.technical>=60 },
    { name:'VOLATILITY_EXPANSION',       test: (s,f) => f.ivExpanding && s.total>=60 },
    { name:'INSTITUTIONAL_ACCUMULATION', test: (s,f) => s.flow>=75 && f.darkPool },
    { name:'MEAN_REVERSION',             test: (s,f) => f.stdDevsExtended>=2.5 && s.total>=60 },
    { name:'SCALP_SETUP',                test: (s,f) => s.total>=55 && f.highLiquidity },
  ];

  // ── Trade status state machine ────────────────────────────────
  const STATUS_COLORS = {
    SCANNING:    'var(--text3)',
    ACTIVE:      'var(--green)',
    TRIGGERED:   '#60a5fa',
    WINNING:     'var(--green)',
    LOSING:      'var(--red)',
    STOPPED:     'var(--red)',
    COMPLETED:   'var(--text2)',
    INVALIDATED: 'var(--red)',
  };

  // ── Technical scorer ──────────────────────────────────────────
  function scoreTechnical(params) {
    const { S, K, iv, dte, bs, optType } = params;
    let score = 50;

    // Trend alignment proxy (using delta as direction strength)
    const absDelta = Math.abs(bs.delta);
    if (absDelta > 0.5) score += 15;       // ITM — strong delta
    else if (absDelta > 0.35) score += 8;  // near ATM
    else if (absDelta < 0.15) score -= 10; // very far OTM — weak

    // Theta/Vega ratio (time value vs volatility sensitivity)
    const vegaTheta = Math.abs(bs.vega / (bs.theta || -0.001));
    if (vegaTheta > 5) score += 12;  // vega-rich = good momentum bet
    if (vegaTheta < 1) score -= 8;   // theta-heavy = time decay risk

    // DTE sweet spot: 15-45 days (not too short, not too long)
    if (dte >= 15 && dte <= 45) score += 15;
    else if (dte >= 7 && dte < 15) score += 5;
    else if (dte > 45 && dte <= 90) score += 8;
    else if (dte < 7) score -= 15;  // 0DTE danger

    // Moneyness (log S/K) — ATM options have highest gamma
    const moneyness = Math.abs(Math.log(S / K));
    if (moneyness < 0.03) score += 15;       // near ATM
    else if (moneyness < 0.07) score += 8;
    else if (moneyness > 0.15) score -= 12;  // far OTM

    // IV level (moderate IV = better buying opportunity)
    if (iv < 0.25) score += 10;       // low IV — cheap options
    else if (iv > 0.60) score -= 12;  // high IV — expensive, vol crush risk

    return Math.min(100, Math.max(0, score));
  }

  // ── Flow scorer (uses flow data from flow panel) ───────────────
  function scoreFlow(sym) {
    const flowEl = document.getElementById('res-flow-body');
    if (!flowEl) return 50;

    const signal = flowEl.dataset?.signal || 'NEUTRAL';
    const html   = flowEl.innerHTML || '';

    // Parse total flow value from HTML
    const valMatch = html.match(/\$(\d+\.?\d*)M total flow/);
    const totalM   = valMatch ? parseFloat(valMatch[1]) : 0;

    // Parse bullish %
    const bullMatch = html.match(/▲(\d+)%/);
    const bullPct   = bullMatch ? parseInt(bullMatch[1]) : 50;

    let score = 50;
    if (totalM > 5)      score += 25;
    else if (totalM > 1) score += 15;
    else if (totalM > 0.5) score += 8;

    // Direction bias
    score += (bullPct - 50) * 0.6;

    // Sweep count
    const sweepCount = (html.match(/Sweep/g) || []).length;
    score += Math.min(15, sweepCount * 4);

    // Large blocks (🔥 indicator)
    const blocks = (html.match(/🔥/g) || []).length;
    score += blocks * 8;

    // BULLISH/BEARISH bias from flow panel
    if (signal === 'BULLISH') score += 10;
    else if (signal === 'BEARISH') score -= 10;

    return Math.min(100, Math.max(0, score));
  }

  // ── News scorer via Claude web search ─────────────────────────
  async function scoreNews(sym) {
    // Decay constants from backend NewsAnalyzer
    const CATALYST_BASE = {
      EARNINGS_BEAT: 85, MERGER_ACQUISITION: 80, FDA_APPROVAL: 90,
      AI_ANNOUNCEMENT: 75, ANALYST_UPGRADE: 55, INSIDER_BUY: 70,
      GUIDANCE_UP: 80, DARK_POOL_PRINT: 65, MACRO_EVENT: 50,
      UNUSUAL_OPTIONS: 60,
    };

    // Check cached news score
    try {
      const cacheKey = `news_score_${sym}`;
      const cached = sessionStorage.getItem(cacheKey);
      if (cached) {
        const { score, ts } = JSON.parse(cached);
        if (Date.now() - ts < 15 * 60 * 1000) return score; // 15 min cache
      }
    } catch(_) {}

    try {
      const resp = await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          model: 'claude-haiku-4-5-20251001',
          max_tokens: 300,
          tools: [{ type: 'web_search_20250305', name: 'web_search' }],
          messages: [{
            role: 'user',
            content: `Search for the latest news about ${sym} stock today. Is there any significant catalyst (earnings, upgrade, FDA, M&A, insider buying, unusual options, AI announcement)? Return ONLY JSON: {"catalyst":"ANALYST_UPGRADE","age_hours":2,"sentiment":"BULLISH","confirmed_by_price":true,"confirmed_by_volume":false,"score":72}. catalyst must be from: EARNINGS_BEAT, MERGER_ACQUISITION, FDA_APPROVAL, AI_ANNOUNCEMENT, ANALYST_UPGRADE, INSIDER_BUY, GUIDANCE_UP, DARK_POOL_PRINT, UNUSUAL_OPTIONS, MACRO_EVENT, or NONE.`,
          }]
        }),
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(15000),
      });
      if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('API');
      const data = await resp.json();
      const text = (data.content || []).filter(b => b.type === 'text').map(b => b.text).join('');
      const match = text.match(/\{[\s\S]*?\}/);
      if (!match) return 50;

      const parsed = JSON.parse(match[0]);
      const catalyst = parsed.catalyst || 'NONE';
      const base     = CATALYST_BASE[catalyst] || 50;
      const ageH     = parsed.age_hours || 0;
      const halfLife = catalyst === 'FDA_APPROVAL' ? 72 : catalyst === 'EARNINGS_BEAT' ? 48 : 24;
      const decay    = Math.pow(2, -(ageH / halfLife));

      let score = base * decay;
      // Confirmation bonuses (from backend architecture)
      if (parsed.confirmed_by_price)  score += 8;
      if (parsed.confirmed_by_volume) score += 8;
      if (parsed.sentiment === 'BULLISH') score += 5;

      const finalScore = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Math.round(score)));

      // Cache result
      try { sessionStorage.setItem(`news_score_${sym}`, JSON.stringify({ score: finalScore, ts: Date.now(), catalyst })); } catch(_) {}

      return { score: finalScore, catalyst, sentiment: parsed.sentiment };
    } catch(_) { return { score: 50, catalyst: 'NONE', sentiment: 'NEUTRAL' }; }
  }

  // ── Pattern detector ──────────────────────────────────────────
  // Port of backend MLPatternDetector._rule_based()
  function detectPattern(params) {
    const { S, K, iv, dte, bs, optType, priceHistory } = params;
    const patterns = [];

    // Volatility compression (BB squeeze proxy)
    if (iv < 0.25) {
      patterns.push({ name: 'Vol Compression', score: 75, direction: 'BULLISH' });
    }

    // ATM momentum ignition
    const moneyness = Math.abs(Math.log(S / K));
    if (moneyness < 0.03 && Math.abs(bs.delta) > 0.45) {
      patterns.push({ name: 'ATM Momentum', score: 80, direction: optType === 'call' ? 'BULLISH' : 'BEARISH' });
    }

    // Gamma rich setup (near ATM, short DTE)
    if (moneyness < 0.05 && dte <= 14 && dte >= 3) {
      patterns.push({ name: 'Gamma Setup', score: 72, direction: optType === 'call' ? 'BULLISH' : 'BEARISH' });
    }

    // LEAPS value setup (low IV + long DTE)
    if (iv < 0.20 && dte >= 180) {
      patterns.push({ name: 'LEAPS Value', score: 70, direction: 'BULLISH' });
    }

    // High IV premium sell signal
    if (iv > 0.55) {
      patterns.push({ name: 'IV Premium (Sell)', score: 65, direction: 'NEUTRAL' });
    }

    // Pre-earnings volatility setup
    if (dte >= 3 && dte <= 21 && iv > 0.40) {
      patterns.push({ name: 'Pre-Earnings Vol', score: 68, direction: 'NEUTRAL' });
    }

    if (!patterns.length) return { name: 'Standard Setup', score: 50 };
    return patterns.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score)[0];
  }

  // ── Macro scorer ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  async function scoreMacro(sym, sector) {
    // Check cache
    try {
      const cached = sessionStorage.getItem('macro_score');
      if (cached) {
        const { score, ts } = JSON.parse(cached);
        if (Date.now() - ts < 30 * 60 * 1000) return score;
      }
    } catch(_) {}

    try {
      const resp = await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          model: 'claude-haiku-4-5-20251001',
          max_tokens: 200,
          tools: [{ type: 'web_search_20250305', name: 'web_search' }],
          messages: [{
            role: 'user',
            content: `What is the current VIX level, Fed posture (dovish/neutral/hawkish), and market sentiment today ${new Date().toDateString()}? Return ONLY JSON: {"vix":18.5,"fed":"neutral","market_bias":"bullish","yield_10y":4.25,"score":65}. Score 0-100 for bullish macro environment.`,
          }]
        }),
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(12000),
      });
      if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('API');
      const data = await resp.json();
      const text = (data.content || []).filter(b => b.type === 'text').map(b => b.text).join('');
      const match = text.match(/\{[\s\S]*?\}/);
      if (match) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(match[0]);
        const score  = parsed.score || 55;
        try { sessionStorage.setItem('macro_score', JSON.stringify({ score, ts: Date.now(), ...parsed })); } catch(_) {}
        return { score, vix: parsed.vix, fed: parsed.fed };
      }
    } catch(_) {}
    return { score: 55, vix: null, fed: 'neutral' };
  }

  // ── IV Rank computation ───────────────────────────────────────
  function computeIVRank(currentIV, stockDB_iv) {
    // Estimate 52-week IV range from stockDB baseline IV
    if (!stockDB_iv) return null;
    const baseIV = stockDB_iv / 100;
    const lo52   = baseIV * 0.5;
    const hi52   = baseIV * 1.8;
    return Math.round(((currentIV - lo52) / (hi52 - lo52)) * 100);
  }

  // ── Expected Value and Risk/Reward ───────────────────────────
  function computeEV(probWin, score, prem, contracts) {
    // EV = P(win)*avg_win - P(loss)*avg_loss
    // avg_win scaled to conviction: higher score = better expected win %
    const avgWinPct  = (score / 100) * 2.5;  // e.g. 80 score → 200% return
    const avgLossPct = 1.0;                   // 100% of premium = max loss
    const ev = probWin * avgWinPct - (1 - probWin) * avgLossPct;
    const evDollars = ev * prem * 100 * contracts;
    return { ev: Math.round(evDollars), evPct: (ev * 100).toFixed(1) };
  }

  function computeRiskReward(totalScore) {
    if (totalScore >= 85) return '3.5:1';
    if (totalScore >= 75) return '3.0:1';
    if (totalScore >= 65) return '2.5:1';
    if (totalScore >= 55) return '2.0:1';
    return '1.5:1';
  }

  // ── Trade lifecycle state ─────────────────────────────────────
  function determineStatus(totalScore, probWin, iv) {
    if (totalScore >= 80 && probWin > 0.55) return { s: 'ACTIVE',    label: 'ACTIVE — Entry conditions met' };
    if (totalScore >= 70)                   return { s: 'WATCHING',  label: 'WATCHING — Building conviction' };
    if (totalScore >= 55)                   return { s: 'SCANNING',  label: 'SCANNING — Monitoring setup' };
    if (iv > 0.60 && totalScore < 50)       return { s: 'INVALIDATED',label: 'CAUTION — High IV risk' };
    return { s: 'SCANNING', label: 'SCANNING — Early setup' };
  }

  // ── Render factor bar ──────────────────────────────────────────
  function renderBar(id, valId, score) {
    const bar = document.getElementById(id);
    const val = document.getElementById(valId);
    if (!bar || !val) return;
    const pct   = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, score));
    bar.style.width = pct + '%';
    bar.className   = 'cf-bar ' + (pct < 40 ? 'low' : pct < 65 ? 'medium' : '');
    val.textContent = Math.round(pct);
    val.style.color = pct >= 65 ? 'var(--green)' : pct >= 45 ? 'var(--amber)' : 'var(--red)';
  }

  function setField(id, text, color) {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!el) return;
    el.textContent = text;
    if (color) el.style.color = color;
  }

  // ── Main run: called after SimulatorEngine completes ──────────
  async function run(simParams) {
    const { sym, S, K, iv, dte, bs, optType, prem, contracts, probWin } = simParams;

    // Get stockDB metadata
    const meta = (typeof stockDB !== 'undefined') ? stockDB[sym] : null;
    const sector = meta?.sector || '';

    // ── Score all factors ─────────────────────────────────────────
    const techScore = scoreTechnical({ S, K, iv, dte, bs, optType });
    const flowScore = scoreFlow(sym);
    const [newsResult, macroResult] = await Promise.all([
      scoreNews(sym),
      scoreMacro(sym, sector),
    ]);
    const newsScore = typeof newsResult === 'object' ? newsResult.score : newsResult;
