    const macroScore = typeof macroResult === 'object' ? macroResult.score : macroResult;
    const pattern = detectPattern({ S, K, iv, dte, bs, optType });
    const patternScore = pattern.score;

    // ── Weighted total ────────────────────────────────────────────
    const totalScore = Math.round(
      techScore   * W.technical +
      flowScore   * W.flow +
      newsScore   * W.news +
      patternScore * W.pattern +
      macroScore  * W.macro
    );

    // ── Category ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const scores = { total: totalScore, technical: techScore, flow: flowScore, news: newsScore, pattern: patternScore, macro: macroScore };
    const flags  = {
      hasEarnings:    dte <= 21 && iv > 0.40,
      gammaExposure:  Math.abs(bs.gamma) > 0.05 ? 'high' : 'low',
      ivExpanding:    iv > 0.40,
      isAI:           ['NVDA','PLTR','MSFT','GOOGL','META','AMD','ORCL','NOW','CRWD'].includes(sym),
      hasCatalyst:    typeof newsResult === 'object' && newsResult.catalyst !== 'NONE',
      hasInsider:     false,  // would come from insider DB in production
      darkPool:       flowScore > 70,
      highLiquidity:  ['SPY','QQQ','AAPL','TSLA','NVDA','MSFT','META','AMZN'].includes(sym),
      shortInterest:  0,
      stdDevsExtended:0,
      reversalSignal: false,
    };

    const cat = CATEGORIES.find(c => c.test(scores, flags))?.name || 'BREAKOUT_MOMENTUM';

    // ── IV Rank ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const ivRank = computeIVRank(iv, meta?.iv);

    // ── EV and R:R ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const { ev, evPct } = computeEV(probWin, totalScore, prem, contracts);
    const rr = computeRiskReward(totalScore);

    // ── Status ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const status = determineStatus(totalScore, probWin, iv);

    // ── Render everything ──────────────────────────────────────────
    // Total conviction score
    const scoreEl = document.getElementById('res-conviction-score');
    if (scoreEl) {
      scoreEl.textContent = totalScore + '/100';
      scoreEl.style.color = totalScore >= 75 ? 'var(--green)' : totalScore >= 55 ? 'var(--amber)' : 'var(--red)';
    }

    // Factor bars
    renderBar('cfb-technical', 'cfv-technical', techScore);
    renderBar('cfb-flow',      'cfv-flow',      flowScore);
    renderBar('cfb-news',      'cfv-news',      newsScore);
    renderBar('cfb-pattern',   'cfv-pattern',   patternScore);
    renderBar('cfb-macro',     'cfv-macro',     macroScore);

    // Category — human-readable
    const catReadable = cat.replace(/_/g,' ');
    setField('res-category', catReadable, 'var(--green)');

    // IV Rank
    if (ivRank !== null) {
      const ivRankEl = document.getElementById('res-iv-rank');
      if (ivRankEl) {
        ivRankEl.textContent = ivRank + '%';
        ivRankEl.style.color = ivRank > 70 ? 'var(--red)' : ivRank < 30 ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--amber)';
        ivRankEl.title = ivRank > 70 ? 'High IV — options expensive, consider selling' : ivRank < 30 ? 'Low IV — cheap options, good for buying' : 'Moderate IV';
      }
    } else {
      setField('res-iv-rank', 'N/A');
    }

    // Pattern
    setField('res-pattern', pattern.name, pattern.score >= 70 ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--text2)');

    // Expected value
    setField('res-expected-value', `${ev >= 0 ? '+' : ''}$${ev} (${ev >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${evPct}%)`, ev >= 0 ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)');

    // Risk/Reward
    setField('res-risk-reward', rr, 'var(--text)');

    // Trade status
    const statusEl = document.getElementById('res-trade-status');
    if (statusEl) {
      statusEl.textContent = status.label;
      statusEl.style.color = STATUS_COLORS[status.s] || 'var(--text2)';
    }

    // Update the AI confidence badge to reflect total conviction
    const confBadge = document.getElementById('res-confidence-badge');
    if (confBadge) {
      confBadge.textContent = `Conviction: ${totalScore}/100`;
      confBadge.style.color = totalScore >= 75 ? 'var(--green)' : totalScore >= 55 ? 'var(--amber)' : 'var(--red)';
      confBadge.style.borderColor = confBadge.style.color;
    }

    // Store for journal
    window._lastConviction = {
      totalScore, techScore, flowScore, newsScore, patternScore, macroScore,
      category: cat, ivRank, pattern: pattern.name, ev, rr, status: status.s,
    };

    console.log(`[Conviction] ${sym}: ${totalScore}/100 | ${cat} | ${status.s}`);
    return { totalScore, category: cat, status: status.s };
  }

  return { run };
})();

// Hook into SimulatorEngine — run conviction scoring after simulation
const _origSimRunConv = SimulatorEngine.run.bind(SimulatorEngine);
SimulatorEngine.run = async function() {
  await _origSimRunConv();
  // If results visible, run conviction engine
  const res = document.getElementById('sim-results');
  if (!res || !res.classList.contains('visible')) return;
  try {
    // Extract sim params from DOM for conviction engine
    const sym      = document.getElementById('stock-sym')?.textContent?.trim() || 'AAPL';
    const K        = parseFloat(document.getElementById('sim-strike')?.value || '0');
    const ivInput  = parseFloat(document.getElementById('sim-iv')?.value || '28') / 100;
    const prem     = parseFloat(document.getElementById('sim-premium')?.value || '0') || parseFloat(document.getElementById('res-theo')?.textContent?.replace('$','') || '0');
    const contracts= parseInt(document.getElementById('sim-contracts')?.value || '1', 10);
    const optType  = typeof currentType !== 'undefined' ? currentType : 'call';
    let S = 0;
    try { S = (typeof PriceEngine !== 'undefined' ? PriceEngine.get(sym)?.price : 0) || (typeof selectedStock !== 'undefined' ? selectedStock?.price : 200) || 200; } catch(_) { S = 200; }

    const today   = new Date(); today.setHours(0,0,0,0);
    const expVal  = document.getElementById('sim-expiry')?.value || '';
    const expDate = new Date((expVal || new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)) + 'T00:00:00');
    const dte     = Math.max(0, Math.round((expDate - today) / 86400000));
    const T       = dte === 0 ? 6.5/(252*6.5) : dte/365;
    const r       = 0.0525;

    // Reconstruct BS for conviction engine
    const sigma = ivInput > 0 ? ivInput : 0.28;
    function _Nlocal(x) { const a=[0.254829592,-0.284496736,1.421413741,-1.453152027,1.061405429],p=0.3275911,s=x<0?-1:1;x=Math.abs(x)/Math.SQRT2;const t=1/(1+p*x),y=1-(((((a[4]*t+a[3])*t)+a[2])*t+a[1])*t+a[0])*t*Math.exp(-x*x);return 0.5*(1+s*y); }
    const sqrtT = Math.sqrt(T||0.001);
    const d1 = (Math.log(S/(K||S)) + (r+0.5*sigma*sigma)*(T||0.001))/(sigma*sqrtT);
    const d2 = d1 - sigma*sqrtT;
    const bs = {
      delta: optType==='call'?_Nlocal(d1):_Nlocal(d1)-1,
      gamma: Math.exp(-0.5*d1*d1)/Math.sqrt(2*Math.PI)/(S*sigma*sqrtT),
      theta: 0,
      vega:  S*sqrtT*Math.exp(-0.5*d1*d1)/Math.sqrt(2*Math.PI)*0.01,
    };

    // POP from results
    const popEl  = document.getElementById('res-pop');
    const popPct = popEl ? parseFloat(popEl.textContent) / 100 : 0.5;

    await ConvictionEngine.run({ sym, S, K: K||S, iv: sigma, dte, bs, optType, prem: prem||1, contracts, probWin: popPct });
  } catch(e) { console.warn('[ConvictionEngine]', e.message); }
};

// ─── Global Nav Search ─────────────────────────────────────────
const NAV_SECTIONS = [
  { label:'AI Simulator',     icon:'⬡', id:'simulator',   desc:'Run options simulations with AI' },
  { label:'Stock Screener',   icon:'🚀', id:'screener',    desc:'Bullish momentum & breakout picks' },
  { label:'Charts',           icon:'📈', id:'charts',      desc:'Interactive candlestick charts' },
  { label:'Indicators',       icon:'📊', id:'indicators',  desc:'RSI, MACD, Bollinger Bands chat' },
  { label:'Congress Trades',  icon:'🏛', id:'congress-bar',desc:'STOCK Act live filings' },
  { label:'Features',         icon:'✦',  id:'features',    desc:'All TradeIQ features' },
  { label:'Pricing',          icon:'💳', id:'pricing',     desc:'Plans & pricing' },
  { label:'FAQ',              icon:'❓', id:'faq',         desc:'Frequently asked questions' },
];

function navSearch(q) {
  const box = document.getElementById('nav-search-results');
  if (!box) return;
  if (!q.trim()) { box.style.display = 'none'; return; }
  const ql = q.toLowerCase();

  // Section matches
  const secMatches = NAV_SECTIONS.filter(s =>
    s.label.toLowerCase().includes(ql) || s.desc.toLowerCase().includes(ql)
  );

  // Stock matches (from stockDB)
  const stockMatches = Object.entries(typeof stockDB !== 'undefined' ? stockDB : {})
    .filter(([sym, d]) =>
      sym.toLowerCase().startsWith(ql) ||
      d.name.toLowerCase().includes(ql)
    ).slice(0, 4);

  if (!secMatches.length && !stockMatches.length) {
    box.innerHTML = '<div style="padding:0.75rem 0.875rem;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--text3)">No results for "' + q + '"</div>';
    box.style.display = 'block';
    return;
  }

  let html = '';
  if (secMatches.length) {
    html += '<div class="ns-section-header">Pages</div>';
    html += secMatches.map(s => `
      <div class="ns-result" onclick="document.getElementById('${s.id}')?.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth'});document.getElementById('nav-search-results').style.display='none';document.getElementById('nav-search-input').value=''">
        <span style="font-size:1rem">${s.icon}</span>
        <div>
          <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.78rem;color:var(--text)">${s.label}</div>
          <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.62rem;color:var(--text3)">${s.desc}</div>
        </div>
      </div>`).join('');
  }
  if (stockMatches.length) {
    html += '<div class="ns-section-header">Stocks</div>';
    html += stockMatches.map(([sym, d]) => {
      const live = typeof PriceEngine !== 'undefined' ? PriceEngine.get(sym) : null;
      const px   = live ? '$' + live.price.toFixed(2) : '';
      const chg  = live ? (live.chgPct >= 0 ? '+' : '') + live.chgPct.toFixed(2) + '%' : '';
      const col  = live && live.chgPct >= 0 ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--red)';
      return `<div class="ns-result" onclick="document.getElementById('simulator')?.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth'});document.getElementById('sim-ticker-search').value='${sym}';loadStockPrice('${sym}');document.getElementById('nav-search-results').style.display='none';document.getElementById('nav-search-input').value=''">
        <div style="width:32px;height:32px;border-radius:6px;background:var(--bg3);border:1px solid var(--border);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.7rem;font-weight:700;flex-shrink:0">${sym.slice(0,3)}</div>
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
          <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.78rem;color:var(--text)">${sym} <span style="font-size:0.65rem;color:var(--text2)">${d.name}</span></div>
          <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.62rem;color:var(--text3)">${d.sector}</div>
        </div>
        ${px ? `<div style="text-align:right;flex-shrink:0"><div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.78rem">${px}</div><div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.62rem;color:${col}">${chg}</div></div>` : ''}
      </div>`;
    }).join('');
  }
  html += '<div style="padding:0.35rem 0.875rem;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.6rem;color:var(--text3);background:var(--bg3);border-top:1px solid var(--border)">↑↓ navigate · Enter select · Esc close</div>';
  box.innerHTML = html;
  box.style.display = 'block';
}

function navSearchKey(e) {
  if (e.key === 'Escape') {
    document.getElementById('nav-search-results').style.display = 'none';
    e.target.value = '';
  } else if (e.key === 'Enter') {
    const first = document.querySelector('#nav-search-results .ns-result');
    if (first) first.click();
  }
}

function navSearchFocus() {
  const v = document.getElementById('nav-search-input')?.value;
  if (v) navSearch(v);
}

// Keyboard shortcut: '/' to focus nav search
document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
  if (e.key === '/' && !['INPUT','TEXTAREA'].includes(document.activeElement.tagName)) {
    e.preventDefault();
    document.getElementById('nav-search-input')?.focus();
  }
  if (e.key === 'Escape') {
    document.getElementById('nav-search-results').style.display = 'none';
  }
});

// ── Back to top + dock active state ──────────────────────────
(function() {
  const btt = document.getElementById('back-to-top');
  window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
    if (btt) btt.style.display = window.scrollY > 400 ? 'flex' : 'none';

    // Update dock active state
    const sy = window.scrollY + 150;
    const DOCK_MAP = [
      { btn: 'simulator', sec: 'simulator' },
      { btn: 'charts',    sec: 'charts'    },
      { btn: 'indicators',sec: 'indicators'},
      { btn: 'congress-bar', sec: 'congress-bar'},
      { btn: 'pricing',   sec: 'pricing'   },
    ];
    let activeSec = null;
    DOCK_MAP.forEach(({ sec }) => {
      const el = document.getElementById(sec);
      if (el && el.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.scrollY <= sy) activeSec = sec;
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('.dock-btn').forEach(b => {
      const href = b.getAttribute('href');
      b.classList.toggle('active', href && href === '#' + activeSec);
    });
  }, { passive: true });
})();
(function() {
  const PILLS = [
    { id:'pill-simulator',  sec:'simulator'    },
    { id:'pill-screener',   sec:'screener'     },
    { id:'pill-charts',     sec:'charts'       },
    { id:'pill-indicators', sec:'indicators'   },
    { id:'pill-congress',   sec:'congress-bar' },
    { id:'pill-features',   sec:'features'     },
    { id:'pill-pricing',    sec:'pricing'      },
    { id:'pill-faq',        sec:'faq'          },
  ];
  function update() {
    const sy = window.scrollY + 130;
    let active = PILLS[0].id;
    PILLS.forEach(({ id, sec }) => {
      const el = document.getElementById(sec);
      if (el && el.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.scrollY <= sy) active = id;
    });
    PILLS.forEach(({ id }) => {
      const el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (el) el.classList.toggle('active', id === active);
    });
  }
  let tick = false;
  window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
    if (!tick) { requestAnimationFrame(() => { update(); tick = false; }); tick = true; }
  }, { passive: true });
  setTimeout(update, 400);
})();

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// NEWS ENGINE — fetches live financial news via Claude web_search
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const NewsEngine = (() => {
  const LS_KEY = 'tiq_news_v1';
  const TTL    = 15 * 60 * 1000; // 15 min cache
  let _allNews   = [];
  let _filter    = 'all';
  let _loaded    = false;

  // Hardcoded recent news for instant display while live loads
  const SEED_NEWS = [
    { id:1,  cat:'fed',      headline:'Fed holds rates at 4.25-4.5% — Powell signals two cuts possible in H2 2026', source:'Reuters',    time:'2h ago',  url:'https://reuters.com', impact:'high',   summary:'Federal Reserve kept interest rates unchanged at 4.25-4.5% at May meeting. Chair Powell noted progress on inflation but emphasized data-dependency for future cuts.' },
    { id:2,  cat:'markets',  headline:'S&P 500 hits fresh record at 5,930 as inflation data cools to 2.3% YoY', source:'CNBC',       time:'3h ago',  url:'https://cnbc.com',    impact:'high',   summary:'CPI came in at 2.3% year-over-year for April 2026, below the 2.5% consensus. Markets rallied with SPY touching new all-time highs.' },
    { id:3,  cat:'politics', headline:'Trump signs executive order on strategic Bitcoin reserve — Treasury to hold 50K BTC', source:'Bloomberg',  time:'4h ago',  url:'https://bloomberg.com',impact:'high', summary:'President Trump signed an order establishing the Strategic Bitcoin Reserve. Treasury Department will not sell existing BTC holdings and may acquire more.' },

PLTR stock up 12% on the news.' },
    { id:12, cat:'fed',      headline:'Treasury yields fall as 10Y hits 4.12% — bond market pricing in 2026 cuts', source:'WSJ',         time:'1d ago',  url:'https://wsj.com',     impact:'medium', summary:'10-year Treasury yields fell to 4.12%, the lowest since January 2026, as bond markets price in 2 rate cuts by year-end.' },
  ];

  async function fetchLive() {
    try {
      const resp = await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          model: 'claude-haiku-4-5-20251001',
          max_tokens: 1500,
          tools: [{ type: 'web_search_20250305', name: 'web_search' }],
          messages: [{ role: 'user', content: `Get the 8 most important financial news stories from today ${new Date().toDateString()}. Include Fed, CPI, earnings, Trump policies affecting markets, crypto, and major stock moves. Return ONLY a JSON array, no other text: [{"headline":"...","source":"Reuters","time":"2h ago","cat":"markets","impact":"high","summary":"2-sentence summary","url":"https://..."}]. cat must be one of: markets, fed, earnings, politics, crypto. impact: high/medium/low.` }]
        }),
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(20000),
      });
      if (!resp.ok) return null;
      const data = await resp.json();
      const text = (data.content||[]).filter(b=>b.type==='text').map(b=>b.text).join('');
      const match = text.match(/\[[\s\S]*\]/);
      if (!match) return null;
      const parsed = JSON.parse(match[0]);
      return parsed.filter(n => n.headline && n.source).map((n, i) => ({ ...n, id: Date.now()+i }));
    } catch(_) { return null; }
  }

  function render() {
    const list    = document.getElementById('news-list');
    const loading = document.getElementById('news-loading');
    if (!list) return;

    const filtered = _filter === 'all'
      ? _allNews
      : _allNews.filter(n => n.cat === _filter);

    if (loading) loading.style.display = 'none';
    list.style.display = 'block';

    if (!filtered.length) {
      list.innerHTML = `<div style="padding:1.5rem;text-align:center;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--text3)">No ${_filter} news right now</div>`;
      return;
    }

    const impactColor = { high:'#ff4757', medium:'#ffa502', low:'#7bed9f' };
    const catEmoji    = { markets:'📈', fed:'🏦', earnings:'💰', politics:'🏛', crypto:'₿' };

    list.innerHTML = filtered.map(n => `
      <a class="news-item" href="${n.url||'#'}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-start;gap:0.5rem;margin-bottom:0.35rem">
          <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:0.4rem">
            <span style="width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%;background:${impactColor[n.impact]||'#ffa502'};flex-shrink:0;margin-top:2px;display:inline-block"></span>
            <span style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.58rem;color:var(--text3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:0.07em">${catEmoji[n.cat]||'📰'} ${n.cat}</span>
          </div>
          <span style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.58rem;color:var(--text3);white-space:nowrap;flex-shrink:0">${n.time||''}</span>
        </div>
        <div style="font-family:var(--sans);font-size:0.8rem;font-weight:600;color:var(--text);line-height:1.4;margin-bottom:0.3rem">${n.headline}</div>
        ${n.summary ? `<div style="font-family:var(--sans);font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text2);line-height:1.5">${n.summary}</div>` : ''}
        <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.6rem;color:var(--text3);margin-top:0.3rem">${n.source||''} ↗</div>
      </a>`).join('');

    // Update badge
    const badge = document.getElementById('news-badge');
    const highCount = _allNews.filter(n=>n.impact==='high').length;
    if (badge && highCount > 0) { badge.style.display='inline'; badge.textContent=highCount+' NEW'; }
  }

  async function init() {
    // Show seed immediately
    _allNews = SEED_NEWS;
    if (document.getElementById('news-list')) render();

    // Try live fetch
    const live = await fetchLive();
    if (live && live.length >= 3) {
      _allNews = [...live, ...SEED_NEWS.filter(s => !live.find(l => l.headline.slice(0,30)===s.headline.slice(0,30)))].slice(0, 20);
      render();
      try { localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY, JSON.stringify({ ts: Date.now(), data: _allNews })); } catch(_){}
    }
    _loaded = true;
  }

  function open() {
    if (!_loaded) {
      // Show seed immediately, fetch in background
      _allNews = SEED_NEWS;
      render();
      init();
    } else {
      render();
    }
  }

  return {
    open,
    filter(f) { _filter = f; render(); },
  };
})();

// MARKET CALENDAR ENGINE — economic events, Fed, CPI, earnings

const CalendarEngine = (() => {

  ];

  function getDaysUntil(dateStr) {
    const today = new Date(); today.setHours(0,0,0,0);
    const target = new Date(dateStr + 'T00:00:00'); target.setHours(0,0,0,0);
    return Math.round((target - today) / 86400000);
  }

  function render() {
    const list = document.getElementById('cal-list');
    if (!list) return;

    const today = new Date(); today.setHours(0,0,0,0);
    const filtered = _filter === 'all'
      ? EVENTS
      : EVENTS.filter(e => e.cat === _filter || (_filter==='cpi' && e.cat==='cpi') || (_filter==='fed' && e.cat==='fed') || (_filter==='earnings' && e.cat==='earnings'));

    const impColor  = { high:'#ff4757', medium:'#ffa502', low:'#7bed9f' };
    const catEmoji  = { fed:'🏦', cpi:'📊', jobs:'💼', earnings:'💰', politics:'🏛', markets:'📈', econ:'🏭', sentiment:'😊' };

    // Group by month
    const grouped = {};
    filtered.forEach(e => {
      const d = new Date(e.date + 'T00:00:00');
      const key = d.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month:'long', year:'numeric' });
      if (!grouped[key]) grouped[key] = [];
      grouped[key].push(e);
    });

    // Count upcoming high-impact events (next 30 days)
    const upcomingHigh = filtered.filter(e => { const d=getDaysUntil(e.date); return d>=0&&d<=30&&e.impact==='high'; }).length;
    const badge = document.getElementById('cal-badge');
    if (badge) badge.textContent = upcomingHigh > 0 ? upcomingHigh : '';

    let html = '';
    Object.entries(grouped).forEach(([month, events]) => {
      html += `<div style="padding:0.5rem 1rem 0.3rem;background:var(--bg3);border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.62rem;color:var(--text3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:0.09em;font-weight:600">${month}</div>`;
      events.forEach(e => {
        const daysUntil = getDaysUntil(e.date);
        const isToday   = daysUntil === 0;
        const isPast    = daysUntil < 0;
        const isSoon    = daysUntil >= 0 && daysUntil <= 3;

        const dLabel = isToday ? 'TODAY' : daysUntil === 1 ? 'TOMORROW' : isPast ? `${Math.abs(daysUntil)}d ago` : `in ${daysUntil}d`;
        const dColor = isToday ? '#00dc96' : isSoon ? '#ffa502' : isPast ? 'var(--text3)' : 'var(--text2)';

        html += `<div class="cal-event" style="${isPast?'opacity:0.55':''}" onclick="calEventClick('${e.title.replace(/'/g,'').slice(0,40)}')">
          <div class="cal-dot cal-impact-${e.impact}" style="margin-top:4px"></div>
          <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
            <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-start;gap:0.5rem;margin-bottom:0.2rem">
              <div style="font-family:var(--sans);font-size:0.78rem;font-weight:600;color:${isPast?'var(--text3)':'var(--text)'};line-height:1.3">${catEmoji[e.cat]||'📌'} ${e.title}</div>
              <span style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.58rem;color:${dColor};font-weight:700;white-space:nowrap;flex-shrink:0;background:${isToday?'rgba(0,220,150,0.12)':isSoon?'rgba(255,165,2,0.12)':'transparent'};padding:0.1rem 0.3rem;border-radius:3px">${dLabel}</span>
            </div>
            <div style="display:flex;gap:0.75rem;flex-wrap:wrap">
              <span style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.62rem;color:var(--text3)">${e.time||''}</span>
              ${e.expected ? `<span style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.62rem;color:var(--amber)">Est: ${e.expected}</span>` : ''}
              <span style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.58rem;color:${impColor[e.impact]};text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:0.05em">${e.impact} impact</span>
            </div>
            ${e.note ? `<div style="font-family:var(--sans);font-size:0.68rem;color:var(--text3);margin-top:0.25rem;line-height:1.4">${e.note}</div>` : ''}
          </div>
        </div>`;
      });
    });

    list.innerHTML = html || '<div style="padding:1.5rem;text-align:center;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--text3)">No events for this filter</div>';
  }

  return {
    init() { render(); },
    filter(f) { _filter = f; render(); },
  };
})();

// ── Panel toggle logic ────────────────────────────────────────
let _openPanel = null;

function togglePanel(name) {
  const panelId = name + '-panel';
  const panel   = document.getElementById(panelId);
  if (!panel) return;

  const isOpen = panel.classList.contains('open');

  // Close all panels first
  document.querySelectorAll('#news-panel, #cal-panel').forEach(p => {
    p.classList.remove('open');
    p.style.display = 'none';
  });

  if (!isOpen) {
    panel.classList.add('open');
    panel.style.display = 'flex';
    panel.style.flexDirection = 'column';
    _openPanel = name;
    if (name === 'news') NewsEngine.open();
    if (name === 'cal')  { CalendarEngine.init(); }
  } else {
    _openPanel = null;
  }
}

function filterNews(f) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.news-filter-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
  const btn = document.getElementById('news-f-' + f);
  if (btn) btn.classList.add('active');
  NewsEngine.filter(f);
}

function filterCal(f) {
  document.querySelectorAll('[id^="cal-f-"]').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
  const btn = document.getElementById('cal-f-' + f);
  if (btn) btn.classList.add('active');
  CalendarEngine.filter(f);
}

function calEventClick(title) {
  // Could open detail modal — for now just searches nav
  const inp = document.getElementById('nav-search-input');
  if (inp) { inp.value = title.slice(0,20); inp.focus(); }
}

// Close panels when clicking outside
document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
  if (_openPanel) {
    const panel  = document.getElementById(_openPanel + '-panel');
    const btn    = document.getElementById(_openPanel + '-btn');
    if (panel && !panel.contains(e.target) && btn && !btn.contains(e.target)) {
      panel.classList.remove('open');
      panel.style.display = 'none';
      _openPanel = null;
    }
  }
});

// Init calendar badge on load
setTimeout(() => CalendarEngine.init(), 300);

  // ── Signal color mapping ───────────────────────────────────────
  const SIG_STYLE = {
    'STRONG BUY':  { bg:'rgba(0,220,150,0.15)',  color:'#00dc96',  border:'rgba(0,220,150,0.4)'  },
    'AI TOP PICK': { bg:'rgba(0,220,150,0.2)',   color:'#00f5a8',  border:'rgba(0,220,150,0.5)'  },
    'BUY':         { bg:'rgba(0,220,150,0.1)',   color:'#00dc96',  border:'rgba(0,220,150,0.25)' },
    'BREAKOUT':    { bg:'rgba(96,165,250,0.15)', color:'#60a5fa',  border:'rgba(96,165,250,0.4)' },
    'AI PICK':     { bg:'rgba(167,139,250,0.15)',color:'#a78bfa',  border:'rgba(167,139,250,0.4)'},
    'PRE-EARNINGS':{ bg:'rgba(255,165,2,0.15)',  color:'#ffa502',  border:'rgba(255,165,2,0.4)'  },
    'VOLUME SURGE':{ bg:'rgba(251,146,60,0.15)', color:'#fb923c',  border:'rgba(251,146,60,0.4)' },
    '52W HIGH':    { bg:'rgba(232,121,249,0.15)',color:'#e879f9',  border:'rgba(232,121,249,0.4)'},
    'WATCH':       { bg:'rgba(255,165,2,0.1)',   color:'#ffa502',  border:'rgba(255,165,2,0.25)' },
  };

  // ── Score bar HTML ────────────────────────────────────────────
  function _scorebar(score) {
    const color = score >= 90 ? '#00dc96' : score >= 80 ? '#ffa502' : '#60a5fa';
    return `<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:0.5rem">
      <div style="flex:1;height:4px;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.08);border-radius:2px;overflow:hidden">
        <div style="width:${score}%;height:100%;background:${color};border-radius:2px;transition:width 0.6s ease"></div>
      </div>
      <span style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.62rem;color:${color};font-weight:700;min-width:28px">${score}</span>
    </div>`;
  }

  // ── Render one card ───────────────────────────────────────────
  function _card(stock, idx) {
    const live = (typeof PriceEngine !== 'undefined') ? PriceEngine.get(stock.sym) : null;
    const price   = live?.price || stock.price || 0;
    const chgPct  = live?.chgPct ?? stock.chgPct ?? 0;
    const chg     = live?.chg    ?? stock.chg    ?? 0;
    const isUp    = chgPct >= 0;
    const col     = isUp ? '#00dc96' : '#ff4757';
    const sig     = SIG_STYLE[stock.signal] || SIG_STYLE['BUY'];
    const isTop   = stock.score >= 90;
    const upside  = stock.upside || 0;
    const tags    = (stock.tags || []).slice(0,3);
    const delay   = (idx % 6) * 0.04;

    return `<div class="scr-card ${isTop ? 'top-pick' : ''}"
        style="animation-delay:${delay}s"
        onclick="loadPickIntoSim('${stock.sym}','${stock.entry||Math.round(price)}','${stock.target||Math.round(price*1.15)}','—','call')">
      <!-- Header -->
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-start;margin-bottom:0.75rem">
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:0.6rem">
          <div style="width:38px;height:38px;border-radius:8px;background:var(--bg3);border:1px solid var(--border);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.65rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--green);flex-shrink:0">${stock.sym.slice(0,4)}</div>
          <div>
            <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.88rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--text)">${stock.sym}</div>
            <div style="font-family:var(--sans);font-size:0.68rem;color:var(--text3);white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;max-width:140px;text-overflow:ellipsis">${stock.name}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align:right">
          <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:1rem;font-weight:600;color:var(--text)">$${price.toFixed(2)}</div>
          <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.68rem;color:${col}">${isUp?'+':''}${chgPct.toFixed(2)}% ${isUp?'▲':'▼'}</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Signal badge + score -->
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:0.5rem;margin-bottom:0.6rem;flex-wrap:wrap">
        <span style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.6rem;font-weight:700;padding:0.18rem 0.55rem;border-radius:4px;background:${sig.bg};color:${sig.color};border:1px solid ${sig.border};letter-spacing:0.06em;white-space:nowrap">${stock.signal}</span>
        <span style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.6rem;color:var(--green);background:rgba(0,220,150,0.1);padding:0.18rem 0.5rem;border-radius:4px;border:1px solid rgba(0,220,150,0.2)">+${upside}% upside</span>
        ${isTop ? '<span style="font-size:0.7rem">⭐</span>' : ''}
      </div>

      <!-- Score bar -->
      <div style="margin-bottom:0.6rem">
        <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.58rem;color:var(--text3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:0.07em;margin-bottom:0.25rem">AI Conviction</div>
        ${_scorebar(stock.score)}
      </div>

      <!-- Entry/Target/Stop -->
      <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr 1fr;gap:0.4rem;margin-bottom:0.6rem;background:var(--bg3);border-radius:6px;padding:0.5rem">
        <div style="text-align:center">
          <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.55rem;color:var(--text3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:0.06em">Entry</div>
          <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.78rem;color:var(--text)">$${stock.entry||'—'}</div>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align:center;border-left:1px solid var(--border);border-right:1px solid var(--border)">
          <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.55rem;color:var(--text3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:0.06em">Target</div>
          <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.78rem;color:var(--green)">$${stock.target||'—'}</div>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align:center">
          <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.55rem;color:var(--text3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:0.06em">Stop</div>
          <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.78rem;color:var(--red)">$${stock.stop||'—'}</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Tags -->
      <div style="display:flex;gap:0.3rem;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:0.6rem">
        ${tags.map(t=>`<span style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.58rem;color:var(--text3);background:var(--bg3);padding:0.12rem 0.42rem;border-radius:3px;border:1px solid var(--border)">${t}</span>`).join('')}
        <span style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.58rem;color:var(--text3);background:var(--bg3);padding:0.12rem 0.42rem;border-radius:3px;border:1px solid var(--border)">${stock.sector||''}</span>
      </div>

      <!-- Thesis -->
      <div style="font-family:var(--sans);font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text2);line-height:1.55;margin-bottom:0.75rem;display:-webkit-box;-webkit-line-clamp:3;-webkit-box-orient:vertical;overflow:hidden">${stock.thesis}</div>

      <!-- CTA -->
      <button style="width:100%;padding:0.45rem;background:var(--green-dim);border:1px solid var(--border2);color:var(--green);border-radius:6px;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.68rem;cursor:pointer;transition:all 0.15s;letter-spacing:0.04em"
        onmouseover="this.style.background='rgba(0,220,150,0.2)'" onmouseout="this.style.background='var(--green-dim)'">
        Simulate ${stock.sym} Options →
      </button>
    </div>`;
  }

  // ── Render table row ─────────────────────────────────────────
  function _row(stock) {
    const live = (typeof PriceEngine !== 'undefined') ? PriceEngine.get(stock.sym) : null;
    const price  = live?.price || stock.price || 0;
    const chgPct = live?.chgPct ?? stock.chgPct ?? 0;
    const isUp   = chgPct >= 0;
    const col    = isUp ? '#00dc96' : '#ff4757';
    const sig    = SIG_STYLE[stock.signal] || SIG_STYLE['BUY'];
    return `<tr style="border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);transition:background 0.1s" onmouseover="this.style.background='rgba(0,220,150,0.04)'" onmouseout="this.style.background=''">
      <td style="padding:0.65rem 1rem">
        <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-weight:700;color:var(--text)">${stock.sym}</div>
        <div style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.58rem;color:var(--text3)">${stock.sector}</div>
      </td>
      <td style="padding:0.65rem 0.75rem;font-family:var(--sans);font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text2);max-width:180px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap">${stock.name}</td>
      <td style="padding:0.65rem 0.75rem;text-align:right;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.78rem;color:var(--text)">$${price.toFixed(2)}</td>
      <td style="padding:0.65rem 0.75rem;text-align:right;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.72rem;color:${col}">${isUp?'+':''}${chgPct.toFixed(2)}%</td>
      <td style="padding:0.65rem 0.75rem;text-align:right">
        <span style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.58rem;font-weight:700;padding:0.15rem 0.45rem;border-radius:3px;background:${sig.bg};color:${sig.color};border:1px solid ${sig.border};white-space:nowrap">${stock.signal}</span>
      </td>
      <td style="padding:0.65rem 0.75rem;text-align:right;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.75rem;color:var(--green)">+${stock.upside}%</td>
      <td style="padding:0.65rem 0.75rem;text-align:right">
        <button onclick="loadPickIntoSim('${stock.sym}','${stock.entry||Math.round(stock.price)}','${stock.target||Math.round(stock.price*1.15)}','—','call')" style="background:var(--green-dim);border:1px solid var(--border2);color:var(--green);padding:0.25rem 0.6rem;border-radius:4px;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:0.62rem;cursor:pointer">Simulate →</button>
      </td>
    </tr>`;
  }

  // ── Render the full screener ─────────────────────────────────
  function _render(stocks) {
    _results = stocks;
    const grid = document.getElementById('screener-grid');
    const tbody = document.getElementById('screener-tbody');
    const loading = document.getElementById('screener-loading');
    const tableWrap = document.getElementById('screener-table-wrap');
    if (loading) loading.style.display = 'none';

    if (_view === 'grid') {
      if (grid)      { grid.style.display = 'grid'; grid.innerHTML = stocks.map((s,i) => _card(s,i)).join(''); }
      if (tableWrap) tableWrap.style.display = 'none';
    } else {
      if (grid)      grid.style.display = 'none';
      if (tableWrap) tableWrap.style.display = 'block';
      if (tbody)     tbody.innerHTML = stocks.map(_row).join('');
    }

    // Update stats
    const bullCount = stocks.filter(s => s.score >= 80).length;
    const avgUp     = Math.round(stocks.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.upside,0)/stocks.length);
    const sectors   = stocks.reduce((m,s)=>{m[s.sector]=(m[s.sector]||0)+1;return m;},{});
    const topSec    = Object.entries(sectors).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1])[0]?.[0] || '—';
    const bullPct   = Math.round(bullCount/stocks.length*100);
    const el = id => document.getElementById(id);
    if (el('scr-stat-bull'))   el('scr-stat-bull').textContent   = bullCount + ' / ' + stocks.length;
    if (el('scr-stat-upside')) el('scr-stat-upside').textContent = '+' + avgUp + '% avg';
    if (el('scr-stat-sector')) el('scr-stat-sector').textContent = topSec;
    if (el('scr-stat-bias'))   { el('scr-stat-bias').textContent = bullPct >= 70 ? 'STRONGLY BULLISH' : bullPct >= 50 ? 'BULLISH' : 'MIXED'; el('scr-stat-bias').style.color = bullPct >= 70 ? 'var(--green)' : bullPct >= 50 ? 'var(--amber)' : 'var(--text2)'; }
    if (el('screener-timestamp')) el('screener-timestamp').textContent = 'Updated ' + new Date().toLocaleTimeString();
  }

  // ── AI-enhanced fetch via Claude ─────────────────────────────
  async function _fetchAI(tab) {
    const prompts = {
      momentum: 'What are the top 6 US stocks with the strongest price momentum and bullish technicals RIGHT NOW today? Focus on stocks making new highs, strong RSI, high relative volume.',
      breakout: 'What are the top 5 US stocks that are breaking out of technical patterns RIGHT NOW? Consolidation breakouts, resistance breaks, cup and handle patterns.',
      ai:       'What are the top 6 AI-related stocks with the strongest growth fundamentals and bullish price action RIGHT NOW?',
      earnings:  'What are the top 5 US stocks with upcoming earnings catalysts in the next 60 days that analysts expect to beat? Include the earnings date.',
      volume:    'What are the top 5 US stocks with unusual volume spikes TODAY — 2x or more their average volume with bullish price action?',
      '52w':     'What are the top 5 US stocks trading near or at their 52-week highs TODAY with strong momentum suggesting further upside?',
    };
    const prompt = `${prompts[tab]||prompts.momentum}

Return ONLY a JSON array (no markdown, no backticks): [{"sym":"AAPL","name":"Apple Inc","price":213.50,"chgPct":1.2,"signal":"STRONG BUY","score":88,"upside":15,"thesis":"2-3 sentence bullish thesis","sector":"Tech","tags":["Tag1","Tag2"],"entry":208,"target":245,"stop":195}]. Score 1-100 (conviction level). Upside in %. Signal: STRONG BUY, BUY, BREAKOUT, AI TOP PICK, AI PICK, PRE-EARNINGS, VOLUME SURGE, 52W HIGH, or WATCH.`;

    const resp = await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        model: 'claude-sonnet-4-20250514',
        max_tokens: 2000,
        tools: [{ type: 'web_search_20250305', name: 'web_search' }],
        messages: [{ role: 'user', content: prompt }],
      }),
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(35000),
    });
    if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('API ' + resp.status);
    const data = await resp.json();
    const text = (data.content||[]).filter(b=>b.type==='text').map(b=>b.text).join('');
    const match = text.match(/\[[\s\S]*?\]/);
    if (!match) throw new Error('no JSON array');
    const parsed = JSON.parse(match[0]);
    if (!Array.isArray(parsed) || !parsed.length) throw new Error('empty array');
    return parsed.filter(s => s.sym && s.score > 0).map(s => ({ ...s, cat: tab }));
  }

  // ── Public run ────────────────────────────────────────────────
  async function run(tab) {
    if (_loading) return;
    _loading = true;
    _tab = tab || _tab;

    const loading = document.getElementById('screener-loading');
    const grid    = document.getElementById('screener-grid');
    const tableWrap = document.getElementById('screener-table-wrap');
    if (loading) { loading.style.display = 'block'; loading.innerHTML = `<div style="font-size:1.5rem;margin-bottom:0.75rem;animation:spin 1s linear infinite;display:inline-block">⬡</div><br><span style="color:var(--green)">AI scanning ${_tab} signals...</span><br><span style="font-size:0.7rem;color:var(--text3);margin-top:0.5rem;display:block">Searching live market data...</span>`; }
    if (grid)     grid.style.display = 'none';
    if (tableWrap) tableWrap.style.display = 'none';

    // Show seed data immediately while AI loads
    const seedData = SEED[_tab] || SEED.momentum;
    _render(seedData);
    if (loading) loading.style.display = 'none';

    // Now fetch AI-powered results in background
    try {
      const aiResults = await _fetchAI(_tab);
      if (aiResults && aiResults.length >= 3) {
        // Merge AI results with seed (AI takes priority, seed fills gaps)
        const aiSyms = new Set(aiResults.map(r=>r.sym));
        const merged = [...aiResults, ...seedData.filter(s=>!aiSyms.has(s.sym))].slice(0,8);
        merged.sort((a,b) => b.score - a.score);
        _render(merged);
        try { localStorage.setItem(CACHE_KEY + '_' + _tab, JSON.stringify({ ts: Date.now(), data: merged })); } catch(_){}
      }
    } catch(e) {
      console.warn('[Screener] AI fetch failed, using seed data:', e.message);
    }

    _loading = false;
    _lastRun = Date.now();
  }

  return {
    run,
    setTab(t) { _tab = t; run(t); },
    setView(v) { _view = v; if (_results.length) _render(_results); },
    refresh()  { run(_tab); },
  };
})();

// ── Screener global functions (called from HTML) ──────────────
function setScreenerTab(tab, btn) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.scr-tab').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
  if (btn) btn.classList.add('active');
  ScreenerEngine.setTab(tab);
}

function setScreenerView(v) {
  ['grid','table'].forEach(t => {
    const b = document.getElementById('scr-view-' + t);
    if (b) b.classList.toggle('active', t === v);
  });
  ScreenerEngine.setView(v);
}

function refreshScreener() {
  const btn = document.getElementById('scr-refresh-btn');
  if (btn) { btn.textContent = '⟳ Scanning...'; btn.disabled = true; }
  ScreenerEngine.refresh();
  setTimeout(() => { if (btn) { btn.textContent = '↻ Refresh'; btn.disabled = false; } }, 8000);
}

// ── Auto-init screener when section scrolls into view ─────────
const _scrObserver = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
  entries.forEach(e => {
    if (e.isIntersecting && !window._screenerInited) {
      window._screenerInited = true;
      ScreenerEngine.run('momentum');
    }
  });
}, { threshold: 0.1 });
const _scrEl = document.getElementById('screener');
if (_scrEl) _scrObserver.observe(_scrEl);

// Auto-refresh screener every 15 minutes if visible
setInterval(() => {
  if (window._screenerInited) {
    const el = document.getElementById('screener');
    if (el && el.getBoundingClientRect().top < window.innerHeight) {
      ScreenerEngine.refresh();
    }
  }
}, 15 * 60 * 1000);

console.log('[TradeIQ] Chart engine initialized ✓');
</script>
</body>
</html>
